## 20. Revision 2: the orbitable machine, Elektron Analog Rytm style (impact plan)

Status: PLAN, 2026-09-29, written before any revision 2 code, from the client brief "/v4 REVISION 2, MACHINE ORBITABLE FACON ELEKTRON RYTM" (7 changes in priority order) and from the code of commit a7b6841 that sections 0 to 19 describe. Where this section contradicts sections 0 to 19, this section wins for the revision 2 build; every stage adds its "As implemented" notes here, as stages 1 to 6 did above. Numbers come from the brief, from the current code, or from three projection scripts run on three 0.186.1 (default view azimuth 45 deg, elevation 38 deg, zoom 1; section 20.14 says how to rerun them). Stage R1 (the orbit, on the revision 1 machine) is implemented: section 20.15 lists what it built, its deviations from this plan and its measurements. Stage R2 (the Rytm body and layout, with the wedge explode, the three-line OLED and the LABEL and SONAA sections brought forward) is implemented: section 20.16. Stage 3 of 5 of the orchestrated build (SWING, DIST and REVERB made real, persisted with the pattern; a drag that starts on an encoder never orbits) is implemented: section 20.17. Stage 4 of 5 (the 15 real social icons, the LABEL chip as a direct Bandcamp link with its yellow hover and chevron, the LIVE chip as a button that opens the French LIVE panel, SONAA, the shared ExternalLink) is implemented: section 20.18. Stage 5 of 5 (polish and budgets: the client's tests T1 to T7 run muted on desktop and at 390 x 844, the Tab order voices, nav pads, encoders, RUN/CLEAR, trig keys, RESET VIEW, two focus fixes, the Dock PAGES row, the budgets, the fallback and reduced-motion checks) is implemented: section 20.19, which also sums up revision 2 as shipped (P-8, every deviation from this plan) and the owner questions still open (P-9). The review of revision 2 (29 findings) is applied: section 20.20 lists every fix, the three findings left as they are and why, the measurements, and the rules it changes (a still press now fires at any duration, an encoder is grabbed by a resting finger on touch, the Mixcloud mark is drawn); P-8 and P-9 carry them.

### 20.0 Rules that do not change

- Write scope: src/v4/** and docs/v4/** only. src/App.tsx, package.json, public/**, src/v2/** (socials.ts included), src/v3/**, src/utils/scWidget.ts and dist/ stay as they are. No new npm dependency; three by named imports only; no OrbitControls, no simple-icons package at runtime, no R3F, drei, GSAP or Tone.
- Git is read-only for the builders; the main session commits.
- Never audible: every run with ?debug=1&mute=1; sound is checked at `__v4.audio.analyser` (peak > 0.01) and with `__v4.clock.drift()`; never click a SoundCloud row, never call scPlay.
- ASCII only in src/v4 and docs/v4 (acceptance 25 keeps its three greps). The LIVE and SONAA panels are French with accents: the source writes them with JSX named entities (`&eacute;`, `&egrave;`, `&agrave;`) in JSX text, or `\u00e9` style escapes in string literals, so the page shows the client's exact French while every file stays ASCII. This file quotes them with `\u` escapes.
- UI copy in English, except the LIVE and SONAA panels (French, exactly as given, `lang="fr"` on both sections). Comments short, French without accents or English. No em dash, no en dash, no emoji anywhere.
- Desktop 1440 x 900 and 390 x 844 (resize_window, touch emulated) at every stage. Dev server only through the Claude_Browser preview ("dev", port 5173), left running. The pane is usually hidden: use the shim of section 19 item 87, force a synchronous render with `__v4.stage.resize(w - 1, h)` then `resize(w, h)`, read pixels back with `gl.readPixels` in the same task, project objects with the camera; screenshots are proof for HTML overlays only.

### 20.1 Decisions taken beyond the brief (read once, then build)

R2-1. Tap rule for EVERY pointer activation, pads included: a hotspot fires on pointerup only if the pointer moved less than 6 px from its pointerdown AND less than 400 ms elapsed AND no second pointer joined the gesture AND the release still picks the same hotspot. Pads no longer fire on pointerdown (rev 1 decision "the sound must not wait" is reversed by the brief's trap rule): the pointer latency of a pad is now the press duration (about 60 to 150 ms for a tap). Keyboard A S D F and the pad twins stay immediate. The CH hold (open hat after 300 ms) is removed from the pointer path: a still press longer than 400 ms activates nothing (brief rule). drums.trigger keeps its `open` argument, unused by the UI. (Superseded by 20.20 RV-3: the duration no longer counts for a still press, which fires at any length; 400 ms only gates the background double tap.)
R2-2. Encoders capture only a VERTICAL drag. When a pointer that went down on an encoder crosses the 6 px threshold, |dy| >= |dx| turns the encoder (the rev 1 potentiometer drag, now starting at 6 px instead of 3) and anything else orbits. The wheel over an encoder turns it (rev 1 behaviour), the wheel anywhere else zooms. (Superseded by 20.17 FX-5: a drag that starts on an encoder never orbits; the dominant axis at the 6 px threshold turns it.)
R2-3. Parallax is removed (class Parallax, PARALLAX, the input pointermove listener): the orbit replaces it, both on the same pointer would fight, and parallax rendered frames on every mouse move.
R2-4. Default view = azimuth 45 deg, elevation 38 deg, zoom 1, as the brief's numbers say, although the brief calls it "the current view" (the rev 1 camera (12, 10, 12) sits at 30.5 deg). 38 deg shows more of the tilted panel, where the 12 pads and 16 keys are, and gives larger touch targets.
R2-5. Constant scale while orbiting. The frustum is fitted once per layout and aspect, on the default view (78 % of the width on desktop, 92 % on mobile, 86 % height guard), never per orientation: turning never "breathes". A full turn at elevation 38 always fits (max projected height 12.07 units against a 13.77 unit frustum at 1440 x 900); above about 55 deg of elevation the closed machine can exceed the canvas height on landscape screens at zoom 1 (16.75 units at 78 deg); zoom 0.55 fits every orientation. Owner question in 20.14.
R2-6. Section framing under orbit: with a panel open on desktop, the machine is fitted by its bounding circle (max projected half-width over all azimuths: 8.35 units closed, 9.23 open) left of the panel, so no azimuth can bring it under the panel at zoom <= 1. This replaces the rev 1 LCD_RIGHT_SX rule (the screen is now at the far left).
R2-7. Frustum offsets are zoom-corrected. three centres the zoomed frustum on (left + right) / 2, so the section offset ox (and any vertical offset) is divided by camera.zoom: zooming never moves the machine's centre away from its free area.
R2-8. The explode keeps the chassis on the table. The new floor is a fixed plane (brief consequence a), so the rev 1 "socle sinks 1.5" would cut through it. Revision 2: the top panel lifts 3.6, slides back 1.5 and tilts to -12 deg (front edge up); the PCB rises 0.9 out of the chassis; the chassis does not move. The camera target rises with the stack instead of the rev 1 frustum shift.
R2-9. Twins follow the view on every rendered frame (brief consequence d), written inside the render pass through a new allocation-free rectangle projection (hit.rects()). The rev 1 review rule "twins wait until the view is still, only the focused one follows" is dropped. The full convex hulls are still computed only for picking (pointer events).
R2-10. The shadow map renders only when a caster changed. The key light and the machine are fixed in the world, so an orbit-only frame has the same shadow map: `renderer.shadowMap.autoUpdate = false`, and `needsUpdate = true` on every frame except orbit-only frames. The draw-call budget is checked on a frame WITH the shadow pass (`__v4.stats.maxDrawCalls`).
R2-11. Picking resolves occlusion, now that one can look from anywhere: when several silhouettes contain the point, the nearest along the view ray wins, and registered occluders (panel slab, chassis wedge) hide what lies behind them (a chip seen through the lifted panel is not pickable). Analytic ray tests, on pointer events and for the trace anchor only; still no Raycaster, nothing added to the render loop's picking.
R2-12. Mobile Dock PAGES row (recommended, owner may veto). At zoom 1 on the 390 px phone the 12 pads are 30 x 24 px with centres 20.5 px apart (20.3.12): navigation by 3D pad on a phone would misfire. A scrollable row of the seven page names (TRACKS to SONAA, 48 px tall, like the sheet tabs) joins the Dock; OPEN is already in its transport row. The 3D pads keep working (48 px target rects, nearest centre) and pinch zoom 2.4 brings them to 49 px apart. (As implemented, 20.19 P-3: at the bottom of the Dock, under the transport row, like a tab bar; hidden on screens under 600 px tall.)
R2-13. A back fill light (DirectionalLight, fixed in the world, no shadow, bone, intensity 0.35, tune 0.25 to 0.6, from (-5, 7, -12)): the key light never reaches the back face, and the back connectors are "the reward of the orbit". It costs no draw call.
R2-14. The OLED shows three lines: line 1 section and BPM, line 2 transport or the playing title and timecode, line 3 the transient messages (moved from line 2) and the encoder readouts (SWING 58%, DIST 20%, REVERB 35%, TONE 80%, LEVEL 64%, for 1200 ms after the last change).
R2-15. LABEL panel copy: one line "VRSTL Records" and the link "Bandcamp" to https://vrstlrecords.bandcamp.com with the chevron. Nothing is invented about the label; the main session can extend the line.
R2-16. The STUDIO chip also gets the yellow trace from its chip, like LIVE (consistency); its behaviour (opens STUDIO) is unchanged. One line in Trace.tsx removes it.
R2-17. SWING, DIST and REVERB are real, discreet and not persisted (like TONE and LEVEL): swing delays the odd 16ths inside the clock, DIST is a parallel tanh drive on the bus, REVERB a synthetic-impulse ConvolverNode send connected lazily. If the convolver costs too much on the mobile tier (20.8), REVERB becomes decorative there and the session report says so. (As implemented, 20.17: persisted with the pattern, as the stage brief asked; REVERB is live on every tier, the mobile check passed.)
R2-18. Silk in SF Pro Display 500 (thin legends, brief), tracking 0.2 em; `FONT_LOADS` gains '500 40px "SF Pro Display"'. The socle mention plane (one draw call) disappears; "V.4 / 2026" is printed on the panel silk.
R2-19. External links: the brief's `rel="noopener noreferrer"` is used on every new-tab link of /v4 (socials, LABEL chip, LABEL and SONAA panels, PRESS, SHOWS, Bandcamp rows).

### 20.2 Orbit (brief item 1, absolute priority)

#### 20.2.1 Parameters (theme.ts `ORBIT`)

| Constant | Value | Note |
|---|---|---|
| default | azimuth 45 deg, elevation 38 deg, zoom 1 | azimuth measured from +z toward +x (camera at +x +z of the target) |
| elevation clamp | 18 to 78 deg | never under the table, never vertical; 18 keeps the -12 deg lifted panel's underside hidden (6 deg margin) |
| azimuth | free, wrapped to [0, 360) for display | full turn |
| zoom | camera.zoom clamped 0.55 to 2.4 | wheel and pinch act on it, never on the camera position |
| pan | none | the machine stays centred in its frame, always |
| damping | 0.08 per 60 fps frame, frame-rate independent: k = 1 - 0.92 ^ (dt / 16.667) | on pending rotation and on pending log-zoom |
| hard stop | a frame step under 0.001 rad (rotation) and under 0.001 (log-zoom) ends the motion, the remainder is dropped | never renders forever; a fresh wheel remainder under the limit is applied, not dropped (20.20 RV-1) |
| rotation speed | pi rad per canvas height in CSS px, both axes (0.2 deg/px on a 900 px desktop canvas, 0.39 deg/px on the 464 px phone canvas) | tune 0.8 to 1.6 x |
| drag convention | drag right turns the machine to the right (azimuth decreases), drag down shows more of the top (elevation increases) | "grab the object" |
| wheel | zoom *= exp(-deltaY x 0.0015), deltaY in px (lines x 40, pages x 800); Ctrl or pinch-trackpad wheel x 6 | preventDefault except over an encoder |
| pinch | zoom = zoom0 x distance / distance0 | two touch pointers |
| tap | < 6 px, single pointer (the < 400 ms condition was dropped by 20.20 RV-3; it still gates the background double tap) | 20.2.3 |
| background double tap | two taps < 300 ms and < 30 px apart, both outside the machine silhouette and on no hotspot | reset |
| reset | 500 ms easeOutCubic to the default, azimuth by the shortest way | reduced motion: cut |
| moved | abs(azimuth delta) > 0.5 deg or abs(elevation delta) > 0.5 deg or abs(zoom - 1) > 0.01 | shows RESET VIEW |
| camera distance | 30 units along the view direction, near 0.1, far 100 | orthographic: distance only matters for clipping |
| target | closed (0, 1.16, 0), the default-view centre of the closed machine (computed); open: y rises to `EXPLODE.targetY` (about 2.7, re-measured at stage R4) | orbit pivot |

Reduced motion: no damping (the pending delta applies in the same frame, so no glide after release), reset is a cut; the user's own drag still rotates.

#### 20.2.2 src/v4/scene/orbit.ts (new, about 120 lines)

```ts
export interface OrbitOpts {
  camera: OrthographicCamera;
  /** element des pointeurs : .v4-stage (les evenements de .v4-hit y remontent) */
  input: HTMLElement;
  /** reveil de la boucle a la demande (Stage.kick) */
  wake: () => void;
  /** zoom change : le Stage recalcule le frustum (decalages / zoom) */
  onZoom: () => void;
  reduced: () => boolean;
}
export class Orbit {
  azimuth: number;           // rad
  elevation: number;         // rad, clamped
  zoom: number;              // clamped
  readonly target: Vector3;  // pivot, written by the Stage (explode framing)
  /** called once per pointer when it crosses 6 px; false = the host keeps the pointer (encoder turn) */
  gate: (pointerId: number, dx: number, dy: number) => boolean;
  constructor(opts: OrbitOpts);
  /** Stage animator: applies pending rotation, zoom glide and reset; true if the camera moved this frame */
  update(now: number, dt: number): boolean;
  /** read by the hit layer in ITS pointerup (before orbit's own): a tap per the brief rule */
  isTap(e: PointerEvent): boolean;
  get dragging(): boolean;   // a pointer is orbiting or pinching (no hover picking meanwhile)
  get moving(): boolean;     // dragging, pending above the stop threshold, or reset running
  get moved(): boolean;      // away from the default view
  reset(): void;             // 500 ms easeOutCubic, cut under reduced motion
  set(azDeg: number, elDeg: number, zoom: number, instant?: boolean): void;  // tests (debug)
  apply(): void;             // camera.position, lookAt(target), zoom, updateProjectionMatrix when zoom changed
  dispose(): void;
}
```

Events it listens to on `opts.input`, bubble phase, and nothing else: pointerdown, pointermove, pointerup, pointercancel, wheel (passive false). Per pointer it keeps { x0, y0, t0, last x/y, maxDist, orbiting, foreign, multi }. pointerdown: record; a second active pointer turns the gesture into a pinch (both marked multi: no tap, no rotation) with distance0 and zoom0. pointermove: maxDist updated; a single pointer crossing 6 px calls `gate()`; true: `orbiting`, and the whole delta since pointerdown is added to the pending rotation (no dead-zone lag), then each move adds its delta; false: `foreign`, ignored until up. Pinch moves set the pending log-zoom to log(zoom0 x d / d0) - log(zoom). pointerup / pointercancel: the record is dropped; the pending delta keeps gliding (soft inertia); when one finger of a pinch lifts, the remaining pointer is re-anchored at its current position and stays multi (no jump, no tap). wheel: ignored if `e.defaultPrevented` (the hit layer turned an encoder), else preventDefault and pending log-zoom += -deltaY x k. Every handler calls `wake()`. Any pointerdown cancels a running reset.

update(): k from dt; stepAz = pendingAz x k, stepEl = pendingEl x k, stepZ = pendingLogZoom x k (k = 1 under reduced motion); apply with the clamps (an elevation or zoom that hits its clamp zeroes its pending); when no pointer is orbiting and every step is under its stop threshold, pending = 0 and the motion ends. The reset tween overrides pending while it runs. Writes the camera through apply(), calls onZoom() when zoom changed, publishes `view.moved` (state/view.ts) only when it flips. No allocation per frame (scratch Vector3 at module level).

#### 20.2.3 Tap versus orbit: the trap, and who decides

Listener order is the contract. The hit layer's handlers sit on `.v4-hit` (target phase) and run BEFORE orbit's (bubble on `.v4-stage`); React runs the HitLayer effect before the V4Shell effect that creates the Stage, and the bubble order holds anyway.

1. pointerdown (hit layer): `gesture()` (audio context), blur a focused twin, `setPointerCapture` for EVERY pointer (so a drag keeps orbiting outside the canvas), pick the hotspot (occlusion aware, 20.7), remember { pointerId -> hotspot or null }. Nothing fires here any more.
2. pointerdown (orbit): record, maybe pinch.
3. pointermove (hit layer): if this pointer is an encoder turn (the gate said false): set the value (TEMPO 2 px per BPM, pots 150 px for the range, as rev 1). Else, mouse hover only when `!orbit.dragging` (cursor pointer / ns-resize / grab).
4. pointermove (orbit): threshold, gate, pending rotation or pinch.
5. pointerup (hit layer): encoder turn ends; else if `orbit.isTap(e)` and the release picks the same hotspot: fire it (20.6 TAP). If the down was on no hotspot and outside the machine silhouette (`hit.onMachine(x, y) === false`) and it is a tap: background tap, a second one within 300 ms and 30 px resets the view. Release capture.
6. pointerup (orbit): drop the record; the glide continues.
7. pointercancel: both drop everything, nothing fires.
8. wheel (hit layer): over an encoder: preventDefault and turn it (rev 1 code, kind 'encoder'); wheel (orbit): zoom unless defaultPrevented.

The gate is set by the hit layer when the Stage exists: `stage.orbit.gate = (id, dx, dy) => !(downOnEncoder(id) && Math.abs(dy) >= Math.abs(dx))`, and it starts the encoder turn when it returns false. The HitLayer therefore takes the `stage` (state) as a prop, like Twins, to re-register the gate on remount.

What this guarantees (T1): a press that stays within 6 px and ends before 400 ms fires on release; a drag that starts on a pad never fires (maxDist >= 6 px), the machine rotates from the first 6 px; a still press held over 400 ms fires nothing (superseded by 20.20 RV-3: it fires, whatever its length); a second finger cancels every pending tap. The sound of a tapped pad starts at the pointerup; on a real phone the window capture pointerup listener of index.tsx resumes the audio context before the hit layer triggers the voice, so the first tap of a page plays (better than rev 1, section 19 item 29).

#### 20.2.4 On-demand loop (scene/renderer.ts)

- The Stage creates `this.orbit = new Orbit({ camera, input: opts.input, wake: () => this.kick(), onZoom: () => this.updateCamera(), reduced: motion.reduced })` and calls `orbit.apply()` once before the first render.
- frame(): `const viewMoved = this.orbit.update(now, dt)` runs first, then the other animators (intro, tweens, explode, pads, playhead). Render if `viewMoved || moved || dirty`. `renderer.shadowMap.needsUpdate = moved || contentDirty` (contentDirty is set by invalidate(); orbit frames do not set it). Keep the loop while `viewMoved || moved || poll`; else stop and emit idle.
- Rendered during drag, inertia, zoom glide, reset and every rev 1 animation; zero frames as soon as everything is still (the hard stop guarantees an end). T7: nothing allocates per frame in orbit, hit.rects(), the twin writes or the trace.
- stats: frames, rafs, drawCalls (last frame), triangles, and new `maxDrawCalls` / `maxTriangles` (worst frame since mount or since `__v4.stats.reset()`), `shadowUpdates`.

#### 20.2.5 Camera and framing composition (Stage.updateCamera)

1. Base half-width per layout and aspect: `hwBase = max(PLATEAU_W / 2 / frame, MACHINE_H / FIT_H / 2 x aspect)` with PLATEAU_W = 16.263 (the 14 x 9 footprint at azimuth 45, unchanged), MACHINE_H = 11.84 (the new closed machine at the default view, was 9.85), FIT_H 0.86, frame 0.78 desktop / 0.92 mobile.
2. Section (desktop): stageW = panelLeft(W) - 16; s_section = stageW / (2 x R) with R = SECTION_FRAME.radius (8.35 closed, blended to 9.23 by the explode progress); hw_section = W / (2 x min(s_base, s_section)); ox = (W / 2 - stageW / 2) / s. Tween 400 ms easeInOutCubic as rev 1 (cut under reduced motion and on layout switches).
3. Explode: hw grows to at least `EXPLODE.fitHalfH x aspect` (the open stack in 86 % of the height at the default view, fitHalfH = H_open / 0.86 / 2 with H_open about 14.6), blended by the explode framing progress; the orbit target y blends from 1.16 to EXPLODE.targetY (about 2.7). The rev 1 `oy` frustum shift is removed.
4. Zoom: `left = -hw + ox / zoom`, `right = hw + ox / zoom` (R2-7), top/bottom = +/-hh, then updateProjectionMatrix (which applies camera.zoom). `ppu` (px per unit) = W x zoom / (2 hw).

Measured with the projection script at the default view: 1440 x 900 hw 11.015 (the height guard applies: machine 1063 x 774 px, 73.8 % of the width, 86 % of the height, 65.37 px/unit); 1280 x 800 same fractions, 58.1 px/unit; 1920 x 950 hw 13.914, 58.4 % of the width (height-bound); 390 x 464 phone canvas hw 8.839, 22.06 px/unit, machine 359 x 261 px (92 % of the width). Section open (closed stack, R 8.35): 1440 55.8 px/unit, 1280 46.2, 1024 34.3.

#### 20.2.6 Floor, fog, halo and shadow (consequences a and b)

- One opaque floor mesh, child of socleGroup (so it rides the intro drop; the chassis never moves in the explode): PlaneGeometry 120 x 120 (2 triangles), rotated flat at world y -0.001, `receiveShadow`, no cast. Material: ShadowMaterial patched in onBeforeCompile (replaces rev 1 `withContactShadow`), `transparent: false`, `toneMapped: false`, `customProgramCacheKey 'v4Floor'`:
  - r = distance of the fragment (world xz) to the machine centre;
  - halo = HALO x exp(-(r / 9)^2) with HALO the linear difference between #121215 and ink (a very discreet lift, 18 against 10 levels at the centre, tune 14 to 20);
  - fog f = 1 - exp(-(0.045 x r)^2) (0.55 at r 20, 0.84 at 30, 0.999 at the plane edge, r 60): the shadows fade into the clear colour (the halo's own falloff is gone by r 20), so there is never a horizon line (the floor base IS the clear colour #0A0A0B);
  - shadow alpha = max(0.5 x (1 - getShadowMask()), contact(d)) x (1 - f), contact = 0.45 x (1 - smoothstep(0, 1.4, d)) with d the distance to the 14 x 9 footprint (corner 0.1), the rev 1 contact shadow re-parameterised (as shipped: cast 0.8 since 20.20 RV-17, contact 0.6);
  - gl_FragColor = vec4((ink + halo) x (1 - shadowAlpha), 1.0), linear, then the colour-space chunk.
  Scene fog (three Fog) is NOT used: it would wash the machine too. Draw calls: 1 (replaces the rev 1 shadow plane).
- Key DirectionalLight stays fixed in the world at (8, 14, 6), target (0, 0, 0), never parented to the camera: the shadow is the same from every angle and follows the machine because nothing moves. Shadow camera: light-space extents measured for the new body: closed |x| 7.84, |y| 8.34, open |x| 9.01, |y| 10.38 against the extent 11, depth 7.4 to 22.0 against near 1 / far 40. Covered from every view (the shadow camera does not depend on the view); extent raised to 11.5 for margin (44.5 texels per unit on the 1024 desktop map). Casters: chassis, panel (desktop), pads, encoders (desktop). Receivers: floor, chassis, panel, silk.
- Back fill DirectionalLight (R2-13) and the rim PointLight (-9, 3, 0) are also fixed in the world. HemisphereLight unchanged.

#### 20.2.7 Panel trace (consequence c)

- Anchor = a 3D point of the object that opened the panel: the pad of a page (TRACKS to SONAA: the pad's top centre, plateau layer), the chip for LIVE and STUDIO (the chip's top centre, pcb layer). `stage.projectAnchor(id, out)` returns { x, y, visible }: projected with the layer's matrixWorld; `visible` = inside the canvas (8 px margin) AND left of the panel (x < panelLeft - 12) AND not occluded (`hit.visible(layer, x, y, z, ignoreId)`: ray from the point toward the camera against the occluders and the other hotspot volumes).
- Rerouted in `stage.onView` on every rendered frame (same pass as the render): the anchor and the machine's projected right edge (max x of the 8 bounding-box corners of the current layers, replacing the rev 1 fixed corner (7, -4.5)) are reprojected, the path string is rewritten only when a number changed (rev 1 review rule, no allocation).
- Not visible: `data-visible="0"` on the svg, CSS fade to 0 over 150 ms; visible again: fade back in (the draw-in animation is not replayed). The panel NEVER moves (it is laid out by CSS only).
- Desktop only, as rev 1. `on` = stage && section has an anchor && desktop; the knob-specific code (NAV_KNOBS, KNOB, NAV_ROW) is removed.

#### 20.2.8 Twins follow the orbit (consequence d)

- `hit.rects()`: a Float32Array [x, y, w, h] per hotspot (list order), recomputed when `hit.version()` changed, from 8 projected points per hotspot (a box's corners, or the bounding box of a disc), enlarged to the 48 px (touch) or 32 px (mouse) target like rev 1; no hull, no allocation.
- Twins register `stage.onView` (called INSIDE render(), after renderer.render: the same pass) and write `transform`, `width`, `height` of the twins whose rounded values changed; `stage.onIdle` writes the exact values once at rest. No separate requestAnimationFrame anywhere for the twins.
- The focused twin's 2 px yellow ring therefore stays on its object during an orbit.

#### 20.2.9 RESET VIEW, key R, background double tap

- `ui/ResetView.tsx` (new): `<button type="button" class="v4-reset" aria-label="Reset view">RESET VIEW</button>`, rendered only while `view.moved` (state/view.ts) is true, 150 ms opacity in and out (none under reduced motion), click -> `resetView()` (actions.ts) -> `stage.orbit.reset()`. Style: silk-like label (11 px, SF Pro Display 600, tracking 0.18 em, bone 70 %, yellow on hover and focus-visible), 44 px tall hit area, transparent background, no border.
- Placement: OUTSIDE `.v4-stage` (sibling in `.v4-root`, z-index 22) so its pointer events never reach the orbit. Desktop: right 24 px, bottom 24 px of the viewport (an open panel ends at 85 vh). Mobile: inside the canvas band, right 12 px, top `calc(var(--canvas-h-mobile) - 56px)` (the default machine spans y 101 to 363 of the 464 px canvas, the corner is free).
- Key R (no modifier, not in an editable field, not a repeat) and the background double tap (20.2.3) call the same action.

### 20.3 The machine (brief item 2)

#### 20.3.1 World frame and hierarchy

World: +y up, +x the machine's width (right), +z toward the front (the user), floor at y 0. The body is 14 x 9 on the floor. "Panel coordinates" below are the plateauGroup's local frame: origin at the centre of the panel's top surface, x right, z down the slope toward the user, y 0 = the top surface.

```
scene
  camera (OrthographicCamera, orbiting; zoom 0.55 to 2.4)
  key DirectionalLight + target, back fill DirectionalLight, HemisphereLight, rim PointLight (all fixed in the world)
  machineRoot (Group; intro drop only, no parallax any more)
    socleGroup (y 0; static in the explode)
      chassis (Mesh, merged: wedge with chamfers, 4 rubber feet, back connectors; vertex colours)
      floor (Mesh, ink + halo + fog + cast and contact shadow)
    plateauGroup (position (0, 1.87, 0), rotation.x +5.711 deg; explode: y +3.6, z -1.5, rotation.x -12 deg)
      panel (Mesh, merged: aluminium slab with chamfer + OLED bezel; roughnessMap brushing)
      silk (Mesh, plane, transparent CanvasTexture)
      oled (Mesh, plane, CanvasTexture, unlit)
      pads (InstancedMesh x12), padHalos (InstancedMesh x12, additive)
      keys (InstancedMesh x18: 16 trig keys, RUN/STOP, CLEAR), leds (InstancedMesh x16)
      encoders (InstancedMesh x6)
    pcbGroup (position (0, 1.629, 0), rotation.x +5.711 deg; explode: y +0.9; visible from "opening" to the end of "closing")
      board (Mesh), parts (Mesh, merged; scale.y 0.001 to 1)
```

#### 20.3.2 Chassis (wedge), feet, back connectors (scene/machine.ts)

| Part | Geometry | Placement | Colour (displayed target, calibrated by read-back) |
|---|---|---|---|
| wedge | side profile (z, y) extruded along x: (-4.5, 0.12), (4.5, 0.12), (4.5, 1.28), (-4.5, 2.18) with 0.06 chamfers on its four corners (8 points), extrude depth 14 - 0.12, bevelSize 0.06, bevelThickness 0.06, bevelSegments 1: every edge chamfered | x -7 to 7, z -4.5 to 4.5, underside y 0.12, top face = the panel's underside plane (back 2.18, front 1.28, slope 0.1) | faces #0C0C0E (brief), chamfer band #2A2B30 (catches the light, tune), top face #09090A (seen only when the panel is lifted), underside never seen |
| feet (4) | CylinderGeometry r 0.42, h 0.12, 16 segments (12 mobile) | centres (+/-6.45, 0.06, +/-3.95): the outer edge 0.13 from the body edges, so the dark sliver of rubber shows at elevation 18 (the gap under a 0.12 underside is visible 0.37 units deep at 18 deg) | rubber #050506 |
| power inlet | outer box 1.1 x 0.75 x 0.06 proud of the back face, inner recess 0.8 x 0.5 (ink), 3 pins 0.05 x 0.18 x 0.04 (leg) | back face z -4.5, centre (5.2, 1.15) | graphite, ink, leg |
| USB-B | box 0.46 x 0.42 x 0.05 (leg), inner 0.3 x 0.26 (ink) | (3.9, 1.15) | leg, ink |
| jacks 6.35 x 2 | nut CylinderGeometry r 0.2 h 0.05 (leg, 6 segments: a hex nut), barrel r 0.14 h 0.08 (line), hole r 0.065 (ink), axis z | (-3.6, 1.1) and (-4.4, 1.1) | leg, line, ink |

All merged into ONE geometry with the chassis (one draw call, `makeBodyMaterial`: vertexColors, flatShading, roughness 0.85 (matte, brief), metalness 0). The connectors are the first geometric detail to cut if the /v4 chunk passes 230 KB (brief). World heights: body top back 2.32, front 1.42 (2.2 and 1.3 above the underside), tilt atan(0.9 / 9) = 5.711 deg ("about 6 degrees").

#### 20.3.3 Top panel (scene/machine.ts, scene/materials.ts, scene/silk.ts)

- Slab: ExtrudeGeometry of a rounded rectangle 13.8 x 8.845 (corner radius 0.2, curveSegments 4) with bevelSize 0.1, bevelThickness 0.05, bevelSegments 1, rotated so the extrusion is local y, top at local y 0, 0.14 thick; outer 14 x 9.045 in panel coordinates = 14 x 9.0 on the floor. Cap UVs are the shape's (x, z) in units (for the brushing map).
- OLED bezel merged into the slab: box 3.86 x 0.02 x 1.61 centred (-4.5, 0.01, -2.35), colour #050506.
- Material (brief): `MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.62, metalness: 0.35, roughnessMap: brush })`; vertex colours by normal: top #141417, upward chamfer band #3A3B40 (the anodised edge that catches the light), sides #101012, bezel #050506. The brief's colours are DISPLAYED targets, as every token since section 19 item 12: the albedo gain is re-derived by read-back at the default view so the top reads 20,20,23 +/- 3 (metalness 0.35 with no environment map darkens the diffuse part, the gain will be above rev 1's x 3).
- Brushing (`makeBrushTexture()` in silk.ts): 256 x 256 grayscale canvas, one value per row, 0.86 to 1.0 random (seeded mulberry32 808), 1 row in 12 a streak at 0.8 or 1.0; RepeatWrapping, repeat 0.5 per unit (the UVs are in units), mipmaps and anisotropy 8, colorSpace NoColorSpace (data). Effective roughness 0.53 to 0.62: invisible from afar (mipmapped), fine horizontal lines when zoomed in. Never createPattern (repo trap).

#### 20.3.5 Pads: 12 in ONE InstancedMesh (scene/pads.ts, rewritten)

- Geometry (one, shared): RoundedBoxGeometry(0.96, 0.22, 0.96, 3 desktop / 2 mobile, 0.08), base at y 0, merged with a dome cap: PlaneGeometry(0.8, 0.8, 6, 6) (the flat part of the top, 0.96 - 2 x 0.08) rotated flat at y 0.222 (no z-fight with the flat top) with y += 0.04 x (1 - (x / 0.4)^2) x (1 - (z / 0.4)^2), zero on its whole border (a RoundedBoxGeometry has no inner vertex on its flat top, so it cannot be domed by itself). Triangles: 588 + 72 = 660 desktop, 300 + 72 = 372 mobile.
- Material: `withInstanceEmissive(MeshStandardMaterial({ vertexColors: true, roughness: 0.9, metalness: 0 }), true)`; vertex colour black rubber (displayed #111113); emissiveMask 1 on the dome and the flat top, 0.55 on the sides (translucent rubber lit from below).
- Instances in reading order: 0 BD, 1 SD, 2 TOM, 3 CH, 4 TRACKS, 5 MIXTAPES, 6 PRESS, 7 SHOWS, 8 CONTACT, 9 LABEL, 10 SONAA, 11 OPEN.
- Backlight states (`PAD_GLOW`, linear emissive radiances, calibrated to the DISPLAYED colour by read-back like rev 1): voices: off (unlit), selected (dim warm white, top about +12 levels: distinct from the pages' faint yellow), flash (bright yellow, displayed #F2C230, 120 ms on a tap, 100 ms on a sequencer hit, as rev 1); pages: idle faint yellow (permanent, about 15 % yellow over the rubber), hover (desktop, about 30 %), active yellowHi (displayed #FFD75E, one section at a time); OPEN: yellowHi while the explode is opening or open, faint yellow otherwise.
- Halo (backlit from below): `padHalos`, InstancedMesh of 12 flat quads 1.25 x 1.25 at y 0.003 (0.145 beyond each pad edge: neighbouring halos barely overlap at the 1.12 pitch), MeshBasicMaterial with a 64 x 64 radial alpha CanvasTexture, AdditiveBlending, depthWrite false, toneMapped false, instanceColor = the pad's glow colour x intensity (idle 0, faint 0.15, active 0.6, flash 0.9). One draw call.
- Press (all 12 pads): the instance sinks 0.06 in 60 ms and comes back in 180 ms easeOutCubic (reduced motion: no movement, the glow only).
- API:

```ts
export type PadId = Inst | PageId | 'open';          // 12 ids, reading order
export class Pads {
  readonly mesh: InstancedMesh;                        // 12
  readonly halos: InstancedMesh;                       // 12
  constructor(opts: { tweens: Tweens; reduced: () => boolean; invalidate: () => void; castShadow: boolean; mobile: boolean });
  press(id: PadId, now?: number): void;                // sink, and the flash for a voice
  flash(inst: Inst, ms?: number, now?: number): void;  // sequencer hits
  setSelected(inst: Inst | null): void;
  setActivePage(id: PageId | null): void;
  setOpen(on: boolean): void;
  setHover(id: PadId | null): boolean;
  update(now: number): boolean | 'poll';               // flash deadlines (rev 1)
  hotspots(layer: Object3D): HotspotDef[];             // kinds 'pad' (4), 'page' (7), 'open' (1)
  info(): PadsInfo;                                    // y, glow names, selected, activePage, open, flashes
  dispose(): void;
}
```

#### 20.3.6 Trig keys, RUN/STOP, CLEAR (InstancedMesh x18) and LEDs (x16) (scene/sequencer3d.ts, modified)

- keys: ONE InstancedMesh of 18: instances 0 to 15 the trig keys, 16 RUN/STOP, 17 CLEAR. Geometry RoundedBoxGeometry(0.52, 0.1, 0.9, 1, 0.04) (108 triangles), base at y 0; RUN and CLEAR are the same geometry scaled per instance (0.62 / 0.52, 1, 0.62 / 0.9). instanceColor: trig keys `LIT.key` (displayed #1E1F23, tune), RUN red / runOn yellow plus RUN_GLOW instanceEmissive (rev 1), CLEAR graphiteHi. receiveShadow, no cast.
- leds: ONE InstancedMesh of 16 flat rectangles (PlaneGeometry 0.22 x 0.07 rotated flat, 2 triangles each), MeshBasicMaterial toneMapped false, instanceColor per section 7.5 (line, ledSet, ledHover, yellowHi; the intro chase unchanged).
- Removed: the OPEN instance (OPEN is a pad), the 5 knob LEDs (`setKnobLed`), `openHotspot()`.
- API kept: setPattern, setHover, setPlayhead, setIntroLed, setRunning, info(); `hotspots(layer)` returns step-1 to step-16 (boxes 0.52 x 0.1 x 0.9), run and clear (boxes 0.62 x 0.1 x 0.62). The 16 steps stay disabled on the mobile layout (section 6.4 as implemented).

#### 20.3.7 Encoders (scene/encoders.ts, new; replaces scene/knobs.ts)

- ONE InstancedMesh of 6, merged geometry with vertex colours: body CylinderGeometry(0.285, 0.3, 0.42, 32 desktop / 20 mobile) (the top slightly narrower: a chamfer read), white marker BoxGeometry 0.04 x 0.012 x 0.2 on the top from the centre toward -z (bone), collar CylinderGeometry(0.36, 0.36, 0.025) at the base (#1A1B1F). Material MeshStandardMaterial roughness 0.55, metalness 0; body displayed #101012. castShadow on desktop.
- Angle about the panel normal: `potAngle(t)` of rev 1 (270 deg sweep centred on the marker at 12 o'clock, clockwise as the value rises); TEMPO t = (bpm - 100) / 50.
- API:

```ts
export type EncId = 'tempo' | 'tone' | 'level' | 'swing' | 'dist' | 'reverb';
export class Encoders {
  readonly mesh: InstancedMesh;                              // 6
  constructor(opts: { mobile: boolean; castShadow: boolean });
  setValue(id: EncId, t: number): boolean;                   // 0..1 -> potAngle
  hotspot(id: EncId, layer: Object3D): HotspotDef;           // kind 'encoder', param id, disc r 0.3, y 0..0.42
  info(): { ids: EncId[]; angleDeg: number[]; values: number[] };
  dispose(): void;
}
export { potAngle, tempoAngle };
```

- Removed with knobs.ts: the five navigation knobs, their caps, rings, rise-on-hover, the screws question (section 19 item 52 is void), NAV_KNOBS, NAV_ROW, AUDIO_KNOBS, KNOB, KNOB_FX, RING_Y, SCREWS.

#### 20.3.8 OLED (scene/screen.ts, modified; state/lcd.ts)

- Plane 3.6 x 1.35 at panel (-4.5, 0.025, -2.35), MeshBasicMaterial toneMapped false, CanvasTexture 640 x 240 (aspect 2.667 = the glass), mipmaps and anisotropy (rev 1 item 69). Background #050506 (deep black), text bone #F6F1E7 at 100 % (the site's white, never a pure white), monospace `400 40px ui-monospace, Menlo, Consolas, monospace`, 20 columns, 24 px padding, baselines 64, 136, 208.
- state/lcd.ts composes three lines at most 4 times per second (rev 1 rule): l1/r1 = section title (TRACKS ... LABEL, SONAA, LIVE, STUDIO) or MM-808 / "130 BPM"; l2/r2 = the rev 1 line 2 rules minus the transient messages; l3 = the transient message (STEP 07 BD ON, CLEARED, TAP A PAD FIRST, NO SIGNAL) or the encoder readout of the last 1200 ms, else empty. `lcd.get().text` becomes a 3-tuple; `__v4.state.lcd` too.
- ui/Lcd.tsx (the role=status twin) announces l1, r1, l2 and the transient messages of l3, not the encoder readouts (the slider twins announce their own values).

Canvas 2048 x 1323 desktop / 1024 x 662 mobile over the 14 x 9.045 panel (146.3 / 73.1 px per unit), plane at y 0.004 (rev 1 polygon offset). Content: every text of 20.3.4, the group line and brackets; SF Pro Display 500, capitals, tracking 0.2 em, bone 85 % (wordmark 600, V.4 / 2026 at 45 %). Removed: the OPEN frame and its label, the rev 1 rules, the nav row. Redraws: after the fonts, and on OPEN/CLOSE (two per explode cycle). `Mention` class and MENTION removed.

#### 20.3.10 Lights and colour calibration

Key (8, 14, 6) 2.2, hemisphere 0.35, rim PointLight (-9, 3, 0) yellow 6, back fill (-5, 7, -12) bone 0.35 (new). Every lit colour of revision 2 is a new material or a new angle (panel metalness 0.35, rubber, keys, encoders, chassis at elevation 38): all albedos and emissive radiances (`ALBEDO_GAIN`, `LIT`, `PAD_GLOW`, `RUN_GLOW`, PCB paint gains) are re-measured by read-back at 1440 x 900 on the default view, targets = the displayed tokens, the method of section 19 items 12, 22, 38, 75.

#### 20.3.11 The explode on a tilted wedge (scene/explode.ts, theme.ts EXPLODE, scene/pcb.ts)

| Layer | Object | Closed | Open | Delay opening / closing |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | plateauGroup (panel and everything on it) | position (0, 1.87, 0), rotation.x +5.711 deg | position (0, 5.47, -1.5), rotation.x -12 deg (front edge up, like a lid), rotation about the MACHINE's x axis through the panel centre, no longer about the rev 1 screen axis normalize(1, 0, -1) | 0 / 80 ms |
| 2 | pcbGroup | position (0, 1.629, 0) (board top 0.1 below the chassis top, hidden inside the solid chassis), rotation.x +5.711, visible false, parts.scale.y 0.001 | y 2.529 (+0.9), parts 1 | 80 / 0 ms |
| 3 | socleGroup (chassis, floor) | static | static | none |

900 ms easeInOutQuart per layer (rev 1), opening 980 ms, closing 980 ms (PCB first, panel 80 ms later); pcbGroup.visible false at the end of closing. Framing progress = max(panel, pcb) (rev 1 review rule). Reduced motion: the two end states only. The music keeps running.

Checks computed on the numbers above (script 20.14): minimum vertical clearance between the panel's underside and the PCB components' tops 1.30 units (open), between the PCB board and the chassis top 0.70; during both timelines the panel's back edge rises 2.2 x k while the PCB rises 1.52 x k' with k' <= k (it starts later on opening and earlier on closing), so no layer ever interpenetrates (acceptance R2-A4 samples every 16 ms). The panel underside faces away from the camera for every elevation >= 18 deg (tilt 12 deg).

Chips: board positions LABEL (-3.4, 2.6), LIVE (0.0, 2.6), STUDIO (3.4, 2.6), labels in front of each chip (board z 3.6, 30 px on the 1024 texture). Visible fraction of each chip's top (ray casts against the lifted panel): 100 % at the default view for all three; 100 % from azimuth 315 to 45 at elevation 38 and 60; 40 to 97 % from the sides (azimuth 90, 270) at 38 deg; 0 to 9 % from behind at 38 deg (the lifted panel covers the board, and the picking honours it: R2-11); low views from the back corners (elevation 18, azimuth 135 or 225) still see one chip whole. The other PCB parts and the PCB silk are re-placed with the same visibility script (visible at the default open view); the seeded router is unchanged. `EXPLODE.chipsFrom` 0.85 kept.

Framing: the open stack projects 17.3 x 14.6 units at the default view (max 15.1 over a full turn at 38 deg; the closed machine 16.3 x 12.2 with the same coarse model), and the pivot that centres it is 1.53 units higher than the closed one: `EXPLODE.fitHalfH` = 14.6 / 0.86 / 2 = 8.49 and `EXPLODE.targetY` = 1.16 + 1.53, about 2.7 (both re-measured on the real meshes with `__v4.measure()` at stage R4). On the phone the portrait frustum (21 units) already fits.

| Hotspot | 1440 x 900 (65.37 px/unit) | nearest centre | 390 x 464 phone canvas (22.06 px/unit) | nearest centre |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| pad (each) | 89.7 x 72.0 px | 60.8 px along a row, 79.6 across rows | 30.3 x 24.3 px | 20.5 px (26.9 across rows) |
| encoder | 41.1 x 46.6 | 53.2 | 13.9 x 15.7 | 18.0 |
| RUN/STOP, CLEAR | 57.6 x 43.2 | 53.2 | 19.5 x 14.6 | 18.0 |

| machine | 1063 x 774 px (73.8 % x 86 %) | | 359 x 261 px (92 % of 390, 56 % of 464) | |

Mitigations: R2-12 (Dock PAGES row), the Dock's instruments, RUN, CLEAR, OPEN and tempo, pinch zoom (2.4: pads 72.7 x 58.3 px, 49.2 px apart).

### 20.6 State machine

#### 20.6.1 Stores and values

| Store | Revision 2 |
|---|---|
| section (state/section.ts) | null, 'tracks', 'mixtapes', 'press', 'shows', 'contact', 'label', 'sonaa', 'studio', 'live' |
| theme types | `SectionId` as above; `PageId` = 'tracks' / 'mixtapes' / 'press' / 'shows' / 'contact' / 'label' / 'sonaa' (the seven page pads, keys 1 to 7, the sheet tabs, the Dock PAGES row); OPEN is a pad, not a section; `NavId` removed |
| SECTION_TITLES | TRACKS, MIXTAPES, PRESS, SHOWS, CONTACT, LABEL, SONAA, STUDIO, LIVE |
| view (state/view.ts, new) | { moved: boolean }, written by the orbit only when it flips; drives RESET VIEW and `data-v4-view` |
| mix (audio/drums.ts) | tone, level, swing, drive, reverb (0..1; not persisted) |
| explode, intro, pattern, clock, sc, lcdMessage, playhead | unchanged API |
| lcd | three lines (20.3.8) |

Root attributes: `data-v4-section` takes the new ids; new `data-v4-view` ("default" or "moved"). The rest unchanged.

#### 20.6.2 Events (changes to section 7.2)

| Event | Guard | Effects |
|---|---|---|
| TAP(h) (new) | pointerup, maxDist < 6 px, single pointer, same hotspot (no duration limit since 20.20 RV-3) | dispatch by kind: pad -> PAD_HIT; page -> PAGE; open -> OPEN_TOGGLE; step -> STEP_TOGGLE; run -> RUN_TOGGLE; clear -> CLEAR; chip -> CHIP; encoder -> ENC_TAP; none and outside the machine -> BG_TAP |
| PAD_HIT(inst) (was PAD_DOWN) | pointer TAP, key A S D F, pad twin | audio.trigger first, instrument = inst, press and flash |
| PAD_HOLD | removed | the open hat has no pointer path any more (R2-1) |
| PAGE(s) (replaces KNOB(s)) | pad TAP, keys 1 to 7, page twin, Dock PAGES row | section.toggle(s); the pad sinks; active pad yellowHi, previous faint; panel and trace (desktop) or sheet (mobile); the LCD line 1 shows the title |
| OPEN_TOGGLE | OPEN pad TAP, keys 8 and O, OPEN twin, Dock OPEN | rev 1 guard (closed or open only); the OPEN pad lights yellowHi while opening or open; the silk label reads CLOSE |
| CHIP(label) | explode open | new tab https://vrstlrecords.bandcamp.com (twin anchor; fallback `window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')`) |
| CHIP(live) | explode open | section.toggle('live'): panel with the trace from the chip |
| CHIP(studio) | explode open | section = 'studio' (unchanged), trace from the chip |
| ENC_SET(param, v) | vertical drag after the gate, wheel over the encoder, slider twin keys | TEMPO_SET, TONE_SET, LEVEL_SET (rev 1), SWING_SET, DIST_SET, REVERB_SET (new, 20.8); encoder angle; LCD line 3 readout 1200 ms |
| ENC_TAP | two taps within 350 ms on the same encoder | its start value: TEMPO 130, TONE 1, LEVEL 0.8, SWING 0, DIST 0, REVERB 0 |
| ORBIT (new) | pointer moved >= 6 px (gate true) | pending rotation, loop awake; any pending tap is void |
| PINCH (new) | second pointer down | zoom from the distance ratio; every pending tap of the gesture is void |
| WHEEL_ZOOM (new) | wheel not over an encoder | pending log-zoom |
| RESET_VIEW (new) | RESET VIEW button, key R, BG double tap | orbit.reset(): 500 ms easeOutCubic (cut under reduced motion) |
| BG_TAP (new) | TAP on no hotspot, outside the machine silhouette | a second one within 300 ms and 30 px: RESET_VIEW |
| KEY(1..8) | rev 1 guards | 1 to 7 PAGE in reading order (TRACKS, MIXTAPES, PRESS, SHOWS, CONTACT, LABEL, SONAA); 8 OPEN_TOGGLE; physical Digit and Numpad keys too (AZERTY) |
| KEY(R) (new) | rev 1 guards | RESET_VIEW |
| KEY(O), KEY(Space), KEY(A S D F) | unchanged | OPEN_TOGGLE, RUN_TOGGLE, PAD_HIT |
| ESC | unchanged | close the open section (the panel), else close the explode, else deselect |
| explode closes while LIVE or STUDIO is open | | the panel stays; the chip's anchor is inside the closed machine, the trace fades (20.2.7) |

### 20.7 Hit testing, hotspots and twins (scene/hit.ts, ui/Hotspots.tsx)

- Kinds: 'pad' (voices), 'page', 'open', 'step', 'run', 'clear', 'encoder', 'chip'. Removed: 'knob', 'tempo', 'tone', 'level' (merged into 'encoder' with `param`). HotspotDef gains `param?: EncId`, `section?: PageId | 'live' | 'studio'`, `shape: 'box' | 'disc'` unchanged.
- The list, always 39 entries, in the twins' Tab order: pads 12 (reading order), steps 16, run, clear, encoders 6 (TEMPO to REVERB), chips 3 (enabled while open, and during the opening once the panel is 85 % up). Twins: 36 while closed, 39 from "opening" to the end of "closing". (As implemented, 20.19 P-1: the order is the 12 pads (the 4 voices, then the 8 navigation pads in reading order), the 3 chips right after OPEN while they exist, the 6 encoders, RUN, CLEAR, the 16 steps; RESET VIEW follows in the Tab order.)
- New HitMap API:

```ts
addOccluder(o: { layer: Object3D; kind: 'box'; min: [number, number, number]; max: [number, number, number] }
          | { layer: Object3D; kind: 'planes'; planes: number[] }): void;  // panel slab (box), chassis wedge (6 planes)
pick(x: number, y: number, coarse: boolean): HotspotView | null;   // silhouettes, then depth along the view ray, occluders hide
visible(layer: Object3D, x: number, y: number, z: number, ignoreId?: string): boolean;  // trace anchor
onMachine(x: number, y: number): boolean;                           // background test (hull of the current layers' boxes)
rects(): Float32Array;                                              // twins, allocation-free (20.2.8)
version(): number;                                                  // rev 1
```

- Picking: candidates whose silhouette contains the point (rev 1); if more than one, or if an occluder exists, cast the view ray (orthographic: origin = the unprojected point on the near plane, direction = the camera's forward) into each candidate's layer frame, entry distance by slab test (discs as their boxes), drop candidates behind an occluder hit, keep the nearest (ties within 0.05 units: nearest centre, the rev 1 cluster rule). Coarse fallback (within 24 px of an edge): only candidates whose top centre is visible. Hover picks are skipped while `orbit.dragging`.
- Hit layer semantics: 20.2.3. Cursor: grab over the machine and the background, grabbing while orbiting, pointer over hotspots, ns-resize over encoders.
- Twins (ui/Hotspots.tsx `Twins`): 12 pad twins (voices: buttons "Bass drum pad, key A" to "Hi-hat pad, key F", aria-pressed = selected instrument; pages: buttons "Tracks, key 1" to "Sonaa, key 7", aria-expanded and aria-controls "v4-section-<id>"; OPEN: "Open the machine, key O", aria-pressed = opening or open), 16 step twins (rev 1), RUN ("Run, Space"), CLEAR, 6 encoder sliders (role slider, aria-orientation vertical; TEMPO 100 to 150 "130 BPM"; TONE, LEVEL, SWING, DIST, REVERB 0 to 100 "80 %"; SWING's valuetext "54 % swing" from the 50 to 70 % ratio), chips (LABEL anchor, LIVE and STUDIO buttons with aria-expanded). Rev 1 keyboard rules unchanged (Enter and Space, no key repeat, sliders on the arrows, Page keys, Home and End; Space on a slider is RUN/STOP; a pointer activation blurs a focused twin).

### 20.8 Audio: the six encoders

- TEMPO, TONE, LEVEL: rev 1 (sections 8.1 and 9), values unchanged.
- SWING (audio/clock.ts): value v in 0..1 -> swing ratio 50 + 20 v % -> every odd 16th (steps 2, 4, ..., 16) is scheduled at `when + 0.4 v x stepDur`; the ring's `expected` includes the same offset, so `drift()` stays under 1 ms; the playhead LED follows the swung time (entryAt reads `when`). Applied at the next step, like a tempo change. Not persisted.
- DIST (audio/fx.ts, new): after levelGain, a parallel drive: dry gain (1 - 0.45 d) and wet = pre-gain (1 + 5 d) -> WaveShaper tanh(x) (1024 points, oversample '2x') -> gain 0.55 d, summed into the compressor. d = 0: the wet gain is 0 (the rev 1 sound is intact).
- REVERB (audio/fx.ts): send gain 0.3 r from the post-drive sum -> ConvolverNode with a synthetic stereo impulse (1.4 s, exponential decay to -60 dB, 10 ms pre-delay, seeded noise, generated on the first r > 0 in about 2 ms) -> return straight into the analyser input (the analyser stays before master: ?mute=1 keeps working). The send is connected only while r > 0.
- All three glide over 20 ms linear ramps (the rev 1 `glide`, section 19 item 51). drums.ts graph after revision 2: voices -> bus -> tone -> level -> [dry + drive] -> compressor -> analyser -> master -> destination, and [dry + drive] -> reverb send -> convolver -> analyser.
- Decorative fallback: if the mobile tier shows an audio or frame cost regression with REVERB on (a 2 minute RUN at 390 x 844 with `drift().lateCount > 0` or `dropped > 0`), REVERB stays connected on desktop only and the report says so, as the brief asks.
- Debug: `__v4.audio.swing`, `.drive`, `.reverb`, `.fx` ({ driveOn, reverbOn, irSeconds }).
- As implemented (stage 3 of 5): section 20.17 FX-1 to FX-4. SWING delays the even steps by up to a third of a step (ratio 50 to 67 %), DIST sits right after the voice bus (before TONE and LEVEL) with a wide tanh curve and no oversampling, REVERB is a 1.2 s impulse on a send after LEVEL, the three values persist with the pattern.
