### 20.13 Acceptance for revision 2 (always ?debug=1&mute=1, master gain 0, no row clicked, links cancelled by a capturing listener)

Run on the final code: section 20.19 P-4 (the client's tests T1 to T7, which cover R2-A1 to R2-A8), P-5 (R2-A9) and P-6; R2-A10 was checked by stage 4 (20.18 C-5 to C-8) and again in the fallback (P-6).

R2-A1 (T1). After one real click (user activation for the muted context): a synthetic pointerdown and pointerup on `.v4-hit` at pad-BD's centre, 2 px apart, 150 ms apart, with pointerType 'touch' at 390 x 844 and 'mouse' at 1440 x 900: `__v4.audio.triggers` + 1, analyser peak > 0.01 within 50 ms of the pointerup, `data-v4-instrument="BD"`. Same start, then moves of 30 px over 200 ms, then pointerup: triggers unchanged, `__v4.orbit.azimuth` changed. A still press held 450 ms: nothing fires. A press on an encoder then a vertical drag: the value changes and the azimuth does not; a horizontal drag from the same encoder: the azimuth changes and the value does not. (Superseded by 20.17 FX-5: a horizontal drag from an encoder turns it too, right = more, and the azimuth does not change.)
R2-A2 (T2). `orbit.set(az, 38)` for az 0 to 355 by 5, then 18 and 78 deg at 8 azimuths: each frame rendered synchronously and read back: no pixel of the exact clear colour (10, 10, 11) inside the machine's projected hull (no hole); floor pixels along the base darker than the ink (the contact shadow follows); at azimuth 180, elevation 25, the projected centres of the jack nuts, the USB port and the inlet read their metal and ink colours, distinct from the chassis.
R2-A3 (T3). Open TRACKS (desktop), then orbit through 8 azimuths: the trace path changes on every rendered frame, the panel's getBoundingClientRect is identical before and after, `__v4.trace.visible` is false (and the svg opacity 0 after 150 ms) whenever the anchor is occluded or out of frame; LIVE opened from its chip, then the machine closed: the trace fades, the panel stays.
R2-A4 (T4). OPEN, then 8 azimuths x elevations 18, 38, 60: renders read back, the chips' visible fractions as in 20.3.11; the layer clearance function sampled every 16 ms over the opening and closing timelines never goes under 0.05 units; the lifted panel's underside is never front-facing.
R2-A5 (T5). 390 x 844: one touch pointer dragged 80 px changes the azimuth; two touch pointers spreading from 100 to 200 px double the zoom (clamped at 2.4); with the sheet open, the same one-finger drag on the canvas band rotates; no page scroll or zoom (scrollWidth 390, visualViewport.scale 1).
R2-A6 (T6). 15 `.v4-social` anchors, in the brief's order with its exact hrefs, target _blank, rel "noopener noreferrer", aria-label = the service, each holding one svg with one path and no text node; grid rows: 2 on desktop (8 + 7), 3 on mobile (5 + 5 + 5); hover and focus-visible colour yellow after 160 ms.
R2-A7 (T7). RUN, then an orbit driven for 120 s (`orbit.set` stepping the azimuth 1 deg per frame on the 16 ms rAF shim, plus real pointer drags at the start and the end): `drift()` maxMs < 1, lateCount 0, dropped 0; `renderer.info.memory` geometries and textures constant; `performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize` sampled every 10 s without a monotonic growth above 2 MB.
R2-A8 (idle). After an orbit release, frames stop within 1.5 s and stay constant over 2 s; same after RESET VIEW, a pinch and a wheel zoom.
R2-A9 (budgets). `__v4.stats.maxDrawCalls` <= 20 desktop and <= 17 mobile in the closed view on a frame with the shadow pass (14 and 12 expected); triangles <= 60k and 35k; `npm run build`: /v4 <= 230 KB gzip with three (report the exact figure, same method as 14.3); the dash, emoji and ASCII greps of acceptance 25 print nothing.
R2-A10 (items 4 to 6). LABEL chip twin: anchor to https://vrstlrecords.bandcamp.com, target _blank, rel "noopener noreferrer"; LIVE chip opens `data-v4-section="live"` with the exact French copy (compared as text with the escapes decoded), two `a[download]` with the right hrefs and the sizes 0.9 MB and 4.5 MB, /press/ and /techrider in new tabs; SONAA pad opens `data-v4-section="sonaa"` with its three lines and the sonaa.ca link; keys 1 to 8 and R behave per 20.6.2; the five rev 1 knob twins, their LEDs and KEY(1..5)-as-knobs no longer exist.

### 20.14 Risks and owner questions

1. Tap latency and the 400 ms limit (R2-1). A pad sounds at release; a deliberate press held over 400 ms plays nothing (brief rule). T1 "press slowly" must be run with a press under 400 ms; `__v4.orbit.lastTap` records distance and duration so the limit can be tuned if real presses run longer. Alternative if the owner prefers: fire a still press when it reaches 400 ms instead of dropping it. (Closed by 20.20 RV-3: a still press fires at its release, whatever its length.)
2. Phone pad pitch 20.5 px at zoom 1 (20.3.12): mis-taps between neighbouring pads on a 390 px phone. Mitigated by the 48 px target rects, nearest centre, pinch zoom and the Dock PAGES row (R2-12, owner may veto).
3. Encoders on the phone (14 x 16 px, 18 px apart): turning one needs a precise start; the Dock keeps the tempo; the gate lets horizontal drags orbit. (As shipped after 20.20 RV-4: on touch an encoder turns only after the finger rests on it for 250 ms; a quicker drag orbits.)
4. Default elevation 38 (R2-4) with the taller wedge: at 1440 x 900 the machine is height-bound, 73.8 % of the width (rev 1: 78 %); 58.4 % at 1920 x 950. Option: FIT_H 0.9 (77.3 % at 1440 x 900) at the cost of a tighter fit during a full turn (the 38 deg worst case is 12.07 of 13.15 units). Owner question.
5. Steep views crop at zoom 1 on landscape screens (R2-5). Option: an orientation-dependent height guard (the machine shrinks toward 78 deg), rejected by default because the scale would breathe while turning.
6. Occlusion picking (R2-11) is new code in the input path: wrong depth order would steal taps near tall encoders at low elevation. Mitigation: the ray test only runs when several silhouettes contain the point or an occluder exists; acceptance probes at 8 azimuths.
7. Twins every frame (R2-9): 39 absolutely positioned empty elements restyled per orbit frame; expected under 0.3 ms, measured at R1 on the phone tier; fallback: write transforms per frame and sizes at rest.
8. Colour calibration (20.3.10): every lit colour is new; the calibration by read-back is mandatory and takes time; the brief's material values are kept and the gains re-derived.
9. Shadow: the key light's shadow becomes visible from the back (the machine no longer hides it); acne or peter-panning on the chassis at grazing elevations must be checked at 18 deg (bias -0.0004 and normalBias 0.02 kept, tune if needed); the shadow frustum margin is small for the open stack (|y| 10.38 of 11: extent 11.5).
10. Hidden preview pane: rAF barely fires, canvas screenshots lie. Inertia, idle and T7 need the 16 ms rAF shim; geometry proof is read-back plus projection only.
11. iOS Safari: pointer events and `touch-action: none` on the hit layer must stop page pinch; if a real device still zooms the page, add `gesturestart` preventDefault on the stage (outside the brief's list of events, a one-liner, to be justified in the report).
12. French copy and the ASCII rule: entities in JSX text only (a string literal holding "&eacute;" would print it raw); acceptance R2-A10 compares the rendered text.
13. Reverb CPU on low-end phones (R2-17): lazily connected, desktop-only fallback reported if needed.
14. Removed features (deviations): parallax, the CH open-hat hold, the five knobs, the socle mention plane, the OPEN button frame, the old LIVE navigation to /techrider. The Dock PAGES row and the back fill light are additions.
15. Chunk: about 212 KB expected; the Simple Icons paths are about 4.5 KB gzip and cannot be cut; the connectors' detail is the brief's lever.
16. PCB visibility from behind is 0 to 9 % at 38 deg (the lifted panel covers it): T4 asks for "several angles", met from the front half, the sides and the low back corners.

How to rerun the numbers of this section: three small Node scripts load `node_modules/three/build/three.cjs` (read-only), build the plateau frame (position (0, 1.87, 0), rotation.x atan(0.1)), an OrthographicCamera at (azimuth, elevation, distance 30) around the target, and project the hotspot boxes (20.3.12), the open stack and the chip tops against the lifted slab (20.3.11), and the stacks in the key light's shadow camera (20.2.6). They live in the builder's scratchpad; any layout change reruns them in seconds.

### 20.15 Stage R1 (the orbit, on the revision 1 machine): as implemented

Scope, as 20.12 R1: scene/orbit.ts, parallax removed, the tap rule for every hotspot (pads on release), the floor with halo, fog and shadows, the shadow autoUpdate rule, twins in the render pass (hit.rects), trace anchors with the visibility fade, RESET VIEW, key R, background double tap, `__v4.orbit`, `data-v4-view`. The machine is still the revision 1 machine (plateau on socle, 4 pads, 5 nav knobs, TEMPO, TONE, LEVEL, LCD, OPEN); stage R2 rebuilds it. Created: scene/orbit.ts, scene/floor.ts, state/view.ts, ui/ResetView.tsx. Modified: theme.ts, scene/renderer.ts, scene/hit.ts, scene/materials.ts (withContactShadow moved into the floor shader), ui/Hotspots.tsx, ui/Trace.tsx, actions.ts (padDown renamed padHit, padHold removed, resetView added), hooks/useKeys.ts (R), index.tsx, debug.ts, v4.css. src/App.tsx, package.json, public and dist untouched (the build check writes into the builder's scratchpad).

R1-1. Orbit controller (scene/orbit.ts): the API of 20.2.2 and the parameters of 20.2.1 (theme.ts `ORBIT`), plus `listen()` (the Stage attaches the five listeners only after all its GL work succeeded, the revision 1 rule that a failed constructor leaves no listener) and `place()` (position and look-at only; the Stage calls it when the explode raises the pivot, without touching the zoom). Additions: the hard stop compares the 60 fps step (|pending| x 0.08 < 0.001), so it does not depend on the frame rate; while a pointer is down and resting, a remainder whose step is under 0.0001 is applied at once (at most 0.07 deg, invisible), so a resting finger stops the loop (1.06 s after the last move, measured) instead of rendering tiny steps for ever; a primary pointerdown clears stale pointer records; under reduced motion the gesture applies in the same frame (no glide, nothing dropped). It is 374 lines (280 without comments and blank lines), not the brief's "about 120": the tap rule (isTap), the encoder gate, the pinch re-anchoring and the test setter of the plan live there too, and nothing else does (no pan, no keys, no damping options).

R1-2. Pivot and framing measured on the real vertices of the revision 1 machine (knobs included) at the default view: `ORBIT.targetY` 0.713 (projected centre of the closed machine; the plan's 1.16 is the wedge's), `MACHINE_H` 10.9 (9.85 at 30.5 deg), `EXPLODE.fitHalfH` 7.83 and `EXPLODE.targetY` 0.846 (the open stack is 13.47 units tall at the default view; `EXPLODE.shiftY` removed), `SECTION_FRAME.radius` 8.23 closed and open (largest horizontal radius of any vertex; the revision 1 tilt about the screen axis does not widen it). Stage R2 re-measures all of them for the wedge (plan: 1.16, 11.84, 8.49, about 2.7, 8.35 and 9.23). Full turn at 38 deg: closed at most 11.14 units tall against a 13.03 unit frustum (1440 x 900); 16.2 units at 78 deg (cropped at zoom 1, accepted by R2-5). `CAMERA` stays in theme.ts for the fallback SVG only.

R1-3. Section framing as 20.2.5: stageW = max(W / 3, panelLeft(W) - 16), hw = max(hw, W R / stageW) blended by the 400 ms tween, frustum offsets divided by camera.zoom; `LCD_RIGHT_SX` and the gutters are gone. At 1440 x 900 with TRACKS open: 56.6 px per unit, the machine box from x 12 to 920 px, the panel at 948. Right edge of every visible vertex over a full turn at 38 deg (zoom 1): 1280 x 800, at most 772 px against the panel at 788 (12 azimuths); 1024 x 768 (34.8 px per unit), at most 572 against 588, closed and open (24 azimuths): the 16 px gap holds at the worst azimuths, where the bounding circle touches it.

R1-4. Floor (scene/floor.ts): one opaque 120 x 120 plane, `ShadowMaterial` patched (transparent false, toneMapped false, cache key v4Floor), child of socleGroup (in revision 1 it still sinks with the socle during the explode; R4 keeps the chassis still). Shader of 20.2.6 with the revision 1 contact footprint (14.3 x 9.3, corners 0.8, falloff 1.6, opacity 0.45; R2 re-parameterises it for the wedge), halo #121215 at the centre (radius 9), cast shadow 0.5, both shadows multiplied by exp(-(0.045 r)^2). The brief's "light exponential fog" (the stage brief says FogExp2) is this exponential squared falloff inside the floor shader, radial from the machine: a scene FogExp2 works on view depth and would fog the machine as well (it sits 30 units from the camera). One draw call, in place of the shadow plane.

R1-5. Shadow: key light fixed in the world, `LIGHT_KEY.extent` 11.5. Light-space extents of the casters (`measure().shadow`): closed |x| 7.69, |y| 7.29, open |x| 7.69, |y| 8.25 (desktop casters), depth 7.3 to 23.1 against near 1 and far 40; the shadow camera does not depend on the view, so every azimuth is covered. `shadowMap.autoUpdate = false`; invalidate() and every animator frame set needsUpdate, an orbit-only frame reuses the map.

R1-6. Tap rule for all 34 hotspots, pads included (the sound starts at the release; the CH hold is gone: `PAD_FX.holdMs`, `padHold`, `PRESS_SLOP_PX` and `TEMPO_UI.slopPx` removed). TEMPO, TONE and LEVEL turn on a vertical drag through `orbit.gate` from the 6 px threshold, the value computed from the pointerdown point (no jump at the threshold); a sideways drag from them orbits; the wheel over them turns them (Ctrl + wheel zooms the view); two taps within 350 ms reset them. The hit layer captures every pointer, reads `orbit.isTap(e)` in its own pointerup (before the orbit's, which listens on the parent) and fires only if the release picks the same hotspot. Cursor: grab (CSS), grabbing while orbiting, pointer over a hotspot, ns-resize over a dial; hover picking is skipped while dragging.

R1-7. Occlusion-aware picking (R2-11, planned for R4, brought forward because the orbit already shows the open machine from behind, where a tap went to a hidden chip, a link): candidates whose silhouette contains the point are ordered along the view ray (analytic box and vertical cylinder tests in each layer's frame), the occluders (plateau slab, socle, PCB board boxes, `hit.addOccluder`) hide what lies behind them, ties within 0.05 units go to the nearest centre, and the touch fallback keeps only candidates whose top centre is visible. `hit.visible()`, `hit.onMachine()` (hull of the visible occluder boxes), `hit.rightEdge()`, `hit.project()` and `hit.rects()` share the per-layer matrices prepared once per view change.

R1-8. Twins in the render pass: `hit.rects()` (Float32Array, no allocation; exact projected ellipses for a cylinder instead of the plan's bounding box, and list() uses the same rects, so both agree to 0.0 px). Twins write transform, width and height of the twins whose value changed (0.1 px) in `stage.onView`, every rendered frame; the review rule "twins wait for the view to settle" is dropped (R2-9).

R1-9. Trace (20.2.7): anchors are the nav knob tops and, brought forward from R2-16 so that the occlusion fade could be tested on real geometry, the STUDIO chip top. `stage.projectAnchor(section, out)` (8 px canvas margin, visible layer, `hit.visible()` ray toward the camera against the occluders and the other hotspot volumes) and `stage.machineRightEdge()`; Trace.tsx adds x < panelLeft - 12, writes `data-visible` only when it flips (CSS fade 150 ms each way) and never moves the panel. `__v4.trace` gains anchor, visible and fades.

R1-10. RESET VIEW (ui/ResetView.tsx): always mounted, hidden by opacity 0 and `visibility: hidden` (out of the Tab order and the accessibility tree) while the view is the default one, 150 ms fade (none under reduced motion); the plan said "rendered only while moved": same result, and the fade out needs the element. Desktop right 24, bottom 24 px; mobile right 12 px, top 408 px (the bottom of the 464 px canvas band). Key R and the background double tap (two taps within 300 ms and 30 px, outside the machine hull and on no hotspot) call the same action; a double tap on the machine body does nothing.

R1-11. Not in R1 (later stages of 20.12): the back fill light (R2-13, with the colour recalibration of stage R2), the Dock PAGES row (R3), the wedge, 12 pads, encoders and OLED (R2 and R3), the explode on the wedge (R4). Parallax is removed (R2-3): class, constants, listeners and `measure().parallax`.

R1-12. Debug (20.11): `__v4.orbit` { azimuth, elevation, zoom, moving, dragging, target, lastTap { dist, ms, fired }, lastUp { id, tap, fired, bg }, bgResets, reset(), set(az, el, zoom, instant) }; `__v4.stats` maxDrawCalls, maxTriangles, shadowUpdates, reset(); `__v4.state.view`; `measure()` orbit, framing.targetY, shadow (framing.oy and parallax removed); root attribute `data-v4-view`.

R1-13. Verification (?debug=1&mute=1, master gain 0 throughout, hidden preview pane with the shim of section 19 item 87, synthetic pointer events dispatched on .v4-hit with bubbles; a real click on the RESET VIEW button):
- T1, desktop (mouse): a still tap on pad-BD (2 px, 150 ms): no trigger during the press, one at the release, analyser peak 0.60, signal 13.8 ms after the pointerup, data-v4-instrument BD. A 30 px drag in 200 ms from the same pad: no trigger, peak 0.000 throughout, azimuth 45 to 39.8. A still press of 450 ms: nothing (lastTap 453 ms). TEMPO: 30 px up gave 130 to 145 BPM with the azimuth unchanged; 40 px sideways from TEMPO turned the azimuth 45 to 37.7 with the tempo unchanged; a double tap gave 130.
- T5, 390 x 844 (touch): a tap on pad-SD fired at the release (peak 0.564, 13.2 ms); a drag from it: no trigger, peak 0, azimuth 45 to 34.0; one finger 80 px: azimuth 45 to 14.7 (31.0 deg asked, the hard stop drops at most 0.72); two fingers from 100 to 200 px: zoom 1.98, 2.4 beyond; the remaining finger after a pinch neither rotates nor taps; with the sheet open a one-finger drag on the canvas band rotates; scrollWidth 390, visualViewport.scale 1; RESET VIEW at 272 to 378 x 408 to 452 px.
- Orbit and idle: an 80 px mouse drag gives azimuth 29.7 (16 deg asked); 0 frames 536 to 636 ms after the release; 0 frames and 0 rAFs over 2 s at rest; elevation clamps 18 and 78; wheel: one notch zoom 1.148, clamps 2.4 and 0.55, Ctrl + wheel x 6, over TEMPO the wheel turned it (+1 BPM) and did not zoom; key R: 500 ms easeOutCubic back to 45 / 38 / 1 (elevation 18 to 38 in 524 ms), data-v4-view "default", the button hidden; a background double tap reset the view, one on the machine body did not. Reduced motion: the drag follows with no glide (idle 52 ms after the release), reset in one frame.
- Picking: all enabled hotspots pick themselves at their top centre at 6 views of the closed machine (31 of 31) and at the default view of the open one (34 of 34); open, from 225 and 270 deg at 38 and from 135 deg at 60 the chips under the lifted plateau are not pickable (the tap goes to the pad above or to nothing), from 180 deg at 18 they are.
- Twins: over a 63-frame orbit every twin equals its hotspot rect on every frame (0.0 px); twins plus trace cost 0.2 ms median, 0.3 ms at worst per frame (1440 x 900).
- Trace: TRACKS open through 8 azimuths, the path rewritten on every frame, the panel rect identical; CONTACT at zoom 2.4 faded when the knob passed under the panel or out of the canvas and came back at zoom 1; STUDIO (machine open) hidden from 180 deg at 38, 225 and 270 (the lifted plateau), visible elsewhere; closing the machine with STUDIO open faded the trace and left the panel in place.
- Floor at elevation 18, azimuths 0, 90, 180, 270, zoom 1 and 0.55 (desktop): 5 full-height columns read back per view; outside the machine's projected hull (6 px margin) no two neighbouring device pixels differ by more than 2 levels (halo up to 15, shadows down to 8) and the top and bottom rows are exactly the clear colour 10,10,11: no horizon line. Snapshots of the WebGL buffer (toDataURL shown in an image overlay, the canvas screenshot of the hidden pane being unreliable) looked right from the four sides, low and high, closed and open.
- Drift during an orbit: RUN, then 20 s of orbit (azimuth 1 deg per 16 ms tick, elevation swept 18 to 50) between two real-speed drags: 209 steps, drift maxMs 1.8e-10, lateCount 0, dropped 0; geometries 12 and textures 6 constant (the two-minute heap sampling is R7's).
- Budgets: desktop closed 16 calls and 13,244 triangles on a frame with the shadow pass, 12 and 9,952 on an orbit-only frame (0 shadow updates over a drag and its glide), open 18 and 15,140; mobile 14 and 9,960, orbit-only 12 and 8,320, open 16 and 11,648. Build (`vite build` into the scratchpad, exit 0): /v4 204.6 kB gzip with three (204,589 bytes by gzip -c: lazy chunk 143,623 raw / 49,908 gzip, three 144,258, CSS 3,715, AudioPlayerContext 1,330, contacts 3,534, mixtapes 1,844), +4.9 kB on the review pass. The dash, emoji and ASCII greps print nothing.
- Unmount: / then /v4 again (SPA): `__v4LastDispose` 0 / 0, the orbit's five listeners on .v4-stage added and removed in pairs, no canvas and no RESET VIEW left on /, the new Stage at the default view.

### 20.16 Stage R2 (the Rytm body and layout): as implemented

Scope, as 20.12 R2 plus what the orchestrated stage 2 of 5 asked for on top of it: the explode adapted to the wedge (plan R4 for the layers and framing), the OLED on three lines with the encoder readouts (plan R3 and R6 for the display only), the LABEL and SONAA sections (plan R3: the new pads need a destination) and the full StaticMachine (plan R7). Created: scene/encoders.ts, ui/sections/Label.tsx, ui/sections/Sonaa.tsx. Rewritten: theme.ts, scene/machine.ts, scene/pads.ts, scene/sequencer3d.ts, scene/screen.ts, scene/silk.ts, scene/explode.ts, scene/renderer.ts (the Stage class), ui/Hotspots.tsx (hit layer and twins), hooks/useKeys.ts, actions.ts, state/lcd.ts, fallback/StaticMachine.tsx. Modified: scene/materials.ts, scene/hit.ts, scene/pcb.ts, scene/floor.ts (comment), state/lcdMessage.ts, state/section.ts, audio/drums.ts (mix values), data.ts, debug.ts, ui/Lcd.tsx, ui/Panel.tsx, ui/sections/common.tsx, fallback/NoWebGL.tsx, index.tsx (comment), v4.css (fallback silk). Deleted: scene/knobs.ts. src/App.tsx, package.json, public and dist untouched (the build check writes into the builder's scratchpad); git untouched.

R2-1. Body (20.3.2, 20.3.3). Chassis: the 8-point chamfered wedge profile extruded along x with a 0.06 bevel (every edge chamfered), 14 x 9 on the floor, underside 0.12, top = the panel's underside plane (2.179 back, 1.279 front, `chassisTopY(z)`), tilt atan(0.1) = 5.711 deg; four feet r 0.42 x 0.12 at (+/-6.45, +/-3.95); back connectors (power inlet with recess and three pins, USB-B frame and socket, two jacks with hex nut, barrel and hole) merged with it: one draw call, 688 triangles. Panel: rounded slab 13.8 x 8.845 + 0.1 bevel, 0.14 thick, OLED bezel merged, `MeshStandardMaterial` roughness 0.62, metalness 0.35, vertex colours, `roughnessMap` = the brushing texture: 296 triangles. The brushing canvas is 4 x 256 (one grey per row, 0.86 to 1, a streak at 0.8 or 1 every 12 rows, seed 808, `NoColorSpace`, repeat 0.5 per unit, mipmaps and anisotropy): the plan's 256 x 256 held the same rows, constant along x. Seen at zoom 2.4 and at grazing views it reads as fine horizontal streaks where the rim and key lights catch it, invisible at the default view.

R2-2. Deviation, the chassis is a tray. The plan hid the PCB "inside the solid chassis" and let it rise through the chassis top face (20.3.11), i.e. through a surface. `toTray()` (scene/machine.ts) removes the wedge's flat top (its exact outline is read from its triangles in the panel frame) and puts back a 0.18 rim, four inner walls and a floor 0.55 lower, all parallel to the panel (18 triangles, same draw call). The PCB sleeps in the tray: `LAYERS.pcbY` 1.528 (board top 0.1 below the rim, 0.35 above the floor; the plan's 1.629 put the board top on the rim). Opening 13.52 x 8.51 in the panel frame, board 12.6 x 7.8: 0.316 of margin at the closest point.

R2-3. Explode on the wedge (20.3.11, R2-8): the panel group rises 3.6, slides back 1.5 and turns from +5.711 to -12 deg about the machine's x axis through its centre; the PCB rises 0.9 out of the tray, parts scale 0.001 to 1; the chassis and the floor never move. Opening: panel first, PCB 80 ms later; closing: PCB first, panel 80 ms later; 900 ms easeInOutQuart per layer, 980 ms in all; framing progress max(panel, PCB). `applyLayers()` is exported for tests. Checked on the real meshes (every vertex of the PCB parts and the board corners), sampled every 16 ms over both timelines (126 poses): the panel's underside stays at least 0.099 above every PCB point (the minimum is the closed pose; it only grows while moving), every PCB point below the rim plane stays inside the tray opening (margin 0.316) and 0.35 above its floor, the panel's underside never goes below the rim plane over the chassis footprint. No surface is crossed at any time, so no orbit angle can show an interpenetration. The underside of the lifted panel faces away from the camera for elevations >= 18 deg (plan, unchanged).

R2-4. PCB re-placed for the visible band of the open default view (board z 0 to 3.9 and the strip x > 4.4): chips LABEL (-3.4, 2.6), LIVE (0, 2.6), STUDIO (3.4, 2.6); U4 and U5 beside them, C1, C2, X1, X2 in the gaps of the chip row, the ten resistors at z 1.25 (x -4.6 to 3.5), BT1, C3, C4 and U6 in the right strip, U7, C5, C6 under the panel; silk MAUDITE MACHINE and MM-808 REV 4.0 at z 0.4, V.4 2026 at (6.1, 1.25). The router re-routes around them (24 traces). The PCB silk keeps its 700 weight and 0.18 em tracking (`PCB_TYPE`); `drawTracked()` and `trackedWidth()` take the weight and tracking.

R2-5. Panel layout as 20.3.4: OLED glass 3.6 x 1.35 at (-4.5, -2.35), six encoders at x -6.0 + 0.98 i, z -0.95, labels at z -0.45; RUN/STOP and CLEAR squares 0.62 at (-6.0, 0.35) and (-5.02, 0.35), labels at z 0.85; pads x 0.47 + 1.12 c, rows z -2.4 and -1.0, labels 0.62 below each pad; VOICES and PAGES at z -3.05 over BD and TRACKS; the separator polyline and the four trig brackets; trig LEDs at z 1.72, keys at z 2.5, numbers 1 to 16 at z 3.18; MAUDITE MACHINE (600), MM-808 and V.4 / 2026 (45 %) along the back. Silk: SF Pro Display 500, tracking 0.2 em, bone 85 %, lines bone 35 % 0.012 wide, canvas 2048 x 1323 (1024 x 662 on the mobile tier) over the whole 14 x 9.045 panel; `FONT_LOADS` gains 500 and 600; the pad labels share the size of the widest (MIXTAPES), the encoder and transport labels share theirs. The OPEN pad label reads CLOSE from the start of the opening to the start of the closing (two redraws per cycle). The socle mention plane and the `Mention` class are gone.

R2-6. Pads (20.3.5): one InstancedMesh of 12 (RoundedBoxGeometry 0.96 x 0.22 x 0.96, 3 segments desktop and 2 mobile, merged with the 6 x 6 dome cap; 660 / 372 triangles each), one InstancedMesh of 12 additive halos (1.25 quads, 64 x 64 rounded-square alpha texture from `makeHaloTexture()`). Reading order BD SD TOM CH TRACKS MIXTAPES / PRESS SHOWS CONTACT LABEL SONAA OPEN; hotspot ids `pad-BD` ... `pad-open`, kinds `pad` (voices, `inst`), `page` (`section`), `open`. Every pad sinks 0.06 on a tap (60 ms, back in 180 ms; light only under reduced motion). Backlight read back at 1440 x 900, default view (top of the dome): voice off 19,17,16 (#111113 is 17,17,19), voice selected 30,29,26 (the rubber plus 12 levels, warm white), voice flash 241,193,74 (#F2C230 is 242,194,48: its blue is out of gamut under ACES, as the revision 1 flash), page and OPEN at rest 40,34,19 (about 10 % yellow: the plan's 15 % read as gold pads next to the black voices), page hover 86,71,24 (about 30 %), page active and OPEN open 246,223,124 (#FFD75E is 255,215,94, same gamut limit). Halo intensities are lower than the plan's (faint 0.025, hover 0.06, active 0.35, flash 0.45, selected 0.025 against 0.15, 0.6, 0.9): the additive quad writes into the sRGB output, 0.06 already added 30 levels next to the pad.

R2-7. Keys (20.3.6): one InstancedMesh of 18 (RoundedBoxGeometry 0.52 x 0.1 x 0.9, one segment, 108 triangles; RUN and CLEAR are instances 16 and 17 scaled square), one InstancedMesh of 16 flat LED rectangles 0.22 x 0.07 (MeshBasicMaterial, the exact tokens). Sequencer behaviour unchanged (LED rules of 7.5, playhead from the audio clock, intro chase). Read back: key 31,30,37 (#1E1F23), RUN 198,69,47 (#C8442F), RUN running 241,198,83 (yellow, blue out of gamut), CLEAR 28,26,31 (#1C1D21), LED at the playhead 255,215,94 exactly. On the mobile layout the 16 step hotspots stay disabled (the Dock edits the steps).

R2-8. Encoders (20.3.7): one InstancedMesh of 6 (slightly conical body r 0.3 to 0.285, 0.42 tall, bone marker toward -z, collar r 0.36; 268 triangles each, 20 segments on the mobile tier), angle `potAngle(t)` about the panel normal (270 deg, clockwise as the value rises). TEMPO, TONE and LEVEL drive the rev 1 parameters; SWING, DIST and REVERB turn and keep their value in `mix` (audio/drums.ts: `setSwing`, `setDrive`, `setReverb`, `mix.swing`, `.drive`, `.reverb`, `__v4.audio.swing`, `.drive`, `.reverb`), with no effect on the sound yet: the swing, drive and reverb processing is the audio stage's (plan R6). Interaction for all six through `actions.dial(id, v)`: vertical drag from the 6 px gate (TEMPO 2 px per BPM, the others 150 px for the range), wheel over the encoder, slider twins (arrows, Page, Home, End), double tap = start value (130 BPM, TONE 1, LEVEL 0.8, the three others 0). Hotspot ids `enc-tempo` ... `enc-reverb`, kind `encoder` with `param`. Read back: encoder top 17,16,15 (#101012).

R2-9. OLED (20.3.8): plane 3.6 x 1.35, CanvasTexture 640 x 240 (the glass's proportions, no compression), bone on #050506, monospace 40 px, baselines 64, 136, 208; at most 4 draws per second and only on change (the rev 1 guard). state/lcd.ts composes three lines: line 1 section or MM-808 and the BPM, line 2 SKIPPED / LOADING / title and timecode / RUN / PAUSED / READY (+ instrument), line 3 the transient message (STEP 07 BD ON, CLEARED, TAP A PAD FIRST, NO SIGNAL) or the value of the encoder being turned for 1200 ms (TONE 80%, LEVEL 64%, SWING 54% from the 50 to 70 % ratio, DIST 20%, REVERB 35%; TEMPO is already on line 1). `lcdMessage.show(text, ms, param)`; the LCD twin announces line 3 only when it is a message, not a readout. `lcd.get().text`, `__v4.state.lcd` and `__v4.screen.text` are 3-tuples. Measured: SWING nudged from the keyboard showed "SWING 54%" on line 3 and cleared after 1.2 s; 50 tempo changes in 1.5 s gave 8 redraws of the texture, the smallest gap 250.7 ms.

R2-10. Navigation state (20.6): `SectionId` = the seven `PageId` (tracks, mixtapes, press, shows, contact, label, sonaa) plus studio; `NavId`, `NAV_KNOBS` and the knob section code are gone. `page(id, stage)` toggles the section and sinks the pad; the active page pad is yellowHi, the others faint, one at a time (`Pads.setActivePage`); OPEN lights yellowHi from "opening" to "closing" (`Pads.setOpen`). The trace anchors are the page pads' top centres (and the STUDIO chip). Keys: A S D F voices, 1 to 7 pages in reading order and 8 OPEN (physical Digit and Numpad keys too), O OPEN, R reset view, Space RUN/STOP, Escape closes. The sheet's tab strip lists the seven pages. LABEL section: "VRSTL Records" and the Bandcamp link (new tab, noopener noreferrer, chevron); SONAA section, `lang="fr"`, the brief's two French lines written with JSX entities and the sonaa.ca link: both built now so their pads open something. Not in this stage: the LIVE chip panel and its section, the LABEL chip's Bandcamp link and hover chevron, the Dock PAGES row, the social icons (next stages).

R2-11. Hit testing and twins (20.7): 39 hotspots in Tab order (12 pads, 16 steps, RUN, CLEAR, 6 encoders, 3 chips); twins 36 closed, 39 from "opening" to the end of "closing"; pad twins "Bass drum pad, key A" to "Hi-hat pad, key F" (aria-pressed), "Tracks, key 1" to "Sonaa, key 7" (aria-expanded, aria-controls), "Open the machine, key O" (aria-pressed); encoder twins are sliders (Tempo 100 to 150 "130 BPM", the others 0 to 100 "80 %", "54 % swing"). Occluders: the panel slab (box), the chassis wedge as a convex polyhedron (six half-spaces and its eight corners, `hit.addOccluder({ layer, planes, corners })`; every occluder is now stored as half-spaces), the PCB board. Self-pick of every enabled hotspot at its top centre: 36 of 36 closed at 8 views (0, 45, 90, 180, 270 deg at 38, 45 deg at 18 and 78, 200 deg at 20), 39 of 39 open at the default and front views; from the sides and behind, the chips under the lifted panel are not pickable, as 20.3.11 predicts. Twins equal their hotspot rects (0.0 px) at 4 views.

R2-12. Lights and calibration (20.3.10). Per-tone albedo gains (`GAIN`, `gainOf()`), read back at 1440 x 900 on the default view: body 7.5 (chassis front 11,10,10 and right 15,14,14 for #0C0C0E), bodyEdge 3 (top chamfers 26 to 52, #2A2B30 is 42,43,48), panel 4.2 (top 22,21,21 for #141417), panelEdge 2.3 (bevel 50,48,49 at the front, 63,61,61 on the right, #3A3B40 is 58,59,64), pad 1.8, encoder and collar 2.4. Back fill light (R2-13): DirectionalLight bone from (-5, 2.5, -12), intensity 0.9, no shadow: at the plan's (-5, 7, -12) and 0.35 the back face read 2,2,2 (a black wall under the connectors) and raising that light brightened the panel top; grazing and stronger, the back face reads 11,10,10 like the other sides while the calibrated top stays 21,20,20. At azimuth 180, elevation 25: jack nuts, USB frame and pins 142 to 150 grey, sockets and holes 0,0,0, against the back face. Floor contact shadow re-parameterised for the 14 x 9 wedge (corner 0.1, falloff 1.4) and raised to opacity 0.6: the floor 0.25 outside the base reads 8,8,9 to 8,8,10 at 45/38, 225/38, 0/18 and 180/18 (the ink is 10,10,11). Silk: the brightest pixel of the wordmark reads 183,178,169.

R2-13. Framing constants measured on the real meshes (`measure().fit`: every vertex of every visible mesh except the floor, instances included, projected on the camera axes): closed at the default view 16.26 x 11.68 units, centre giving the pivot y 1.265, horizontal radius 8.32; open 17.23 x 14.04, pivot y 2.763, radius 9.17. Set: `MACHINE_H` 11.68 (plan 11.84), `ORBIT.targetY` 1.265 (plan 1.16), `SECTION_FRAME.radius` 8.33 / 9.18 (plan 8.35 / 9.23), `EXPLODE.fitHalfH` 8.17 (plan 8.49), `EXPLODE.targetY` 2.763 (plan about 2.7). At 1440 x 900: 66.27 px per unit, machine 1072 x 766 px (74.5 % of the width, 85 % of the height); TRACKS open: machine from x 13.5 to 918.6 px, panel at 948. Light-space extents of the casters: closed |x| 7.76, |y| 7.97, open |x| 8.81, |y| 9.97, depth 7.8 to 21.9 (extent 11.5, near 1, far 40).

R2-14. Budgets (renderer.info after a forced render, `__v4.stats.maxDrawCalls` on the frame with the shadow pass). Desktop 1440 x 900: closed 14 calls (10 main + 4 shadow: chassis, panel, pads, encoders), 23,030 triangles (12,518 main: pads 7,920, keys 1,944, encoders 1,608, chassis 688, panel 296, LEDs 32, halos 24, silk, OLED and floor 2 each; shadow 10,512); an orbit-only frame 10 calls; open 16 calls. Mobile 390 x 844 (DPR 1.5, mobile tier): closed 12 calls (10 + 2: chassis, pads), 13,510 triangles; open 14 calls, 15,198 triangles. Budgets 20 / 17 calls and 60k / 35k triangles. Programs 14, memory 12 geometries and 8 textures while mounted, `__v4LastDispose` 0 / 0 after / (SPA), a fresh Stage with 39 hotspots and 36 twins on return. Build (`vite build` into the scratchpad, exit 0): /v4 207.2 kB gzip with three (207,179 bytes by gzip -c: lazy chunk 149,672 raw / 52,692 gzip, three 144,059, CSS 3,718, AudioPlayerContext 1,332, contacts 3,534, mixtapes 1,844), +2.6 kB on R1, cap 230. The dash, emoji and ASCII greps on src/v4, docs/v4 and src/App.tsx print nothing.

R2-15. Verification (?debug=1&mute=1, master gain 0 throughout, the hidden-pane shim of 20.15, synthetic pointer events on .v4-hit after one real click for the audio context; pattern and tempo restored to the defaults after the tests).
- T1 desktop (mouse): a still tap on pad-BD (2 px, 150 ms) fired nothing during the press and one BD at the release (peak 0.60, first sample above 0.01 at 10.5 ms, data-v4-instrument BD); a 30 px drag from it fired nothing (peak 0) and turned the azimuth 45 to 39.7; a still 450 ms press fired nothing. Drags of 32 px from step-5, pad-tracks, pad-open, RUN and CLEAR changed nothing and orbited. Taps on all 12 pads (4 voices each +1 trigger; the 7 pages open then close their section with the pad yellowHi; OPEN opens and closes, silk CLOSE and OPEN) and on the 16 trig keys (16 of 16 toggled and restored). Encoders: 30 px up from TEMPO gave 130 to 145 BPM with the azimuth unchanged, from SWING and REVERB 0 to 0.2; 30 px sideways from each turned the azimuth to 39.7 with the value unchanged.
- T1 phone (390 x 844, touch): taps at the centres of BD, SD, TOM, CH, RUN and CLEAR each fired their own control; an 80 px touch drag from BD fired nothing and turned the azimuth 45 to 14.7.
- Phone touch targets at the default view, computed from the projection (hit.rects, the twins): BD, SD, TOM, CH, RUN and CLEAR are 48 x 48 px targets. Their raw projected silhouettes are 30.3 x 24.3 px (pads) and 19.5 x 14.6 px (RUN, CLEAR); neighbouring centres are 20.4 to 20.6 px apart for the pads (26.9 across rows) and 18.0 px for RUN and CLEAR, so the 48 px targets overlap and the nearest centre decides. Region resolving to each, sampled on the 1 px grid with the touch rule: BD 52 x 48 px (1,572 px2), SD, TOM and CH 41 x 47 (926 px2), RUN 47 x 59 (1,723 px2), CLEAR 48 x 47 (1,430 px2); the fully resolved centred disc is 18 to 22 px. Exclusive 48 px areas for adjacent pads would need 48 px between centres, 2.3 times the 12-pad pitch of the brief's layout at zoom 1 on a 390 px phone, and zooming in pushes the pads out of the frame (no pan). The Dock keeps RUN and CLEAR at 48 px; its instrument row selects without playing (rev 1). Owner options: make the Dock's instrument buttons play, and the Dock PAGES row (R2-12) for navigation.
- T2: 88 views (every 5 deg at 38, 8 azimuths at 18 and at 78) rendered with the floor hidden and a magenta clear colour: inside the silhouette of the chassis and panel vertices (2 px margin, 36.5 M pixels) at most 5 background pixels per view, all on the anti-aliased extreme corners: no hole. The floor next to the base is darker than the ink from every tested side (R2-12). The back connectors read as metal and ink from behind (R2-12).
- Sequencer: RUN for 30 s with 60 orbit frames at the start, desktop and phone: 261 steps each, drift maxMs 5.7e-10, lateCount 0, dropped 0, max tick gap 28 ms; the voice pads flashed with their steps; the playhead LED is yellowHi.
- Idle: 0 frames and 0 rAFs over 2 s at rest; after an 80 px drag the last frame came 566 ms after the release, then 0 frames over 2 s. Reduced motion: no intro, OPEN in one frame, a pad press lights without moving, 0 frames at rest.
- Keyboard: 1 to 7 opened TRACKS to SONAA (7 again closed), the AZERTY physical Digit1 opened TRACKS, Escape closed, 8 opened and O closed the machine, A S D F gave 4 triggers, R returned to 45 / 38 / 1, Space ran and stopped, Escape deselected.
- Fallback (?nowebgl=1): the new StaticMachine (wedge, panel, OLED, 6 encoders, 12 pads with the pages in faint yellow, RUN, CLEAR, 16 keys and LEDs, silk; 113 paths, 44 texts) at 720 x 432 px, no canvas, no twin, eight sections expanded (LABEL, SONAA in French), links with noopener noreferrer.
- Console: no error or warning from /v4 between two markers over a load, a section, OPEN and CLOSE, an orbit and a reset (the earlier errors in the tab were hot reloads during the edit).

R2-16. Left for the next stages: the LIVE chip as a panel button and its LIVE section, the LABEL chip's Bandcamp link with its yellow hover and chevron and `rel="noopener noreferrer"` on the chips (the chips keep their revision 1 behaviour and links), the Dock PAGES row, the social icons, the swing, drive and reverb audio, the final StaticMachine review. The OLED text is 3 to 4 px tall on the phone (the LCD twin carries it for assistive technology).

### 20.17 Stage 3 of 5 (SWING, DIST, REVERB made real): as implemented

Scope, from the orchestrated stage brief (plan R6 for the audio, plus the encoder gesture): SWING, DIST and REVERB change the sound, discreetly, start neutral, persist with the pattern, turn by a drag on their 3D encoder (tap versus drag respected, a drag that starts on an encoder never orbits), turn from the keyboard through their slider twins, and show their value on the OLED while turning. Created: audio/fx.ts. Modified: audio/drums.ts (DIST and REVERB wired, `mix.swing`, `.drive`, `.reverb` read from the pattern store), audio/clock.ts (swing), audio/pattern.ts (the `fx` store, persisted), theme.ts (`SWING`, `swingRatio()`; `SWING_RANGE` removed; comments), actions.ts (readout), ui/Hotspots.tsx (encoder gate, axis lock, cursor, swing text), debug.ts (comment). src/App.tsx, package.json, public and dist untouched (the build check writes into the builder's scratchpad); git untouched. Nothing is decorative: both effects run on every tier.

FX-1. SWING (audio/clock.ts). The even steps 2, 4 ... 16 (index odd) are scheduled `v x stepDur / 3` late (theme `SWING.maxDelay` 1/3: up to the triplet shuffle, the stage brief's "about 33 % of a sixteenth"; the plan's 0.4 would reach 70 %). The offset is added to the scheduled time (`when`, never a timer) and to the ring's `expected`, so `drift()` is measured against the swung grid; it is read from the store at every scheduled step (a change is heard within the 100 ms horizon, like a tempo change). Ring entries gain `off` (seconds). The swung times stay in order (the offset is under a third of a step), so `entryAt()`, the playhead LED, the pad flashes and the Dock follow the swung time. Display: the sixteenth ratio 50 to 67 % (`swingRatio()`: OLED line 3 "SWING 58%", slider twin "58 % swing"). The default pattern has every hit on an odd step (1, 3, 5 ...): SWING is inaudible until an even step is programmed, as on an Elektron, where swing acts on the even trigs (owner question 1 below).

FX-2. DIST (audio/fx.ts). A parallel drive right after the voice bus, BEFORE TONE and LEVEL (the plan put it after LEVEL: there, turning LEVEL down also cleaned the drive; here LEVEL stays a volume and TONE softens the harmonics the drive adds): dry `1 - m` plus wet `m x tanh(D x)` with `D = 1 + 5 d` and `m = d / 2`. The WaveShaper curve `tanh(8 u)` is computed once on 2049 points (odd count: an exact 0 at the centre) and its input is pre-scaled by `D / 8`, so it never hard-clips (the plan's `tanh(x)` on [-1, 1] after a 6x pre-gain would have clipped flat at 0.76); oversample 'none' (the '2x' of the plan delays the wet branch by 1 to 2 ms through its resampling filters, and the dry plus wet sum would comb-filter; the harmonics of a 50 to 150 Hz kick stay far from Nyquist). At 0: dry gain 1, wet gain 0 and the wet branch disconnected from the bus 60 ms after its 20 ms ramp, i.e. the revision 1 signal exactly (same peak and RMS read at the analyser before and after).

FX-3. REVERB (audio/fx.ts). A send after LEVEL (post-fader; the plan took it post-drive) into a ConvolverNode (`normalize` false) whose output goes straight into the analyser input, before master (`?mute=1` holds, the tail is measurable). Impulse: 1.2 s stereo (stage brief; plan 1.4), 10 ms pre-delay, 3 ms fade-in, two independent seeded noise channels (mulberry32 808 and 909: decorrelated stereo, the same impulse on every visit), a one-pole lowpass falling from 9 kHz to 2.5 kHz along the tail (the highs die first), an exponential envelope reaching -60 dB at the end, each channel scaled to unit energy. Generated once, at the first REVERB above 0 (or at the first gesture when a stored value is above 0). Send gain `0.3 r` (low wet: at most about -10.5 dB of reverb energy per channel under the dry signal). The send is connected only while r > 0 (disconnected 60 ms after its ramp to 0; the tail already in the convolver dies out by itself). Cost on the dev Mac: generation 2.1 ms warm, 7.3 ms on the very first call (JIT), convolver load 3 to 4 ms; 7.4 to 12.5 ms in all, once per page.

FX-4. Persistence (stage brief; replaces R2-17's "not persisted"). `pattern.fx` (audio/pattern.ts) = { get, set(patch), subscribe }, with its own listeners so a turn re-renders neither the pattern consumers (Dock, root, LEDs) nor the LCD composer; saved with the pattern by the same 300 ms debounce and the pagehide flush: `{"v":1,"bpm":130,"steps":{...},"fx":{"swing":0,"drive":0,"reverb":0}}`. Values are written to the thousandth, validated in 0..1 (anything else, a missing fx, a revision 1 value or a v other than 1 gives 0), and `v` stays 1 (revision 1 ignores the extra field). TONE and LEVEL stay unpersisted (section 10). drums.ts subscribes once: DIST and REVERB follow the store on the graph when it exists (20 ms ramps), then `mix` notifies the encoders and the twins; the clock reads SWING from the store.

FX-5. Encoder gesture (stage brief "not starting an orbit when the gesture starts on an encoder"; replaces R2-2 and the last clause of R2-A1). A drag that starts on an encoder never orbits: at the 6 px gate the hit layer keeps it until the release, and the dominant axis at that moment becomes its axis: vertical (up = more) or horizontal (right = more), measured from the pointerdown point (no jump), TEMPO 2 px per BPM, the five others 150 px for the range; the mouse cursor is ns-resize over an encoder and ns-resize or ew-resize while turning. Unchanged: a tap does nothing, two taps within 350 ms reset the start value, a still press over 400 ms does nothing, the wheel over an encoder turns it (the view does not zoom), a second finger does not pinch with a turning finger. Trade-off on the phone: with the touch rule at the default view, 12.2 % of the machine's projected area resolves to an encoder (the six 48 px targets overlap, 15 to 18 px apart), and a one-finger drag that starts there turns it instead of orbiting (R2-2 let the horizontal ones orbit); 54 % of the machine has no hotspot and the remaining 34 % are hotspots that orbit on a drag. (Changed by 20.20 RV-4 for touch pointers: the drag turns the encoder only after the finger rested 250 ms on it, any quicker drag orbits; mouse and pen keep this rule.)

FX-6. Debug (20.11): `__v4.audio.fx` { drive, reverb, driveOn, reverbOn, irSeconds, irMs, unlinks } (null before the first gesture), `__v4.audio.setSwing()`, `.setDrive()`, `.setReverb()`, `__v4.pattern.fx`, `__v4.state.pattern.fx`, `__v4.clock.scheduled[i].off`.

FX-7. Verification (?debug=1&mute=1, master gain 0 throughout, the hidden-pane shim of 20.15, one real click for the audio context, synthetic pointer and key events; measurements through a second AnalyserNode (fftSize 32768, about 0.68 s) tapped on `__v4.audio.analyser` and removed with the page; pattern, tempo and effects restored to the defaults afterwards).
- DIST, one BD hit at the analyser (LEVEL 0.8, TONE open), d = 0 / 0.5 / 1: peak 0.600 / 0.617 / 0.633 (+0.46 dB at 1), RMS over the first 200 ms 0.349 / 0.396 / 0.472 (+2.6 dB), crest factor 4.71 / 3.84 / 2.54 dB, RMS frequency (sqrt of the mean squared frequency, from the first difference) 97 / 101 / 118 Hz, RMS 290 to 310 ms after the onset 0.0139 / 0.0225 / 0.0484. At d = 1: SD peak 0.545 to 0.656, RMS 0.070 to 0.135, crest 17.8 to 13.7 dB; TOM RMS 0.180 to 0.296, crest 11.7 to 7.2 dB; CH peak 0.693 to 0.855, RMS 0.040 to 0.066. So the drive raises the peak a little (0.5 to 1.8 dB), the RMS clearly (2.6 to 5.7 dB) and the harmonic content, and lowers the crest FACTOR (2.2 to 4.6 dB), the signature of saturation; the stage brief's "DIST raises the crest" holds for the peak level, not for the peak-to-RMS ratio. Back at 0: peak 0.6002 and RMS 0.34908, the dry values to the last digit.
- REVERB, one BD hit, r = 0 / 0.35 / 1: RMS 300 to 350 ms after the onset 0.0095 / 0.0144 / 0.0269 (+3.6 dB, +9.0 dB), 290 to 310 ms 0.0139 / 0.0157 / 0.0220, 490 to 510 ms (the kick is over) 0 / 0.0035 (-49.1 dB) / 0.0101 (-40.0 dB); peak 0.600 / 0.626 / 0.686. Back at 0: the dry values exactly, the send disconnected (`reverbOn` false).
- SWING at max, CH on all 16 steps, 130 BPM: scheduled gaps alternate 153.846 and 76.923 ms (stepDur x 4/3 and x 2/3), `off` 38.462 ms on the even steps and 0 on the odd ones; onsets read back from the analyser 76.94, 153.83, 76.94 ms apart (one sample is 0.02 ms). Desktop 1440 x 900, 30 s with SWING 1, DIST 0.5 and REVERB 0.5 while the orbit stepped every 16 ms (1745 rendered frames): 260 steps, drift maxMs 3.1e-10, lateCount 0, dropped 0, max tick gap 27 ms, min lead 73.6 ms, maxDrawCalls 14.
- Mobile tier, 390 x 844 (touch emulation, DPR 1.5), 120 s with the same settings and an orbit step every 16 ms (6972 rendered frames): 1040 steps, drift maxMs 2.3e-9, lateCount 0, dropped 0, max tick gap 27 ms, maxDrawCalls 12, maxTriangles 13,510, geometries 10 and textures 7 constant, JS heap sampled every 10 s between 107.7 and 112.6 MB with no growth (first 110.1, last 108.5). REVERB therefore stays live on the mobile tier. Offline render of 20 s of a dense pattern (OfflineAudioContext, buildFx from the module, dev Mac): dry 0.80 % of real time, DIST 1 0.80 %, REVERB 1 1.45 %, both 1.48 %.
- Encoders, desktop (mouse): from SWING, 30 px up gave 0.2 ("SWING 53%" on OLED line 3), then 45 px to the right from the same point gave 0.5 ("SWING 58%"), the azimuth staying 45; the same for DIST and REVERB (0.2, then 0.5; "DIST 50%", "REVERB 50%"). A tap left DIST at 0.5, a double tap set 0 ("DIST 0%"), a still 450 ms press did nothing, one wheel notch up over DIST gave +0.02 with the zoom unchanged, drags of 40 px from the floor and from pad-BD turned the azimuth 45 to 37.7 with no trigger. Cursor ns-resize on hover, ew-resize during a horizontal turn, ns-resize during a vertical one. TONE and LEVEL unchanged (CH peak 0.035 at TONE 0.47 against 0.679 open; SD 0.106 at LEVEL 0.3 against 0.59 at 0.8).
- Encoders, phone (touch): the same values from SWING, DIST and REVERB with the azimuth unchanged; a 5 px, 100 ms press on SWING is a tap (no change, no orbit); an 80 px drag from the trig keys (disabled there) turned the azimuth 45 to 14.7; a tap on pad-BD fired at the release (first sample above 0.01 9.1 ms after the pointerup, peak 0.60).
- Keyboard (slider twins): Home 0 ("50 % swing"), three arrows 0.06 ("51 % swing"), Shift + Up 0.16, End 1 ("67 % swing", aria-valuenow 100, OLED "SWING 67%"), Down 0.98; DIST and REVERB the same in percent ("100 %", OLED "DIST 100%", "REVERB 100%"); the 3D encoders follow (-129.6 deg at 0.98).
- Persistence: SWING 0.4, DIST 0.3, REVERB 0.25 stored as `"fx":{"swing":0.4,"drive":0.3,"reverb":0.25}`; after a reload the store, the encoder angles (27, 54, 67.5 deg) and the twins ("57 % swing", "30 %", "25 %") came back before any gesture, and the first gesture built the graph with both branches connected (impulse 7.4 ms). `validateFx` gave neutral values for a revision 1 object, v 2, a string, an array, out-of-range, NaN and string values, and kept the valid ones.
- Both at once (desktop, after a reload): DIST 1 and REVERB 1 gave a BD peak of 0.769, RMS 0.478, 0.0547 at 300 to 350 ms and 0.0127 at 500 ms; back to 0, the dry values again (0.6002, 0.34908, 0.00952, 0).
- Idle: 0 frames and 0 rAFs over 2 s at rest after the two-minute run (phone) and after the tests on desktop (14 calls, 23,030 triangles on the last frame, as stage 2). Console: no error or warning from /v4 after a reload (the only warning is the main site's @sanity/image-url deprecation).

FX-8. Build and budgets: `vite build` into the scratchpad, exit 0: /v4 208.5 kB gzip with three (208,494 bytes by gzip -c: lazy chunk 153,042 raw / 54,009 gzip, three 144,059, CSS 3,718, AudioPlayerContext 1,330, contacts 3,534, mixtapes 1,844), +1.3 kB on stage 2, cap 230. Draw calls and triangles unchanged (desktop 14, mobile 12 closed; the audio adds no mesh). The dash, emoji and ASCII greps on src/v4, docs/v4 and src/App.tsx print nothing.

FX-9. Owner questions and limits. (1) The default pattern never plays an even step, so SWING is silent on it: add an even-step hit to the default (a CH ghost on step 16, say) or keep the Elektron behaviour. (2) The drive is heard mostly as density and sustain (RMS +2.6 to +5.7 dB, longer kick tail) rather than grit, because the kick is already saturated in its own voice; a stronger drive is one constant (`DRIVE.gain` or `DRIVE.mix` in audio/fx.ts). (3) The reverb's first use costs 7 to 13 ms on the main thread on the dev Mac, perhaps 3 to 4 times that on a phone: one dropped frame, once. (4) Not tested on a real iOS or Android device (390 x 844 is the Mac's touch emulation); no sound was ever audible, so the balance of the three effects was set by measurement, not by ear.

### 20.18 Stage 4 of 5 (icons, LABEL, LIVE, SONAA): as implemented

Scope, from the orchestrated stage brief (brief items 3 to 6, plan 20.4 and 20.5, and a shared component for the links that leave the site). Created: ui/icons.tsx, data/socials.ts, ui/ExternalLink.tsx, ui/sections/Live.tsx. Modified: theme.ts (`SectionId` gains 'live', `SECTION_TITLES.live`, `LABEL_URL`, `EXTERNAL_MARK`, `CHIP.extGapPx` and `extStroke`, `CHIPS` with `href` and `section` instead of `href` and `external`), data.ts (`SOCIALS` re-exported from data/socials.ts, the v2 socials import removed, `LABEL_LINK` from `LABEL_URL`, `LIVE_DOCS`, `LIVE_PAGES`; `EXTERNAL_REL` moved to ui/ExternalLink.tsx), actions.ts (`chipAction`), scene/pcb.ts (lit LABEL chip, label zone, chevron place in the router, `section` on the chip hotspots), scene/renderer.ts (chip anchors from the hotspot defs, hover and keyboard focus states, `setChipFocus()`), ui/Hotspots.tsx (chip twins), ui/Panel.tsx and fallback/NoWebGL.tsx (the LIVE section), ui/sections/common.tsx (`DownloadMark`, `DocLink`, Bandcamp rows through ExternalLink; `ExternalMark` moved to ui/ExternalLink.tsx), Contact.tsx, Label.tsx, Sonaa.tsx, Press.tsx, Shows.tsx, Studio.tsx, debug.ts, v4.css, comments in state/section.ts, scene/hit.ts and ui/Trace.tsx. Deleted: ui/SocialIcon.tsx (the initial-letter discs and the v2 `socialIcons` import). src/App.tsx, src/v2 (socials.ts included), package.json, public and dist untouched (the build check writes into the builder's scratchpad); git untouched.

C-1. Icons (ui/icons.tsx). One exported component per brand (`FacebookIcon` to `LinktreeIcon`, in the brief's order) and `SOCIAL_ICONS: Record<SocialId, React.FC>`; each renders `<svg class="v4-icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" width="24" height="24" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">` with ONE `<path fill="currentColor">`. The 14 `d` strings were written into the file by a script from the scratchpad's `simple-icons/paths.json` (Simple Icons 16.33.0, CC0) and checked back byte for byte: 14 of 14 identical. gigmit: the plan's tile path, unchanged, `fill-rule="evenodd"`: a 20 x 20 rounded square with a bold G knocked out; it reads as a tile, not as a letter or a question mark. Optical weight: a per-icon scale written as a matrix on the path (`OPTICAL`), set from the ink coverage of each glyph in its 24 box (Apple Music 81.9 %, Spotify 64.2, YouTube 62.4, Facebook 58.2, Deezer 56.1, Songkick 55.8, gigmit 52.3, Instagram 42.0, Bandcamp 38.8, Linktree 36.5, TikTok 35.6, Beatport 28.9, SoundCloud 24.8, X 24.6, Mixcloud 10.2) and checked by eye at 2x and 3.4x: Apple Music 0.84 (its tile then matches the 20 px gigmit tile), Facebook, Spotify, Songkick, YouTube and Instagram 0.9, Deezer and X 0.92, Linktree 0.94, the others 1 (the wide, low glyphs cannot grow past their box).

C-2. Mixcloud and Songkick. The Simple Icons Mixcloud glyph is the brand's current mark (an M, a bar and a double chevron, 24 x 6.8 in the box); Font Awesome, used by the v2 icon the brief calls "written M-X", draws the same mark, so it still reads close to "M-X" (owner question 1 below). Songkick's glyph is the brand's "sk" knocked out of a filled rounded shape, not a letter in a thin circle.

C-3. Social grid (Contact.tsx, v4.css). `ExternalLink` with `mark={false}` around each icon: `class="v4-social"`, target _blank, rel "noopener noreferrer", `aria-label` = the service name, one svg, one path, no text node. `.v4-socials`: `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(44px, 1fr))`, gap 0, max-width 392 px on desktop and 260 px on the phone, left margin -12 px (the first glyph lines up with the text column), links at least 44 px wide and 48 px tall, icon 24 px, bone, yellow on hover and focus-visible, `transition: color 160ms ease` (none under reduced motion; links and Bandcamp rows get the same 160 ms colour transition). Measured: 1440 x 900, 8 columns of 49 px, rows 8 + 7; 1024 x 768, 8 columns of 48 px, 8 + 7 (the desktop panel content is 372 or 412 px wide, always 8 columns); 390 x 844, 5 columns of 52 px, 5 + 5 + 5; the fallback page at 390 px, 5 + 5 + 5. A real mouse hover on Facebook gave rgb(242, 194, 48); keyboard focus on Bandcamp gave the same colour and the 2 px yellow outline; the computed transition is `color 0.16s ease`. The 15 anchors in order, each checked for label, href, target _blank, rel "noopener noreferrer", one svg with viewBox 0 0 24 24 and one path with fill currentColor, no text: Facebook https://www.facebook.com/MauditeMachine, Instagram https://www.instagram.com/mauditemachine/, Spotify https://open.spotify.com/artist/2FHPGWPEBQbCsgkLP9uuI4, Deezer https://www.deezer.com/fr/artist/8651600, SoundCloud https://soundcloud.com/mauditemachine, Bandcamp https://mauditemachine.bandcamp.com, YouTube https://www.youtube.com/@mauditemachine-official, Mixcloud https://www.mixcloud.com/mauditemachine/, TikTok https://www.tiktok.com/@mauditemachine, X https://x.com/mauditemachine, Apple Music https://music.apple.com/us/artist/maudite-machine/1028417516, Beatport https://www.beatport.com/artist/maudite-machine/500537, Songkick https://www.songkick.com/artists/10363218-maudite-machine, gigmit https://www.gigmit.com/maudite-machine, Linktree https://linktr.ee/mauditemachine. No "hypeddit" anywhere in the page.

C-4. ExternalLink (ui/ExternalLink.tsx). `ExternalLink` (children, then the chevron; `mark={false}` for icon links), `ExternalMark` (the 12 px north-east arrow, path `EXTERNAL_MARK` in theme.ts, shared with the PCB) and `EXTERNAL_REL`. Every new-tab link of /v4 goes through it: socials, LABEL, SONAA, LIVE, PRESS, SHOWS rows and "All shows", STUDIO "Tech rider", the Bandcamp rows of TRACKS and MIXTAPES; the LABEL chip twin (a transparent anchor) uses `EXTERNAL_REL` directly. Counted on the page: 26 anchors with target _blank, all with rel "noopener noreferrer", all with the chevron except the 15 icons; the only same-tab anchors are the two PDF downloads and the two mailto links. Deviation (brief item 5's reason, "the visitor leaves /v4"): STUDIO "Tech rider" (/techrider) and SHOWS "All shows" (/shows) navigated the same tab in revision 1; they now open a new tab with the chevron, like the LIVE panel's /techrider (owner question 2).

C-5. LABEL chip (scene/pcb.ts, scene/renderer.ts, ui/Hotspots.tsx). Twin: `<a href="https://vrstlrecords.bandcamp.com" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="VRSTL Records on Bandcamp">`; a canvas tap activates it (the pointerup's user activation), Enter and Space too; `chipAction` falls back to `window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener,noreferrer')` only when no twin is mounted. Hover, and keyboard focus-visible on its twin (`stage.setChipFocus()`): the chip rises (as every chip), the top face of its body turns yellow (its four vertex colours re-uploaded as one range; per-channel gain 2, 1.3, 0.3 on #F2C230, read back 241, 195, 73 at the default open view, the pads' flash reads 241, 193, 74), and its PCB silk turns yellow with the outgoing chevron after it (the `EXTERNAL_MARK` path, as tall as the capitals, 7 px after the text, stroke 2 in the 12 box to match the 700 weight). Only the label zone is redrawn (150 x 30 px on the 1024 texture, 77 x 15 on the 512 one): after every full draw of the board the zone is copied into a small canvas (`drawImage` at scale 1, no `getImageData` readback, so Chrome's willReadFrequently warning never fires); lit, it is filled with the board colour and redrawn in yellow; unlit, the copy is drawn back (0 bytes differ from the copy after 8 lit and unlit redraws); each change re-uploads the texture once. The router keeps the chevron's place once for all (the LABEL text rect is widened by 7 + 21 px on its right for routing only): the seeded walk now draws 24 traces of 161 segments instead of 169, and no copper comes near the chevron. LIVE and STUDIO (buttons) only rise. Verified on desktop: a real mouse hover lit the chip (cursor pointer), leaving it restored the zone, 0 frames and 0 rAFs over the next 2 s; Tab from the REVERB slider twin onto the LABEL twin (focus-visible) lit it, Tab to LIVE unlit LABEL and raised LIVE; a mouse tap and a touch tap on the chip, and Space on its twin, activated the anchor (https://vrstlrecords.bandcamp.com, _blank, "noopener noreferrer"; cancelled by a capturing listener, no tab opened, no `window.open`). The mobile texture shows the same lit label at half size.

C-6. LIVE chip and panel (ui/sections/Live.tsx). The twin is a `<button aria-label="Live setup and documents" aria-expanded aria-controls="v4-section-live">`; a tap, Enter or Space toggles the LIVE section (`section.toggle('live')`, like a page pad); it no longer navigates to /techrider. The panel is the shared Panel (same background, blur, radius, type, close button as CONTACT), `lang="fr"`, the yellow trace from the chip's top on desktop (anchor chip-live, visible at the default open view), the sliding sheet on the phone (focus on its close button). Content, compared as text with the escapes decoded: h2 "LIVE"; h3 "Setup"; "DJ set sur CDJ, ou live hybride Ableton Live, Push 3, Dreadbox Typhon, APC40."; "Dur\u00e9e : 90 minutes \u00e0 4 heures en DJ, 60 \u00e0 75 minutes en live." (U+00E9, U+00E0, U+00E0; plain spaces around the colon, as given); h3 "Documents"; then four rows: "Tech rider (PDF)" to /Tech_Rider_Maudite_Machine_2026-27.pdf with `download`, the arrow on the left and "0.9 MB" on the right (878,617 bytes); "Press kit (PDF)" to /Presskit_Maudite_Machine_2026-27.pdf with `download`, arrow, "4.5 MB" (4,460,360 bytes); "Photos et logos" to /press/ and "Fiche technique compl\u00e8te" to /techrider, new tab, "noopener noreferrer", chevron. The downloads have no target; a space between the label and the size keeps the accessible name "Tech rider (PDF) 0.9 MB" (flex ignores it). The OLED line 1 reads LIVE; `data-v4-section="live"`. Closing the machine with LIVE open leaves the panel where it is (same rect) and fades the trace (`data-visible="0"`); Escape closes the panel.

(checked as text), link "sonaa.ca" to https://sonaa.ca, new tab, "noopener noreferrer", chevron; the trace from the SONAA pad. Text in the DOM at load: the nine sections (tracks, mixtapes, press, shows, contact, label, sonaa, live, studio) are rendered at load in the panel, visually hidden while inactive, so the LIVE and SONAA French text is indexable; the fallback page (?nowebgl=1) renders the nine expanded, LIVE and SONAA with `lang="fr"`, the socials 5 + 5 + 5 at 390 px.

Desktop 1440 x 900: closed 14 calls and 23,030 triangles on a frame with the shadow pass, open 16 and 24,926; phone 390 x 844: closed 12 and 13,510, open 14 and 15,198 (unchanged: no mesh added); 14 programs; 12 geometries and 8 textures. `vite build` into the scratchpad, exit 0: /v4 213.2 kB gzip with three (213,217 bytes by gzip -c: lazy chunk 170,368 raw / 61,984 gzip, three 144,058, CSS 3,791, AudioPlayerContext 1,331, contacts 209, mixtapes 1,844), +4.7 kB on stage 3, cap 230. C-10. Owner questions. (1) Mixcloud: the official mark from Simple Icons reads close to "M-X" at 24 px; keep it (the real brand icon) or accept another mark (not in the downloaded set, it would have to be drawn). (2) STUDIO "Tech rider" and SHOWS "All shows" now open a new tab like the LIVE links; revert either with one prop if the same-tab behaviour was wanted. (3) Not in this stage: the Dock PAGES row (R2-12) and the final StaticMachine review.

### 20.19 Stage 5 of 5 (polish, budgets, the client's tests): as implemented, and revision 2 as shipped

Scope, from the orchestrated stage brief: run the client's tests T1 to T7 muted and fix what fails, measure the budgets, the accessibility pass (twins that follow the orbit, the Tab order voices, nav pads, encoders, RUN/CLEAR, trig keys, RESET VIEW, a visible focus ring, reduced motion, the ?nowebgl=1 fallback with LABEL, LIVE, SONAA and the 15 icons), this section, the greps; plus what the earlier stages left for it (the Dock PAGES row R2-12, the final StaticMachine review). Modified: ui/Hotspots.tsx (twin order), scene/renderer.ts (hotspot list order), scene/hit.ts (a comment), ui/Dock.tsx (the PAGES row; the Dock now gets the Stage so a page or OPEN tapped there also sinks its 3D pad), index.tsx (the Dock's prop), ui/Panel.tsx (two tabIndex fixes, `keepInRow` on the sheet tabs), ui/sections/common.tsx (`keepInRow`), v4.css (the PAGES row, short screens), this file. Reviewed and left as they are: fallback/StaticMachine.tsx, fallback/NoWebGL.tsx. src/App.tsx, package.json, public and dist untouched; git untouched. `npm run build` itself is not run by the builders: its prebuild rewrites public/sitemap.xml and the build writes dist/, both read-only here; the size comes from the same `vite build` into the scratchpad (P-5).

P-1. Tab order (stage brief; replaces the order of 20.7 and of 20.16 R2-11). The twins, in DOM and Tab order: the 4 voice pads (BD, SD, TOM, CH), the 8 navigation pads in reading order (TRACKS, MIXTAPES, PRESS, SHOWS, CONTACT, LABEL, SONAA, OPEN), the 3 PCB chips while they exist (from "opening" to the end of "closing"), the 6 encoder sliders (TEMPO to REVERB), RUN, CLEAR, the 16 trig keys; RESET VIEW follows (index.tsx renders it right after .v4-stage) whenever the view has moved. The chips sit right after OPEN, which reveals them (the disclosure pattern: after opening the machine with the keyboard, one Tab reaches LABEL; closing it returns a focus left on a chip to OPEN, as before); the brief's list does not name them, and in the closed state the order is exactly the brief's. The hotspot list (`hit.ids()`, the rects buffer, `__v4.hotspots`) follows the same order; picking does not depend on it (ties go to the strictly nearest centre): after the reorder every enabled hotspot still picks itself at its top centre, 36 of 36 closed at 8 views (0, 45, 90, 180, 270 deg at 38, 45 deg at 18 and 78, 200 deg at 20) and 39 of 39 open at 2 views. Checked with real Tab presses from the page start. 1440 x 900, view moved: pad-BD, pad-SD, pad-TOM, pad-CH, pad-tracks ... pad-sonaa, pad-open, enc-tempo ... enc-reverb, run, clear, step-1 ... step-16, RESET VIEW, then back to pad-BD (38 presses, each one `:focus-visible`, outline 2px solid rgb(242, 194, 48)); with LABEL open: step-16, the panel's close button, the Bandcamp link, pad-BD. 390 x 844: the same 36 twins and RESET VIEW, then the Dock (the 4 instruments, the 16 steps, RUN, CLEAR, OPEN, tempo down, tempo up, the 7 pages), then back to pad-BD.

P-2. Two focus fixes found by that run. (a) Chrome makes a scroll container keyboard-focusable when it holds no focusable element: with the sheet closed on the phone, the sheet's tab strip (its tabs are at tabIndex -1 then) took the focus after the Dock, off screen. The strip now carries tabIndex -1 (its tabs carry the focus when the sheet is open), and the panel body carries tabIndex -1 while the panel is closed (the last section stays shown during the fade out; a long list would make the invisible body a focusable scroller, on desktop too). Checked: after the Dock's last page the focus returns to pad-BD on the phone; on desktop, after TRACKS was opened and closed, step-16 is followed by pad-BD. (b) In the preview pane a Tab onto a button of a sideways-scrolling row did not scroll it into view (CONTACT at x 379 to 480 in a row clipped at 385: 6 px visible). `keepInRow()` brings the focused button fully into its row (8 px margin) on focus, for the Dock PAGES row and the sheet tabs (CONTACT then at 273 to 374); the page never scrolls.

P-3. Dock PAGES row (plan R2-12, "owner may veto"). The seven page names (TRACKS to SONAA) at the BOTTOM of the Dock, under the transport row, like a tab bar in the thumb zone (the plan did not place it; above the instruments it would have split the instrument row from the steps it programs): 48 px tall buttons, graphite, bone 60 %, 12 px 700, tracking 0.18 em (the instruments' style), 4 px gaps, the row scrolls sideways like the sheet tabs (643 px of buttons in the 380 px row at 390 x 844; the button cut at the edge says there are more; `touch-action: pan-x`, no scrollbar), `role="group" aria-label="Pages"`, each button named "Tracks" ... "Sonaa" with `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls="v4-section-<id>"`, the focus ring drawn inside the button (outline-offset -2 px, the row clips). A tap calls `page(id, stage)`: the section opens (the sheet slides up over the Dock and its tabs take over; the focus goes to its close button), the 3D pad sinks and turns yellowHi. Fit: at 390 x 844 the row sits at y 754 to 802 in the Dock (464 to 844). Under 700 px of height the Dock's gaps and padding go from 8 to 4 px (375 x 667: rows from 377 to 656 in 367 to 667); under 600 px the PAGES row is hidden (320 x 568: the four other rows from 327 to 554 in 312 to 568), the 3D pads and the sheet tabs still navigate. No row overflows the Dock at the three sizes. This stage's code costs 0.3 kB gzip in all (P-5).

P-4. The client's tests, run muted on the final code (?debug=1&mute=1, master gain 0 throughout, one real click for the audio context, synthetic pointer events on .v4-hit, the hidden-pane shim of 20.15; sound checked at the analyser only; pattern, tempo and effects restored to the defaults).
- T1 desktop (mouse, 1440 x 900). A 30 px drag in 214 ms from the same pad: 0 triggers, analyser peak 0.003 (the tail of the previous hit), azimuth 45 to 39.66. A still 452 ms press: nothing. From SWING, 30 px up gave 0.2 ("SWING 53%" on OLED line 3) and 45 px to the right 0.5 ("SWING 58%"), the azimuth staying 45 (FX-5); from TEMPO, 30 px up gave 130 to 145 BPM.

- T2. 55 views of the closed machine (every 10 deg at elevation 38, 8 azimuths at 18 and at 78, and 180/25, 45/60, 300/30), each rendered with the floor hidden and a magenta clear colour and read back: 0 magenta pixel inside the convex silhouettes of the chassis (feet and connectors left out) and of the panel, shrunk by 2 px (90.7 M device pixels tested; with the chassis hidden the same test counts 281,123 and 512,984 at two views, so it does see holes). The contact shadow reads 8,8,9 to 8,8,10 at 0.25 outside the base on every visible side, at 8 azimuths and elevations 38 and 18 (the ink is 10,10,11); the key light's cast shadow reads 8,8,9 on the floor 0.9 outside the back left, against 12,12,14 (the halo) 0.9 outside the front left, seen from 270/38 and from 200/30: the shadow is the world-fixed one from every side.
