P-9. Owner questions still open, all stages together. (1) SWING is silent on the default pattern, which never plays an even step (FX-9): add an even-step hit to the default or keep the Elektron behaviour. (2) Mixcloud's official mark reads close to "M-X" at 24 px (C-10). (3) STUDIO "Tech rider" and SHOWS "All shows" now open a new tab (C-10); one prop reverts each. (4) The Dock PAGES row (R2-12) is in; a veto removes one block of ui/Dock.tsx and its CSS. (5) On the phone at zoom 1 the 3D targets overlap (20.16 R2-15) and the Dock's instrument buttons select without playing: make them play? (6) The default elevation 38 makes the machine height-bound on desktop (74.5 % of the width at 1440 x 900; FIT_H 0.9 would give about 78 %), and views above about 55 deg crop at zoom 1 on landscape screens (R2-5; 20.14 items 4 and 5). (7) The drive is heard as density more than grit (FX-9). (8) Not tested on a real iOS or Android device (390 x 844 is the Mac's touch emulation): iOS page pinch against the canvas pinch (20.14 item 11), the first tap's audio, the reverb's first-use cost; no sound was ever audible, the effects' balance was set by measurement. (9) The fog is a radial falloff in the floor shader, not a scene FogExp2 (R1-4). Updated by the review (20.20 RV-23): (2) is answered by a drawn Mixcloud mark the owner can refuse; new questions on the tap rule, the touch grab of the encoders and the floor shadow strength.

P-10. Test limits of this stage. The preview pane stays hidden: the page gets no rendering update, so rAF is replaced by a 16 ms timer (the shim of 20.15), CSS transitions do not advance (fades checked by computed style), a Tab press does not scroll its target into view by itself (hence P-2 b, which is right in any case) and matchMedia change events do not arrive: a live switch between the desktop and phone layouts without a reload (a window resized across 768 px) was therefore not re-observed in this stage; every layout was loaded fresh. Canvas screenshots were used only as a visual check, the proofs are read-backs, projections and the DOM.

### 20.20 Review of revision 2 (29 findings): fixes, skips and measurements

Scope: the 29 findings of the orchestrated review of revision 2 as shipped (6 major, 23 minor). Every major is applied; every minor is applied except finding 26 (RV-21); findings 13 and 29 needed no change (their numbers are refreshed in RV-22) and findings 6, 15 and 19 are one fix (RV-6). Modified: scene/orbit.ts, ui/Hotspots.tsx, ui/ResetView.tsx, scene/renderer.ts, scene/pads.ts, scene/silk.ts, scene/encoders.ts, ui/Trace.tsx, audio/fx.ts, audio/drums.ts, audio/clock.ts, index.tsx, debug.ts, theme.ts, ui/icons.tsx, v4.css, this file. src/App.tsx, package.json, public and dist untouched; git untouched. Verification: ?debug=1&mute=1 only, master gain 0 throughout, the hidden-pane shim of 20.15 (rAF on a 16 ms timer, visibility forced), synthetic pointer events on .v4-hit unless a real click, drag or key press is named; sound read at the analyser only; pattern, tempo and effects restored to the defaults afterwards (stored `{"v":1,"bpm":130,...,"fx":{"swing":0,"drive":0,"reverb":0}}`).

RV-1 (finding 1, major). Slow wheel input was thrown away: the release stop (a remainder under 0.0125 of log-zoom is dropped) also ate fresh input, so a wheel frame under 8.3 px, or a Ctrl pinch frame under 1.4 px, did nothing. The orbit now marks wheel input as fresh (`wheelFresh`), and a fresh remainder under the stop limit is applied in full in the next frame instead of being dropped; the stop still ends a glide once no new input arrives. Measured (desktop, wheel events on the background every 16 ms): 60 events of 2 px gave zoom 0.835 (expected exp(-0.18) = 0.835; unchanged at 1 before), 30 Ctrl events of -1 px gave 1.310 (expected 1.310), one isolated 5 px event 1.008 (expected 1.0075); a 100 px notch still glides, zoom 1.148 (1.162 asked, the stop drops the end as before), 29 frames, settled 534 ms after the event.

RV-2 (findings 3, 4, 5, 7). Orbit input. (a) A primary pointerdown zeroes the remainders: the glide stops under a new finger or click, as native orbit controls do; a 150 px flick then a still press: azimuth 32.83 at the press and 32.83 after 300 ms held; a tap on pad-SD during a glide fired (the pad did not move under the pointer, 0 px). (b) A pinch that passes 0.55 or 2.4 re-anchors on the fingers (the goal is clamped, the anchor follows): out to 400 px (the limit is reached at 240 px), then back to 300 px, the zoom went straight toward 1.8 (1.842 after 400 ms of damped glide); in to 20 px, then back to 60 px, it rose toward 1.65; before, nothing moved until the spread came back to where it had hit the limit. (c) A reset at the default view starts no tween: key R there rendered 0 frames. A reset clears the remainders while it runs, so a mouse drag during it no longer jumps at its end: R pressed mid-drag, more moves during the tween, the view landed at azimuth 45 and stayed (45 after 300 ms and after the release); a pinch running when a tween ends is re-anchored. (d) A lost pointerup: a mouse move with no button pressed is a release in both layers, and `lostpointercapture` (it bubbles to .v4-stage) is routed to the orbit's release and to the hit layer's `forget()` (nothing fires, the encoder cursor resets): after a drag whose pointerup was lost, hover moves left the azimuth still, `dragging` false, the cursor back to default; a dispatched lostpointercapture mid-drag gave `dragging` false.

RV-3 (finding 21, major; a deviation from the brief's rule, owner decision). The brief's trap rule ("fire only if the pointer moved less than 6 px AND less than 400 ms elapsed") and its own test T1 ("press slowly on a pad without moving: the sound fires") conflict for a deliberate press of 400 to 800 ms: a still pointer can never be an orbit, so that press was a dead gesture (no sound, no rotation). `Orbit.isTap` now drops the duration: a tap is a single pointer that never passed 6 px, never orbited and was not kept by an encoder; `lastTap.quick` (under 400 ms) still gates the background double tap. A drag still never fires. Measured, desktop (mouse): taps of 150, 450 and 800 ms (SD) and a 2 s press with a 4 px wiggle (TOM) each fired once, analyser peaks 0.60, 0.60, 0.508, 0.693, the first sample above 0.01 at 9 to 11 ms after the pointerup; a 120 px drag from pad-BD: 0 triggers, peak 0, azimuth 45 to 26.4. Phone (touch, 390 x 844): a 120 ms tap on SD fired (0.443, 12.3 ms), a 600 ms press with 3 px of jitter on BD fired (0.601), an 80 px drag from BD: 0 triggers, peak 0, azimuth 45 to 14.67. Revert: add `&& ms < ORBIT.tapMs` back in `Orbit.isTap`.

RV-4 (finding 23, major). On the phone, orbit drags that started on an encoder turned TEMPO or TONE (12 % of the machine's area resolves to an encoder), and the BPM was saved for later visits. A touch pointer now grabs an encoder only after resting on it: at the 6 px gate, `orbit.gate` keeps the pointer for the encoder only if 250 ms have passed since its pointerdown (theme `ENC_GRAB.touchHoldMs`); a quicker drag orbits. Mouse and pen keep the immediate grab of FX-5. Measured at 390 x 844 (touch): a quick 80 px horizontal drag from TEMPO: 130 BPM unchanged, azimuth 45 to 14.6; a quick 60 px drag up: 130 BPM, elevation 38 to 18; a 300 ms rest then 30 px up: 130 to 145 BPM, the view unchanged; SWING, 300 ms rest then 45 px to the right: 0 to 0.3. 60 quick touch drags in random directions while the view kept moving, 21 of them started on an encoder's centre: 0 triggers, 130 BPM, SWING, DIST and REVERB 0, TONE 1, LEVEL 0.8. Trade-off: the grab has no visual cue on the phone (the OLED is 3 to 4 px tall there); the Dock keeps TEMPO minus and plus, the slider twins keep every encoder.

RV-5 (finding 2). The cursor stayed on 'grabbing' after a mouse orbit: the hover recompute ran in a microtask, which real events run after each listener, so before the orbit's pointerup on the parent. It now runs in a `setTimeout(0)` task, after the event. A real mouse drag in the preview (azimuth 45 to 20.5): cursor '' after the release.

RV-6 (findings 6, 15, 19). RESET VIEW kept the focus while it hid itself (aria-hidden on a focused element, then visibility hidden: the focus fell to body). A `view` listener runs inside `view.set(false)`, before React renders the hidden state: a keyboard focus (focus-visible) moves to the last twin, step-16, the element just before RESET VIEW in the Tab order; a focus left by a mouse click is blurred (Space stays RUN/STOP instead of activating a twin). Checked with a real Tab onto RESET VIEW and a real Enter: the view returned to 45 / 38 / 1, the focus on step-16 with focus-visible, the button aria-hidden with tabIndex -1; a real mouse click: the focus on body; no "Blocked aria-hidden" warning in the console.

RV-7 (finding 8, major). Phone, open machine: the pads cast shadows but the panel under them did not, so they threw floating squares on the floor behind the machine. On the mobile tier the panel now casts while it is lifted (`Stage.syncCasters()`, from the explode animator and `applyExplode`): closed it does not (the chassis' shadow covers it), so the closed budget is unchanged. Floor-only read-back along z -6.1 behind the open machine (view 200 / 60, the shadow map of the full scene kept): panel not casting, the old behaviour, "14 14 14 14 15 15 12 4 7 4 4 4 4 8 4 8 4 6 4 4 15 14 14 14 14" (the pads' islands); panel casting, one continuous band "4" over 21 samples, then "14". Draw calls on the phone: closed 12 (13,510 triangles, the panel not casting), open 15 (12 main + 3 shadow, 15,494 triangles).

RV-8 (finding 9). The shadow map was redrawn for colour-only changes (every sequencer step, flash end, LED, OLED, hover). `Stage.repaint()` now requests a frame without the shadow pass, and an animator returns 'paint' for a frame that changed only colours, textures or objects that cast no shadow; `invalidate()` and an animator's `true` keep the shadow pass for casters that move (pad sink, encoders on desktop, explode, intro). Repaint: pads' flash and glow (the sink runs through the caster tweens), the sequencer's playhead, LEDs and RUN colour, the OLED, hover, chip focus and lift, the section framing and the active page pad; the chip lift and the section framing moved to a second `Tweens` list whose frames are 'paint'. Measured (desktop): RUN for 3 s: 34 frames, 0 shadow updates, 10 calls on each step frame; a hover on a page pad: 2 frames, 0; a pad tap: 14 frames, 14 with the shadow pass (the pad sinks); one encoder step from the keyboard: 1 frame with it (desktop encoders cast); 30 s of RUN with the orbit stepping every 16 ms: 1,742 frames, 0 shadow updates, maxDrawCalls 10.

RV-9 (finding 10). The trace re-ran the anchor projection and its occlusion ray test from its own draw-animation rAF (frames the Stage does not render, possibly before the orbit's update) and on every rendered frame even when the view had not moved. The draw loop now only advances the stroke and the dot; `reroute()` runs in `stage.onView` and only when `hit.version()` changed (camera, layers, size), forced on mount and on a panel resize. Measured (desktop): TRACKS open, then RUN for 2 s: 27 rendered frames, 0 reroutes; an animated orbit to 80 deg: 29 reroutes, the path changed, the trace visible.

RV-10 (findings 11, 12). The brush roughnessMap is created without anisotropy (`makeBrushTexture()`, anisotropy 1, mipmaps kept): anisotropic filtering kept its rows sharp at grazing views where they must average out, and added up to 8 taps per panel fragment. The silk, OLED and PCB textures, which carry text, keep 8. Its comment claimed the canvas was freed after the upload; it is not, and must not be: three re-uploads a CanvasTexture from its canvas after a context restore. Comment corrected.

RV-11 (finding 14). On unmount the context is suspended, and a suspended ConvolverNode keeps its internal tail: it played again at the first gesture of the next visit. `FxChain.silence()` (called by `drums.quiet()` in the index.tsx cleanup, before `suspend()`) disconnects the send and the convolver and forgets them, keeps the impulse buffer, and resets the applied REVERB to 0; `ensure()` re-applies the stored value at the next gesture, which builds a fresh, empty convolver on the cached impulse (no new noise generation; `irMs` keeps its first figure). The master gain is never touched (?mute=1 holds). Measured: REVERB 1, a BD hit, the page left 550 ms later (reverb tail peak 0.020): after the unmount `fx.reverbOn` false, the context suspended; back on /v4 (SPA), at the first real click the analyser went from its frozen pre-suspend buffer straight to silence (2 distinct buffers over 1.2 s, nothing decaying), then `reverbOn` true again. Control on the same scene with the old path (suspend only): 35 distinct buffers after the resume, peaks 0.020 to 0.026 decaying over about 700 ms, the old tail. Voice tails already sounding at the unmount (0.47 s at most) still resume, as in revision 1.

RV-12 (finding 16). Text links turned yellow on hover only. `.v4-link`, `.v4-row-link` and `.v4-contact-mail` now also turn yellow on `:focus-visible`, like the icons; the mail links gain the 160 ms colour transition (none under reduced motion). With transitions switched off (the hidden pane does not run CSS transitions), keyboard focus gave rgb(242, 194, 48) on all 9 text links of LIVE, PRESS and CONTACT.

RV-13 (finding 17). The OPEN twin reads "Open the machine, key 8 or O" (`OPEN_ARIA`), like the page twins that announce their digit.

RV-14 (finding 18, major). The `__v4.state` getter is annotated `V4DebugState` (its `view` literal widened to string). Strict type check (TypeScript 5.6.3 from the local npx cache, the repository tsconfig, strict, noEmit) over src/v4 and what it imports, 57 v4 files: 0 errors (1 before).

RV-15 (finding 20). The dead `tempoAngle` export and the BPM import of scene/encoders.ts are removed; the header of scene/orbit.ts describes the gate as it is (a drag that starts on an encoder, in any direction; the touch rest of RV-4 in ui/Hotspots.tsx).

RV-16 (finding 22, major; a deviation, owner question). Mixcloud's current official mark (Simple Icons, and Font Awesome 7 draws the same) is an M, a bar and two chevrons: it reads "M-X", the client's complaint. ui/icons.tsx now draws the brand's classic mark instead, as one path with `fill-rule="evenodd"` in the 24 box: an outlined cloud, stroke 2.2, three lobes on a flat base at y 15.346 (radius 3.7 centred at (4.3, 11.646), 5.3 at (8.9, 10.046), 3.2 at (13.6, 12.146)), the inside cut by the exact inward offset (the lobes' concentric arcs and a 2.2 rounding in each valley), and two waves 2.2 wide with round ends, centred on the right lobe (centre-line radii 5.8 and 8.7, half-angles 0.76 and 0.66 rad); the whole mark is centred on (12, 12). Ink coverage 23.1 % (SoundCloud 24.8, X 24.6, the M-X glyph 10.2), optical scale 1. Checked as T6: 15 anchors in the brief's order, each one svg with one path, no text; rendered at 24 px beside SoundCloud it reads as a cloud with sound waves. Revert: the Simple Icons path is in paths.json of the icon download (key mixcloud).

RV-17 (finding 24). The cast shadow on the floor read 4 levels out of 255. `FLOOR.shadow` 0.5 to 0.8. Floor-only read-back at the default view (red channel, the full scene's shadow map kept): the shadow side (behind and left of the base, 0.6 to 1 unit out, and 9 units from the centre) read 8 to 9 before and 4 after, the lit side at the same distances 10 to 12 (contact shadow included): 6 to 8 levels of contrast instead of 2 to 4. The review's target of 10 needs an opacity of 1, which turns the band black (1 to 2 levels) next to the dark chassis: not applied, one constant if the owner wants it.

RV-18 (finding 27). The phone's social grid was capped at 260 px and left-aligned (100 px empty on the right). Under 768 px it is now 5 columns of 44 px spread over the sheet (`justify-content: space-between`, margin -10 px so the first glyph sits on the text and the last on the right edge): at 390 x 844, gaps 39.5 px, the first icon at x 16 (the text at 16), the last ending at 374 (the content edge), rows 5 + 5 + 5, links 44 x 48, scrollWidth 390; the fallback page at 390 px the same (16 to 370). Desktop unchanged (8 columns of 49 px, 8 + 7). The brief's `repeat(auto-fit, minmax(44px, 1fr))` would give 8 columns and two rows on a phone, against its own "three or four rows on mobile", so the phone keeps a fixed five.

RV-19 (finding 28). The clock computes each step from its anchor, `nextTime = anchor + n x stepDur`, instead of adding `stepDur`: drift maxMs is 0 (it grew about 2e-11 ms per step). 30 s of RUN with the orbit stepping every 16 ms (desktop): 260 steps, drift maxMs 0, lateCount 0, dropped 0, min lead 73.6 ms, max tick gap 28.4 ms.

RV-20 (finding 25). T4 is judged on solids, not on the layers' bounding boxes: the chassis is a tray (R2-2) and the PCB sleeps in it, so the PCB and chassis boxes intersect by design in the open state (PCB minimum y 2.040 against the rim top 2.169, inside the walls), while no PCB vertex is inside the chassis solid (0 of 3,312 by a parity ray cast), the panel's underside stays at least 0.0994 above the PCB while moving and 1.5311 above it open. A box test reports an overlap that is not an interpenetration.

RV-21 (skipped). Finding 26, a two-finger tap on two voice pads playing both: the finding itself marks it optional; with the tap on release rule the two hits would sound at the two releases, not together, and it would add a second tap path through the pinch state for a gesture the brief assigns to zoom. Left for the owner.

RV-22 (build, budgets, greps). `npm run build` (prebuild sitemap, `vite build --manifest`, postbuild prerender) run in an isolated APFS clone of the sources, public included, in the builder's scratchpad (so the prebuild's public/sitemap.xml and the dist writes stay out of the repository): exit 0. /v4 route 214,286 bytes gzip (214.3 kB) against the 230 kB cap: three 144,059, the lazy chunk 173,506 raw / 62,940 gzip, CSS 3,903, mixtapes 1,844, AudioPlayerContext 1,331, contacts 209 (+0.8 kB on the reviewed build). Draw calls with the shadow pass: desktop closed 14 (23,030 triangles), phone closed 12 (13,510), phone open 15 (was 14). Memory over the 30 s run: 10 geometries and 7 textures before and after, 14 programs; then 0 frames and 0 rAFs over 2 s at rest. Reduced motion (?motion=reduce): no intro, a 120 px drag stops dead at the release (azimuth 21 then and 300 ms later), RESET VIEW is one frame, 20 wheel events of 2 px gave zoom 0.942 (0.9418 asked), 0 frames at rest. Greps on src/v4 and docs/v4: em dash, en dash, non-ASCII and emoji print nothing; no `import * as`, OrbitControls or simple-icons. Console: no error or warning from /v4.

RV-23 (owner questions from this review). (1) The tap rule (RV-3): a still press now fires at any duration, against the brief's 400 ms clause and for its test T1; one condition reverts it. (2) The touch grab of the encoders (RV-4): a 250 ms rest; alternatives are a vertical-only turn on touch, or a shorter rest. (3) Mixcloud (RV-16): the drawn classic cloud, or the official "M-X" mark back. (4) The floor shadow at 0.8 (RV-17), or 1 for the review's contrast target. (5) Finding 26 (RV-21): two-finger taps on the pads.

## 21. Revision 4 (2026-09-30): the machine on `/`, BASS, GOODIES, MERCH, STUDIO, mobile grid

R4-1. Merch images. `public/store.json` paths are absolute (`/images/...`), `Merch_Hoodie F.webp` is renamed `Merch_Hoodie-Front.webp`. The file stays FLAT (one entry per view): the admin writes that format (server.js `/api/save-merch`, `src/components/Admin.tsx`) and /v1 and /v2 read it; `data.ts groupMerch()` builds the products (Sweatshirt, Hoodie, Hip Bag with 5 colours, T-shirt) with their views. Restructuring the file would break the admin, which this revision must not touch.

R4-2. Pads. 14 pads in 2 rows of 7 (size 0.86, pitch 1.0, x0 0.13): BD SD TOM CH BASS TRACKS MIXTAPES / PRESS SHOWS CONTACT LABEL LIVE SONAA OPEN. Keys A S D F G, pages 1 to 8, OPEN 9 and O. OPEN glows orange (`PAD_GLOW.orange`), dim orange once open (CLOSE). No pulse on the 3D pad: 0 frames at rest.

R4-3. PCB. Three chips: GOODIES, MERCH, STUDIO (x -3.4, 0, 3.4). LABEL and LIVE left the board: LABEL and LIVE are page pads and dock buttons.

R4-4. BASS. `audio/bass.ts`: sawtooth plus a square one octave down at 30 %, two cascaded lowpass filters (Q 6 on the first) swept 1200 to 180 Hz in 120 ms, attack 4 ms, decay 380 ms, C minor pentatonic from C2 (65.4 Hz), 30 ms glide only when the previous scheduled step played, monophonic (a new note closes the previous gate in 5 ms). Joins the common bus (TONE, LEVEL, DIST, REVERB, mute). Its row stores a digit per step (0 to 5). Tap: next degree, 5 back to empty; hold 400 ms (`STEP_HOLD_MS`), Delete or Backspace on a step twin: clear. Default line `1010103010105030` (I, III, V read as pentatonic degrees 1, 3, 5: C, F, Bb). Level: `BASS_SYNTH.out` 0.42. Measured at the analyser (master at 0): drums alone already peak at -1.0 dBFS over the pattern; with the bass at 0.42 the mix peak stays -1.1 dBFS, 0 clipped samples; 0.55 peaks at -0.6, 0.85 clips (40 samples in 3.7 s). Single hits: kick peak -4.4 dBFS, bass -5.5 (1.1 dB under); 50 ms momentary RMS kick -5.8, bass -16.0. The resonant attack gives the bass a high crest factor and the bus compressor holds it down: 4 dB under the kick in energy is not reachable without clipping. One constant to raise if the owner wants it louder.

R4-5. Mobile dock. No horizontal scroll: instruments (5), 16 steps, transport (RUN/STOP, CLEAR, tempo), then a 6 x 2 navigation grid (4 x 3 under 372 px): TRACKS MIXTAPES PRESS SHOWS CONTACT LABEL / LIVE SONAA RESET + OPEN on three cells. Open: GOODIES MERCH STUDIO replace LIVE SONAA RESET, OPEN becomes CLOSE. The brief asked 5 x 2 with OPEN on two cells: 9 entries plus 2 cells make 11, which a 5 x 2 grid cannot hold; 6 x 2 with OPEN on three cells fills 12 exactly. Font Awesome 6.5.1 icons (index.html), aria-hidden. Canvas height on phones 50dvh (was 55) so the dock fits 390 x 844 without scrolling.

R4-6. GOODIES reuses `src/data/goodies.ts` (now shared with /v1/goodies): 6 desktop wallpapers, 5 phone wallpapers, 21 covers, direct downloads with their weight. The sticker was removed from /goodies on purpose earlier (commit 84d6a66): not added back.

R4-7. Stickers. `public/stickers.json`, one example pack `"active": false`. A pack shows only if active, complete, and it holds an item with `"brand": "vrstl"` and one with `"brand": "massive"`. Absent file or no showable pack: no block at all. PNG files go in `public/images/stickers/`.

R4-8. Order by email, all products: `mailto:mauditemachine@gmail.com`, subject `Order - <product>`, body with Product, Size, Colour (the chosen ones or n/a), Quantity 1, Price, then the buyer's fields.

R4-9. /techrider. `scripts/prerender-seo.mjs` writes `dist/techrider/index.html` (the SPA shell with its own meta): GitHub Pages answers 200 on /techrider/ (301 from /techrider) instead of the 404 fallback. The sitemap lists `/techrider/`. /radar keeps the fallback (not touched in this revision).

## 22. 2026-10-01: OH, the arrival pattern, front view, brand colours, contact form

R5-1. The BASS voice of revision 4 is replaced by OH, the open hi-hat (pad OH, key G): noise through a 6.8 kHz high-pass and a +4 dB peak at 10 kHz, 340 ms. Any hi-hat (closed or open) chokes the ringing OH in 8 ms, like an 808. `audio/bass.ts`, the pitched steps, the LED bars and the dock bars are gone; a hold (400 ms) or Delete still clears a step. A stored pattern keeps its rows; its old BASS row is ignored and OH gets the default row.

R5-2. Arrival pattern (130 BPM): BD 1000100010001000, SD 0000100000001000, TOM 0000001000010010, CH 1101110111011101, OH 0010001000100010, with SWING 0.3 (55 %) when nothing is stored (`DEFAULT_FX`). It starts at the visitor's first gesture, as before.

R5-3. Default view: azimuth 0, elevation 40 (the machine straight on, still 3D). The framing constants stay those of azimuth 45, the worst case, so no orbit ever pushes the machine out of the canvas.

R5-4. Social icons take their brand colour on hover and keyboard focus (`--brand`, data/socials.ts): Simple Icons 16.33.0 colours; X in white and TikTok in its cyan (both brands are black, invisible on graphite); gigmit green #16C98D from its stylesheet.

R5-5. Contact form (ui/sections/ContactForm.tsx) at the top of CONTACT: name, email, subject, message, sent by EmailJS with the account, template and public key of the /v1 form (src/data/emailjs.ts; the library loads only on send). The subject is filled from where the visitor came from (state/contactDraft.ts): CONTACT pad or dock Booking, LIVE "Book a set" Booking - live set, STUDIO "Ask about a lesson" Lesson, PRESS "Interview or press request" Press, MERCH "Order" Merch order - <product> with the order (size, colour, price) written in the message. A hidden trap field stops simple bots. Merch orders no longer use mailto.

R5-6. TRACKS: a Buy link to the release's Bandcamp page on each of the 37 tracks; the SoundCloud widget is created when TRACKS or MIXTAPES opens (no playback) so the first click plays inside the gesture, and its iframe allows encrypted-media (some tracks are served as encrypted HLS).

## 23. 2026-10-01 (second pass): SOLO / MUTE, LABEL and SONAA to CONTACT, compact machine

R6-1. Transport: RUN/STOP, CLEAR, MUTE, SOLO under the first four encoders (TRANSPORT.mute, TRANSPORT.solo; 20 key instances). MUTE toggles the selected voice out of the sequencer (no selection: all voices back on); SOLO plays only the selected voice (no selection: solo off). Pads hit by hand always sound. State in state/voices.ts, not persisted. MUTE lights orange (LIT.muteOn, MUTE_GLOW) when the selected voice is muted (or, with no selection, any voice); SOLO lights yellow during a solo. Twins with aria-pressed; the mobile dock has both in its transport row and strikes a muted instrument, rings the solo one in orange.

R6-2. LABEL and SONAA pads and sections are removed; their links (VRSTL Records on Bandcamp, sonaa.ca) join Massive Medias in CONTACT_LINKS. 12 pads, 6 columns: BD SD TOM CH OH TRACKS / MIXTAPES PRESS SHOWS CONTACT LIVE OPEN; pages keys 1 to 6, OPEN 7 and O.

R6-3. CONTACT order: socials, links, booking addresses, then the form. A request coming from a button (MERCH order, LIVE, STUDIO, PRESS) scrolls the panel body (only the body: scrollIntoView also moved the overflow-hidden panel frame) to the form.

R6-4. Compact machine: BODY 12.6 x 8 (was 14 x 9), panel, feet, connectors and PCB (11.2 x 6.8) derived from BODY; PCB parts, chips and PCB silk scaled by pcbAt(). Layout: OLED x -3.95 z -2.05, encoders x0 -5.55 pitch 0.9 z -0.6, transport z 0.48, pads x0 0.37 rows -2.05 / -0.75, trig keys x0 -5.55 pitch 0.74 z 2.4. Re-measured with measure().fit: MACHINE_H 10.64 (azimuth 45, elevation 38), ORBIT.targetY 1.216 (arrival view), SECTION_FRAME radius 7.47 / 8.33, EXPLODE.fitHalfH 7.61 (open stack 13.09 at azimuth 45), EXPLODE.targetY 2.976. Shadow extents 6.9 x 7.3 closed, 8.0 x 9.3 open, inside LIGHT_KEY.extent 11.5.

R6-5. LIVE is merged into PRESS (one pad): PRESS holds the setup and set lengths, "Book a set" (contact subject Booking - live set), "Interview or press request", then the documents (both PDFs with their size, /press/ and /techrider). 11 pads: BD SD TOM CH OH on top, TRACKS MIXTAPES PRESS SHOWS CONTACT below, OPEN alone in the sixth column, halfway between the two rows (pages keys 1 to 5, OPEN 6 and O). Silk: VOICES label, a line under the voice row and one before OPEN's column; the PAGES label is gone. Mobile grid: the five pages and RESET, then OPEN on the full row (open: GOODIES MERCH STUDIO and CLOSE).

R6-6. Intro (replaces spec 7.4's 700 ms rise): the machine arrives exploded and assembles itself in 3 s (INTRO): it holds open 300 ms, the PCB goes back into the chassis (300 to 1800 ms), the panel comes down (800 to 2800 ms, it starts later and ends later, so it always stays above the board), then the step LEDs run their test (2700 to 3100 ms). Explode.assemble(t) drives the layers while the OPEN store stays closed (chips and the OPEN pad never act as open); the framing follows from the wide shot to the closed machine. The first gesture ends it at once (Explode.snap); nothing under reduced motion. Measured: plateau 1 / pcb 1 at 0.26 s, 0.90 / 0.08 at 1.4 s, 0.28 / 0 at 2.0 s, 0 / 0 at 3.4 s, then 0 frames at rest.

R6-7. Perspective camera (replaces the orthographic one: in parallel projection the box read as distorted once orbited). PerspectiveCamera, vertical field 30 deg (ORBIT.fovDeg). The framing keeps its rule (hw: the half-width seen in the pivot plane, fixed, worst case at azimuth 45); the camera distance follows, D = (hw / aspect) / tan(15 deg), and Orbit.place() reads orbit.distance. The section framing shifts the render window by setViewOffset, in px, so zooming never moves the machine's centre. hit.ts: the view ray goes from the near plane to the far plane (unproject at -1 and 1), the occlusion ray points at the camera position, the projected ellipse of the encoders is divided by w. Checked: the twin centre of 7 objects picks its own object at azimuth 0, 45, 180 and 300, and pad BD at zoom 1, 1.5 and 2.2.

R6-8. Desktop header (ui/Header.tsx, 52 px over a fading gradient): the Maudite Machine logo (/logo/mauditemachine-logo-gold.svg) back to the arrival view, and a menu whose links press the machine's buttons: Tracks, Mixtapes, Press, Shows, Contact (actions.page, the pad and its trace), then Goodies, Merch, Studio (the hood opens, then the chip's section). Hidden under 768 px (the dock carries the same buttons).

R6-9. No autoplay: the arrival pattern is loaded, nothing plays until RUN (the first-gesture autoRun is removed). Pattern storage key 'mm.v4.pattern.2': patterns stored before are left aside once so everyone gets the new arrival pattern.

R6-10. Velocities: each step is 0 (empty), 1 (high, gain 1), 2 (mid, 0.6) or 3 (low, 0.32); a tap moves to the next notch, 0 1 2 3 0 (an old '1' stays high); hold or Delete clears. trigger() puts a gain node between the voice and the bus. LEDs: ledSet, ledMid (68 %), ledLow (38 %); the dock steps the same three oranges. Screen: STEP 05 CH HIGH / MID / LOW / OFF. Measured at the analyser (SD): -4.4, -8.8, -13.1 dBFS peak. Arrival pattern with velocities: CH 2301230123012301, TOM 0000003000020010, BD, SD and OH high.

R6-11. Step press: the trig key goes down 0.045 and glows (STEP_PRESS) in 40 ms, back in 160 ms (Stage.pressStep, paint tweens, no shadow pass; glow only under reduced motion); dock steps scale to 0.92 while pressed.

R6-12. Default view: azimuth 0, elevation 69 (the angle Mika picked on a screenshot: the panel read whole, the front face a thin edge; matched by the panel depth / front width ratio 0.59 and back / front width 0.93, which camera.zoom does not change). The intro starts at elevation 40 (the exploded layers show) and reaches 69 as the machine assembles (elevation = 69 + (40 - 69) x explode frame), set directly on the orbit so the view never counts as moved (RESET VIEW stays hidden). Measured: 40 deg at 0.2 s, 53.8 at 1.8 s, 68.5 at 2.5 s, 69 at the end.

R6-13. Screen during a track: line 2 is the title, scrolling one column per 250 ms when it does not fit in 20 columns (title + 3 spaces, looping; it restarts at each new track); line 3 (when no message) shows the position, a progress bar and the duration (LcdState.l3 / r3 / bar). The screen draws the bar between the two times (OLED_BAR) and keeps its rect; a hotspot over the line 3 band (kind 'seek', enabled only while the bar shows) sends a click to Stage.seekAt, which casts a ray on the screen mesh and turns the texture x into a position (sc.seek, which the engine bridge now passes). The current row of TRACKS and MIXTAPES also gets a native range input (mouse, finger, arrow keys). Checked with the mock engine: a click at 75 % of the screen bar gave 0.752, the range input at 300/1000 gave 0.301, playback kept going.

## 24. 2026-09-30: revision 5 (OPEN breathes, a real PCB, TONE, STRETCH)

R7-1. OPEN breathes. Dock button: keyframes on filter brightness (0.88 to 1) and box-shadow (0 to 6 px of orange), 1.6 s ease-in-out infinite alternate (a 3.2 s cycle), nothing else moves; stopped when open (CLOSE), on :focus-visible and under reduced motion (media query and data-v4-motion). The 3D OPEN pad is not animated through its emissive: that would keep the render loop running. Its twin (already placed on the pad's silhouette at each rendered frame) carries a ::after veil (rgba 255,170,110 at 0.08) and a 6 px orange glow whose opacity alone animates on the same cycle and stops in the same cases (compositor animation, 0 WebGL frames at rest, measured).

R7-2. PCB (scene/pcb.ts rewritten): chamfered board (0.03) with a bare fiberglass edge; colour map and ORM map (ambient occlusion in red, roughness in green, metalness in blue) at 2048 x 1280 (1024 x 640 mobile), drawn once when the PCB first shows (the intro shows it exploded, so in practice at the intro; reduced motion: at the first OPEN), 13 ms measured. Varnish: low-frequency colour and roughness blotches (roughness about 0.75, metalness 0.05), faint dust specks. Copper (metalness 0.85, roughness 0.34) on a 0.2 grid at 45 deg only (no 90 deg turn): signal 0.03, power 0.1, 4 differential pairs (two lines 0.07 apart, mitred offset), 2 length-matching meanders, signal traces that end on a via (layer change), free vias, a hatched ground pour (back left) with stitching vias every 0.32 and 0.08 clearance around parts; gold (ENIG) pads under every leg and terminal; mounting holes with a metal ring and Phillips screws. Contact shadows baked under each part (colour darkened, AO darkened). A small procedural equirectangular environment (256 x 128, a warm softbox, a yellow rim, dark floor) gives the metals their reflections; three prefilters it once. Parts: electrolytics of two heights (sleeve, light polarity band, aluminium top with a raised cross), ceramic caps (beige, silver ends), SMD resistors (black, silver ends, a 3-digit code), ICs with silver legs on the sides, pin-1 mark and a white reference, oval metal crystals lying down, a TO-220 regulator against a finned vertical heatsink, the coin cell in its holder, a 2 x 8 shrouded header with gold pins, a blue 3-way screw terminal, a lit LED (D1, PWR) whose additive glow lies on the copper. Four merged geometries, one per material (plastics, metals, white markings from an atlas, LED): one InstancedMesh per family would cost a draw call each. Measured in the open view at 1440 x 900: 19 draw calls (16 before), 27,424 triangles (24,088 before, +268 of them from the seventh encoder). No overlap between the 48 part footprints (computed), none outside the board.

R7-3. TONE, -1 to 1 (screen -100 to +100), 0 at the centre and the default (not persisted). Right: pitch up to +7 semitones and a high-pass from 20 to 180 Hz; left: down to -7 semitones and a low-pass from 20 kHz to 900 Hz. The pitch is a factor 2^(st/12) on the voices' frequencies at scheduling time (BD start and end, TOM, SD band-pass and body, CH and OH high-pass); there is no bass voice since OH replaced it. 0 is a real bypass (audio/insert.ts): the input is connected straight to the next stage, no filter, no unity gain; engaged, a 20 ms crossfade between a dry gain and the filter branch, which is unlinked 60 ms after coming back to 0. While engaged the unused filter stays at its neutral bound (high-pass 20 Hz, low-pass 20 kHz), never 0 Hz or Nyquist: there browsers switch to an identity transfer function and an old filter state jumped (a click measured on a slow drag, fixed). Snap: |v| < 0.04 is 0; arrows and wheel move TONE by 0.05; double tap back to 0. The mark points up at 0; a silk tick marks the centre.

R7-4. STRETCH, 0 to 1, next to TONE (seven encoders, pitch 0.74, r 0.27). A granular time stretch on the drum bus (audio/stretch.ts): an AudioWorklet (loaded from a blob URL at the first value above 0) writes the bus into a 2 s ring buffer and reads it back in Hann grains of 60 ms (low) to 120 ms (full), 50 % overlap, the read head moving 1 / ratio as fast as the write head (ratio 1 to 4); each grain plays at speed 1, so the pitch stays; when the lag leaves the buffer the read head comes back to the present (the grain crossfade makes the join). Mix: dry 1 - v, stretched v. The sequencer does not slow down. At 0 the worklet is disconnected and stops (process returns false): nothing runs at rest.

R7-5. Tests (offline renders of the real graph, audio/drums.ts renderOffline, and the live graph with ?mute=1): each voice alone, TONE 0 and the bare graph give the same samples (0 different out of 48,000; the full mix differs only by the bus summation order, 2.4e-7, as two identical bare renders do). TOM 110 to 164.81 Hz at +1 and 73.42 Hz at -1 (expected 164.81 and 73.42), BD 48 to 71.97 and 32.04 Hz; mix peak 0.955 at +1 and 0.775 at -1. Slow drags across 0 (both ways, back and forth, full sweep): largest second difference 0.0014 against 0.0008 for the sines alone (0.047 before the fix). STRETCH: at 0 no worklet exists and the insert is direct; back to 0, the worklet is stopped and removed. A 440 Hz sine stretched at 1 keeps 440.4 Hz and the same second difference as the sine alone after start-up (no click at grain joins or buffer returns); a TOM tail goes from 0.22 s to 0.89 s (x 4.05). Mid-course, the dry and stretched copies comb-filter on held tones (a slow phasing), without clicks. Clock unchanged: 115.4 ms steps at 130 BPM before and during STRETCH. Chunk: 82.4 KB (v4) + 144.8 KB (three) + 5.6 KB (CSS) = 232.8 KB gzip, under 250.

## 25. 2026-10-01: effects per track, DELAY and CHORUS

R8-1. Each voice (BD, SD, TOM, CH, OH) has its own strip (audio/drums.ts Channel): LEVEL (gain (v / 0.8)^2, exactly 1 at the 0.8 default) -> TONE (filter insert, audio/tone.ts) -> DIST (insert, fx.ts buildDrive, same law as the bus DIST) -> CHORUS (insert, audio/chorus.ts) -> bus, plus a REVERB send and a DELAY send. Per-voice TONE also pitches the voice: its frequencies are multiplied by pitchFactor(pattern TONE) x pitchFactor(voice TONE). State in audio/voicefx.ts, in memory only (a visit starts neutral).

R8-2. A selected pad (pattern.instrument, the same selection the step keys use) makes TONE, LEVEL, DIST, REVERB, DELAY and CHORUS set that voice; TEMPO, STRETCH and SWING stay pattern-wide. No selection: the knobs set the whole pattern as before. The knobs turn to the target's values when the selection changes; the twins add the voice to their name ("Distortion, bass drum"); the screen says KNOBS > BD on selection, KNOBS > PATTERN when it is cleared (Escape, or the selected instrument tapped again in the mobile dock), and BD DIST 40% while turning.

R8-3. DELAY and CHORUS, pattern-wide (persisted with the pattern in pattern.fx, like DIST and REVERB) and per voice. DELAY (audio/sends.ts): one shared line, dotted eighth (3 steps, 346 ms at 130 BPM, follows the tempo), feedback 0.42 through a 3.8 kHz low-pass and a 180 Hz high-pass, send 0.55 at 1; it stops 8 s after the last send went back to 0. REVERB now runs through the same send bus (one convolver for the pattern send after LEVEL and the five voice sends). CHORUS: two delay lines (14 and 21 ms) modulated by LFOs (0.53 and 0.71 Hz, +/-3.5 ms), panned -0.7 / +0.7, dry 1 - 0.35 v, wet 0.7 v; on the bus between TONE and STRETCH. Every insert and send is a real bypass at its neutral value (audio/insert.ts); the chorus branch and its LFOs exist only while engaged.

R8-4. Nine encoders: TEMPO TONE STRETCH LEVEL SWING DIST REVERB DELAY CHORUS, r 0.24, pitch 0.64 from x -5.6; labels share a 0.6 max width. RUN, CLEAR, MUTE and SOLO move to the first four trig-key columns (x -5.55, pitch 0.74).

R8-5. Measured (offline renders of the real graph): each voice through its neutral strip equals the voice without a strip, 0 different samples; SD rendered with every BD effect at full is identical to SD alone; a TOM with DELAY 1 repeats at 0.36, 0.75, 1.08 s (peaks 0.58, 0.29, 0.14); CHORUS on held sines adds no discontinuity (second difference 0.0012 against 0.0008). Live: BD selected, DIST arrow up x5 -> BD 0.10, pattern 0; after Escape -> pattern 0.02, BD kept. Delay unit gone 8 s after its send returned to 0; 0 frames at rest.

## 26. 2026-10-01: RANDOM, STRETCH like Impulse, velocity bars

R9-1. STRETCH works like the global Time control of Ableton Impulse: -100 to +100 %, 0 at the centre (same centre detent as TONE, +/-0.04, and a silkscreen tick at noon). It shortens (left) or lengthens (right) every hit: pitch sweeps and envelopes are multiplied by 4^v (x0.25 to x4), frequencies stay. It is applied when a hit is scheduled, like the TONE pitch (audio/time.ts): no processing on the bus, nothing runs at rest, a hit already playing keeps its length. STRETCH is now per voice too (a selected pad), the pattern and voice factors multiply, the product is clamped to x0.15..x6. The 2 ms attack of the BD is not stretched. Noise voices loop their 1 s buffer, so a long OH or SD tail never runs out. The granular AudioWorklet of revision 5 (audio/stretch.ts) is removed. Screen: STRETCH -40%, BD STRETCH +20%; twin: "-40 %, shorter".

R9-2. Tests (offline renders, nothing played): lengths to -60 dB at -1 / 0 / +1: TOM 0.08 / 0.30 / 1.20 s, BD 0.16 / 0.48 / 1.74 s, OH 0.14 / 0.40 / 1.42 s, SD 0.08 / 0.17 / 0.64 s. Pitch kept: TOM tail 110 Hz, BD tail 48 Hz at 0 and +1. Each voice with a neutral strip equals the bare graph (0 difference); SD is identical with or without a stretched BD; a voice STRETCH of +1 equals a pattern STRETCH of +1 on that voice; both at +1 clamp at x6 (TOM 1.8 s).

R9-3. Velocity bars: each step shows up to three bars stacked above its key (the bottom one is the old LED, pitch 0.09, 0.22 x 0.055): strong three, mid two, soft one, empty only the bottom LED, off (line). Lit bars are ledSet; during playback the current step's lit bars (or its bottom LED when empty) turn yellowHi; hover only lights the bottom LED of an empty step (ledHover). Before, the single LED changed hue with velocity and the hover colour covered it, so a click hid what it had set. The mobile dock steps carry the same bars (currentColor, above the number). seq.info() adds bars ("2103...") and barTones.

R9-4. RANDOM: a square button like CLEAR, left of the voice pads (x0 - pitch, on the voice row), label on the pad label line; twin "Random house pattern"; in the mobile dock a dice button left of BD. It draws a house pattern from a grammar (audio/house.ts): BD four on the floor (sometimes a soft pickup), clap on 2 and 4 (sometimes a ghost or a pickup), OH on the offbeats (or none, then CH takes the offbeats), CH 16ths with accents chosen per beat and a few ghosts dropped, never on an OH step, TOM one of six syncopated figures with varied velocities (or none). Tempo, effects and voice settings are kept; the result always differs from the current pattern; it is saved like any edit. Screen: RANDOM HOUSE. 2000 draws: 0 rule broken, 1912 distinct.

R9-5. Chunk: 85.4 KB (v4) + 144.3 KB (three) + 6.1 KB (CSS) = 235.8 KB gzip.

R9-6. Two encoder rows (the nine-knob row was too crowded on the left): TEMPO, TONE, STRETCH, LEVEL and SWING stay under the screen, back to r 0.27 (collar 0.32, mark 0.18) on the trig-key grid (pitch 0.74 from x -5.55, above RUN/STOP, CLEAR, MUTE and SOLO), z -0.6, labels at -0.1; DIST, REVERB, DELAY and CHORUS move under the page pads, centred on their block (x 0.87 to 3.87, pitch 1.0), at the transport height (z 0.48), labels on the transport label line (0.98). Positions come from theme.encPos(i). All nine hotspots pick their own knob, no overlap with any other hotspot.

R9-7. Trig keys (and the square buttons, same geometry): really rounded corners and top edges, same radius 0.04, three segments on desktop (588 triangles), two on mobile (300); with one segment the rounding was a 45 deg chamfer.

## 27. 2026-10-01: panel layout, MIX page, wider OPEN, realistic PCB

R10-1. Layout. TEMPO and VOLUME (the former LEVEL: master volume, or the selected voice's volume) sit right of the screen, centred between its bezel and the voice pads (pitch 0.86), at the screen and voice-pad height, labels on the pad label line. The seven others sit under the page pads, centred on the pad block (BD left edge to OPEN right edge), pitch 0.8 plus 0.2 between groups: SWING | TONE STRETCH | DIST CHORUS DELAY REVERB (effects in signal order). The transport row is RUN/STOP, CLEAR, RANDOM, MUTE, SOLO on the first five trig-key columns, moved to z 0.58 (labels 1.06) with the knob row, so the knobs never hide the page labels in the open view. ENCODERS is in reading order; positions come from theme.encPos (id-based). Silk "FIRMWARE V.2.1 / 2026" replaces "V.4 / 2026".

R10-2. MIX page (state/lcdMix.ts, scene/screen.ts): turning VOLUME with a pad selected shows, for 1.5 s after the last change, five drawn dials in the Elektron style: one 128 px cell per voice separated by rules, the voice name on top (the voice being set in negative, rounded tag), a 270 deg track with the value arc and a needle, the value (0 to 100) below. Any other screen message closes it. The text lines (twin) read "VOLUME SD / BD80 SD66 TOM80 / CH80 OH80".

R10-3. OPEN opens wider: lift 4.0 (was 3.6), slide back 2.4 (was 1.5), tilt -14 deg (was -12): about twice as much board shows. Framing: the stack is 11.30 high at the arrival view and 16.76 at azimuth 45; fitHalfH 8.4 keeps the arrival share of the height (67 %) and still holds azimuth 45 whole; targetY 5.562 measured.

R10-4. PCB. Traces, the ground pour and vias run under the solder mask (pcbTrace #22573A, non-metal, slightly glossier than the board) instead of bare copper; only component pads and mounting rings stay gold. Thinner, denser routing (signal 0.022, power 0.08, pairs 0.018; 150 trace attempts, 90 vias). Satin mask (roughness 0.55). The three clickable chips are QFPs: fine legs (0.04 wide, pitch 0.1) on four sides, 14 front and back, 9 left and right. Silk at real scale: MAUDITE MACHINE 26 px, MM-808 REV 4.0 18 px, designators 10 px; chip outlines white like the rest; chip designators above their right corner. A new row of five resistors and four ceramics under MAUDITE MACHINE.

## 28. 2026-10-01: MASTER and voice VOLUME, aligned knob row, transport under the screen, real back panel

R11-1. Two volumes. MASTER (encoder id level, the former LEVEL) is the main volume only, never a voice; it sits above TEMPO right of the screen (z -2.62 and -1.55, labels 0.45 below; TEMPO, in front, does not hide the MASTER label). VOLUME (new encoder id vol) only sets the selected voice's level (voiceFx level): with no pad selected it does nothing and the screen says TAP A PAD FIRST (twin: "Voice volume, tap a pad first"); with a pad it shows the MIX page. The knob shows the voice level (0.8 neutral without a selection).

R11-2. The knob row under the page pads sits exactly on the trig-key grid: VOLUME, SWING, TONE, STRETCH, DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB above keys 9 to 16 (x 0.37 to 5.55, pitch 0.74), z 0.58, labels 1.06 (max width 0.66).

R11-3. Transport under the screen: RUN/STOP, CLEAR, RANDOM, MUTE, SOLO are 0.8 squares (0.62 before, about 30 px more on screen), z -0.5, left-aligned with the screen bezel (x -5.48, pitch 1.0), labels 0.58 below like the pads. They get their own rounded geometry (InstancedMesh buttons, same material, regular corners) instead of scaled trig keys.

R11-4. Back panel (theme BACK, machine.ts, scene/backplate.ts): PHONES, MAIN OUT L and R (6.35 jacks), SYNC IN and OUT (3.5 jacks), MIDI IN and OUT (5-pin DIN: flange, shell, dark insert with five holes and a key), USB-C (stadium shell, tongue), DC 12V jack, POWER rocker switch, merged into the chassis (no draw call). A silkscreen texture on a plane 0.003 in front of the face (one draw call): the MAUDITE MACHINE wordmark, MM-808 DRUM MACHINE, FIRMWARE V.2.1 / 2026, a serial sticker with a barcode (S/N MM808-000808), each port's name, and brackets with titles over MAIN OUT, SYNC and MIDI. The old mains inlet and USB-B are gone.

Press kit: six A4 pages (docs/presskit-2027), a photo on every page and a large one on pages 1, 3 and 5: 1 cover (full room facing the stage), 2 Background (colour portrait in pink light, bio, the four key figures, a wide booth photo), 3 The sound (large booth photo, the set, DJ set and hybrid live photos, edits), 4 Selected shows (the crowd seen from the booth, performances, label and roster, two photos), 5 Listen (large booth photo, mix, tracks, platforms, Limbos, the 13 releases as covers), 6 Technical and contact (tech rider, hospitality, contacts with the black and white portrait as a thumbnail, links). Pages are WebP images (public/press/pages/presskit-2027-01..06.webp, 1200 px, quality 78, 870 KB total: 1400 px at quality 80 went over 900 KB, so the width dropped first), in one continuous scrolling column, laid out as sheets; the first loads eagerly, the others lazily; under the last page, the PDF's links as HTML (same 27 destinations, checked by the build). R14-3. Press feedback (2026-10-01): RUN/STOP, CLEAR, RANDOM, MUTE and SOLO sink and glow on every press, like the trig keys (stage.pressButton; mouse, keyboard twins, Space and the mobile dock); a button's own glow (RUN playing, MUTE, SOLO) is kept under the press glow. Light mode: a pressed key or button turns solid orange (PRESS_TINT, about #F9945D measured) with a stronger glow; the pale glow alone read as peach on the light keys.

R14-4. Shows: Cirque du Boudoir, 30 Oct 2026, Théâtre du Lion d'Or, and 31 Oct 2026, Théâtre Paradoxe, b2b with Omni (public/events.json).

R14-5. Transport indicators (2026-10-01, theme BTN_LED): a thin light strip on top of each transport button, near its back edge (0.46 x 0.05), sinking with the button. Off: a discreet slot (line token). On: orange ledSet, yellowHi on RUN (orange is lost on its red). RUN/STOP, MUTE and SOLO are latching: the strip stays lit while playing, while the selected voice is muted, while a solo runs. Every press flashes the strip (full for half of 520 ms, then it fades), the only sign on CLEAR and RANDOM, one-shot buttons. The buttons themselves no longer change colour: RUN stays red while playing (RUN_GLOW, LIT.runOn, LIT.muteOn and MUTE_GLOW removed), MUTE and SOLO stay neutral; the press tint of R14-3 is kept.

Page #0C0B09 (oklch 0.15 0.004 70, Sonaa's dark page), Sonaa's 800 px SVG granite tile inline at 12 % on a fixed pseudo-element under everything (v4.css), theme-color #0C0B09. The canvas clears transparent (theme BACKDROP) and the floor writes only its shadow as alpha (corrected for sRGB compositing), so the granite runs under and around the machine, on mobile down through the dock. No halo in dark: an additive lift (colour above alpha) is only kept by Chrome where alpha is non zero and drew a light rectangle around the machine. Light mode is unchanged (opaque cream floor).

R14-7. Two knob rows (2026-10-01). GLOBAL, left, under RUN/STOP, over trig keys 1 to 6: SWING, STRETCH, DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB, always the whole pattern (as the old knobs without a selected pad). VOICE, right, under the pads, over keys 10 to 16: VOLUME, TONE, STRETCH, DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB, always the selected voice; without a selected pad they show the voice defaults and answer TAP A PAD FIRST. SWING left the voice row, TONE is voice only (the global tone keeps its centre). Voice knobs have their own ids (vstretch, vdist, vchorus, vdelay, vreverb; vol and tone kept) and are scaled 0.85 (ENCODER.voiceScale: 34.5 px against 40.6 at 1440 x 900, 6 px less), their labels follow the collar. Same black knobs and colours in both rows. Each row has a group line under its labels, broken in the middle by GLOBAL or VOICE (silk, cap 0.06). The transport row moved up 0.06 (z -0.56) and both knob rows down 0.04 (z 0.62) so the global knobs clear the transport labels. Twins: 15 sliders, "Global distortion", "Voice distortion, snare" or "Voice distortion, tap a pad first".

R14-8. OPEN breathes in the scene (2026-10-01, theme OPEN_BREATHE). The pad itself pulses: its backlight and halo go from 45 % to 100 % and its tint (OPEN_TINT) from 50 % to 100 %, a 3.2 s sine, at 20 images per second without a shadow pass (Stage.stepBreathe, Pads.breathe). Measured on the pad top at 1440 x 900: dark 228,103,38 to 255,161,67; light 223,87,9 to 248,119,78 (the light alone was 246,112,63 to 248,119,78, invisible on the saturated orange, hence the tint). Only with the machine closed, on the desktop tier and in full motion; open, reduced motion or mobile tier: full light and the loop sleeps again. The CSS glow on the OPEN twin (a screen-aligned rectangle blinking over the pad) is removed; the mobile Dock OPEN button keeps its CSS breathing.

Displayed colours aimed and converted by pads.ts into a per-channel multiplier of the rubber albedo. Light: rose #E3B1A9 (measured 229,177,168), blue #8DB2E4 (139,178,230). Dark: rose #7A5662 (measured 150,98,106; #7E4E50 read as brick under ACES), blue #2B5896 (41,99,164). The mobile Dock voice buttons take the same colours (muted ones stay struck through).

R14-10. Fine control (2026-10-01, like Ableton): Shift held while dragging a knob makes it ten times finer (DIAL_FINE.drag 0.1: 1 % per 15 px instead of 1.5 px); taken or released mid-gesture, the travel restarts from the current value, no jump. Shift + wheel: 1 % per notch (macOS turns Shift + wheel into deltaX, read instead); TEMPO keeps 1 BPM per notch. Keyboard unchanged (Shift + arrows = big steps, slider convention). Measured: 30 px = 20 % plain, 2 % with Shift.

Positioning (2026-10-01): "Indie dance and psy prog" (FR "Indie dance et psy prog", ES "Indie dance y psy prog") replaces "Deep techno and indie dance" everywhere: i18n EN/FR/ES (bios, genres "Indie Dance · Psy Prog · Electronic", radar, catalogue, SEO titles), seo-meta.json, index.html (title, description, keywords, JSON-LD genre ["Indie Dance", "Psy Prog", "Electronic"] and knowsAbout, Open Graph, Twitter), manifest, v2, v3, v1 admin, the v4 page title, the press kit source. No "deep techno", "hypnotic techno" or "hard techno" label left in src and public (grep -ri empty); "techno" stays in descriptive sentences (Techno Parade). R14-12. Mobile (2026-10-01). Touch: one finger never turns the view (scene/orbit.ts), it stays with knobs, pads and steps; a knob is grabbed at once (the 250 ms rest of ENC_GRAB is gone); two fingers turn the view (their midpoint) and pinch to zoom. Mouse and pen unchanged. Framing on the mobile layout follows the front width (FRONT_W 14, not PLATEAU_W 16.3): the machine is about 20 % larger. Header: logo left, hamburger right (ui/MobileHeader.tsx, 52 px over the top of the scene on a gradient); the menu drops under it: the five pages, GOODIES MERCH STUDIO, Open / Close the machine, Reset view, Dark / Light; a choice, Escape, the cross or a tap outside closes it (focus back to the hamburger). Dock compact (--dock-h-mobile 174 px): instruments 38, two rows of 8 steps 34, transport 40; navigation, OPEN and the appearance toggle moved to the menu; a step tapped without an instrument flashes the instruments. The scene takes the rest of the height (100dvh - 174 px). Measured at 375 x 812: scene 638 px, Dock 174, no overflow; touch drag on REVERB 40 px = 27 %, view unmoved; one finger on the background: view unmoved; two fingers: turn and zoom.

Dock steps: velocity as one to three dots under the number (strong three, medium two, soft one), the number moves up 4 px; the three stacked bars of the machine overlapped the number. Safari 26 on iPhone (not the home-screen app, not CriOS / FxiOS / EdgiOS): its floating tab bar covers the bottom of the page with no announced safe area and hid the transport row; data-v4-iosbar='1' (user agent, Version/26 and later) reserves 64 px under the Dock (--ios-bar), taken from the scene, which has room to spare.
