MM-DECKS: the enlarged deck screen ran under the header logotype (its bezel at z -4.92); dj/theme.ts DECK.logo { h 0.32, z -5.13 }: smaller and higher on the decks, whole above the screen (the mixer keeps 0.42).
R14-94. MM-ARP oscillators like the Mini V (2026-10-04, Mika, a photo of the Arturia Mini V oscillator panel: "tu pourrais avoir les oscillateurs de cette maniere"). One row per oscillator, its number in an ink tab with the digit cut out, then WAVEFORM (the morphing selector and its yellow crown, size sel 0.85, phone 0.8), RANGE (LO 32' 16' 8' 4' 2', six detents over 180 deg, each one printed; 8' plays the note, LO three octaves down), SEMI (-7 to +7, fifteen detents over the full throw, odd values printed, 0 longer; chrome cap), FINE (-50 0 +50 cents; chrome cap), ON (a two-position switch, its name below, a red LED above that glows when on). They replace TUNE 2 and FINE (one spread around the note). Defaults keep the old sound: OSC 1 8', OSC 2 16', FINE -8 / +8 cents. Desktop: OSCILLATORS section widened to x -6.25..-1.05 (rows -0.72 and 0.66), the mixer and FM below (OSC 1, OSC 2, NOISE, FM, RATIO); FILTER, the envelopes, EFFECTS and OUTPUT tightened to the right (rules at -1.05, 0.75, 3.65, 5.07). Phone: rows -2.25 and -1.0 with FM and RATIO at their end, no OSCILLATORS bracket (the numbers say it), filter row 0.2, envelope rows 1.58 and 2.56. Engine (moog.worklet.js): tune1 / tune2 in semitones (RANGE + SEMI), fine1 / fine2 in cents (smoothed), on1 / on2 multiply each oscillator's level (smoothed, no click); the FM operator follows OSC 1. Knobs: a second InstancedMesh for chrome caps (aluminium disc, black mark, metalness 0.55); knobThrowDeg gives each knob its throw (switch, RANGE 180, full). A stored state or a preset from before converts (migrateKnobs: TUNE 2 -1 OCT / 0 / 5TH / +1 / +2 -> RANGE 2 16' / 8' / 8' +7 / 4' / 2'; FINE spread -> FINE 1 and 2 around the note). RANDOM writes the same choices as before through RANGE and SEMI. Checked muted: offline pitch 8' 440 Hz, 16' 220, SEMI +7 660, FINE +50 452.9, OSC 2 alone at 16' 220, both off silent, no NaN; LCD and twins read RANGE 1 8', SEMI 2 +7, FINE 1 -9 CT, OSC 1 ON; 300 RANDOM draws valid; desktop and phone screenshots; production build passes.
R14-95. MM-DECKS waveform views (2026-10-04, Mika, with a photo of decks C and D: "la waveforme est correcte mais on a du mal a voir les choses, ya pas un autre affichage pour ca ?"). dj/math.ts bandEnergy: three bands per detail slice (400 per second) on the sum of the channels, lows under 180 Hz (two cascaded Butterworth low-passes, 24 dB/oct), highs over 2.5 kHz (two high-passes), mids a real band-pass (two high-passes at 180 Hz then a low-pass at 2.5 kHz: x minus lows and highs kept a phase residue that followed the kick); filter states flushed under 1e-20 each slice (denormals made it ten times slower). wavePeaks3: RMS on a short window (lows 4 slices, mids 2), each band brought to 1 at its 99.5th percentile, that reference never under a fifth of the strongest band's (nearly absent highs stay small). bandOverview: the whole-track bands from the detail ones. A generator: dj/engine.ts measureBands runs it 10 ms at a time after the track is placed (0.56 s for a 6-minute track in Node), dropped if another track arrives; djWaveBands tells the rig, which re-uploads. dj/waveform.ts: RGBA texture (r the energy as before, g b a the bands), uBands per deck, uMode. Three views for every deck, kept in the mixer's stored state (djState.wave, default 3BAND): 3BAND (lows blue, mids amber, highs bone, drawn one over the other, heights 0.92 / 0.72 / 0.48), RGB (the outline of the three bands tinted by their mix: lows red, mids green, highs blue, lighter at the core), MONO (the bone energy of before, also while the bands are computing). Played part darker in every view; head, cues and loop bounds ringed in black so they read on any colour. Deck screen: overview u 0.02..0.71, a WAVE key at u 0.73..0.845 (its name, the current view in orange; touch: next view), zoom unchanged; keyboard V. Checked muted (master 0, never PLAY): a synthetic 124 BPM WAV (kick, offbeat bass, hats from 6 s, a pad break 14..22 s): kick slice lows 0.56 / mids 0.00, break mids 1.00 / lows 0.01; desktop and phone screenshots of the three views; production build passes.
R14-96. MM-DECKS REMOVE DECK readable and safe (2026-10-04, Mika: "comment on fait quand on a add un deck par erreur, comment on l'enleve ?"). The key existed on the last added deck but its name was 0.065 high. Now REMOVE DECK in orange at 0.11, the key 0.72 x 0.28 at x 1.42, z -5.1 (clear of the screen bezel), and the removable deck drops its DIGITAL DECK subtitle to make room. A deck that is playing does not go in one press: the first arms REMOVE (djState deck.remove: the key glows orange, the deck screen shows PLAYING: PRESS REMOVE DECK AGAIN in place of the artist), a second within 3 s stops it and removes it, otherwise it disarms by itself; a paused or empty deck goes in one press as before. Twin name says "while it plays: press twice". Checked with a simulated playing flag (no audio): armed, removed on the second press (paused, flag cleared), a paused deck C removed in one press, disarmed after 3.2 s.
R14-97. MM-ARP OVERDRIVE in place of DIST (2026-10-04, Mika: "pour ARP c'est pas DIST qu'on veut c'est OVERDRIVE"). The knob keeps its id 'dist' (stored states and presets still load), label OVERDRIVE, aria Overdrive. moog.worklet.js: a Tube Screamer topology on the voice sum at the oversampled rate: the clean signal passes whole; a copy high-passed at 720 Hz (one pole) is amplified up to 1 + 40 d^2, rounded by tanh(u + 0.22) - tanh(0.22) (slightly asymmetric: even harmonics), low-passed at 4.2 kHz and added at 0.42 (ramped in over the first quarter of the throw), the level trimmed by 1 / (1 + 0.85 d). The filter input drive drops from 1 + 5 d to 1 + 2.5 d, the per-voice output tanh from 1 + 7 d to 1 + 1.5 d. Checked offline (bass note C2, sends off): from 0 to full, lows (40-180 Hz) +3.5 dB, mids (0.7-3.5 kHz) +12.8 dB, peaks -5.7 to -3.7 dBFS, no NaN.
R14-98. MM-RYTM 909 kick and TWEAKS under the hood (2026-10-04, Mika: "le kick sonne flat.. met un kick de 909 s'il te plait et tweakable comme il faut dans le OPEN de la machine ; sous le capot, a la place des liens de mauditemachine qui sont deja dans le header, un systeme de Tweaks.. genre changement de samples pour les voices"). audio/kit.ts: a stored kit (mm.v4.kit.1): a sound per voice family (KICK BD, SNARE SD, HATS CH/CHopen/OH, CLAP CP, TOMS TOM/HT, RIM RS) among 909, 808 and MM (the sounds of before; KICK 909 by default, the rest MM), and five knobs 0..1: TUNE, ATTACK, DECAY, DRIVE (the kick, all three sounds) and SNAPPY (the snare, all three). audio/shotsdsp.ts synthesizes the 909 and 808 sounds (no sample of either machine): 909 kick (sweep from 4.4x the note in 4.5 ms then 30 ms, slightly pinched sine, decay tau 0.09 to 0.63 s, trigger click near 3.2 kHz and a 3 ms breath, tanh 1.2 to 5.2), 808 kick (bridged-T sine, a quarter drop in 10 ms, tau 0.16 to 1.3 s), 909/808 snares (two oscillators and noise, SNAPPY the noise share), 909 hats (metal x1.47 plus as much noise, 6-bit rounding) and 808 hats (the six squares in two narrow bands), 909/808 claps (bursts and tail), toms and rims; TUNE gives the kick an octave around 52 Hz (808: 49 Hz), DECAY its tail; the MM kick and snare take the same knobs (their defaults rebuild the old sound). Kick peak trims: 909 -1.5 dB, 808 -2 dB (RMS over 150 ms: 909 -7.5, 808 -8.6, MM -8.6 dB). audio/shots.ts: the kit signature (only what changes that sound) is part of each sample key; jobs carry the kit to the worker; a turned TWEAK re-renders its family in the background after 120 ms (stale queued jobs of that sound dropped), a hit before then plays the nearest ready sample (+20 for another kit setting). scene/tweakplate.ts: the TWEAKS plate made generic (geometry, silk, knobs, 2- and 3-step switches whose name rises above their middle detent); voyager/tweaks.ts now builds on it (the ARP plate renders identically). scene/rytmTweaks.ts: the RYTM plate in place of the page chips (Pcb chips: false): desktop 10.2 x 3.95 at board z 1.2, two rows of six (KICK switch, TUNE, ATTACK, DECAY, DRIVE, title / SNARE, SNAPPY, HATS, CLAP, TOMS, RIM); portrait 6.3 x 9.9, four rows of three. Pcb clear: no decor part or footprint under the plate (they poked through); an empty part bucket builds an empty geometry (the phone board is almost all plate). Hotspots rk-<id> (kind rknob), live with the hood open like the chips were; DialId r:<id> (drag, wheel, double tap for the default, a tap steps a switch), twins (role slider, arrows, readout). The RYTM LCD reads the tweak (KICK TUNE 64 HZ, KICK 808, SNARE SNAPPY 50). RYTM presets keep the kit (an older preset leaves it). Menu GOODIES / MERCH / STUDIO open their section directly (no hood). Checked muted: all models render (no NaN), 909 TUNE 0 / 0.5 / 1 -> 37 / 52 / 74 Hz; live: 0 tweak hotspots closed, 11 open, a 60 px mouse drag moves TUNE 0.5 -> 0.9, a tap moves HATS MM -> 909; offline single BD at 808 TUNE 0.8 -> 60 Hz (60.3 expected), 909 -> 52 Hz; desktop and phone screenshots; production build passes.
R14-99. Oscilloscope in OPEN (2026-10-04, Mika, with an occularScope capture on his kick track: "dans OPEN je voudrais un oscilloscope pour voir l'onde comme le fait Occulator ; un peu parametrable, et de ce fait on peut voir si la phase bouge ou pas ; je veux quelque chose d'ultra precis"). audio/scope.worklet.js: an AudioWorklet (two inputs: A stereo, B summed to mono; its silent output through a 0 gain so it is pulled) copies blocks of 1024 samples stamped with currentFrame (the context clock, sample exact) and posts them; port {on} puts it to sleep. audio/scope.ts: a ring of 2^19 samples per channel (about 11 s at 48 kHz), the taps (audio/drums.ts scopeTaps: RYTM out, the kick channel alone, ARP out, KICK+ARP as two traces, OUT after the limiter), the beat grid (clock.gridAfter, else arp.grid of the arpeggio playing alone), settings stored in mm.v4.scope.1 (FREEZE not kept). routeMachines now disconnects only the previous destination (the scope taps survive the MM-DECKS taking the machines). ui/Scope.tsx (mounted outside .v4-stage: its pointers never reach the orbit): shown when the RYTM or ARP hood is open; desktop a panel at the right (min(480 px, 32 vw), draggable by its bar, position kept, double click resets), phone a SCOPE pill under the header that unfolds over the top of the screen (36 px controls). Window 1/16, 1/8, 1/4, 1/2, 1 BAR on the grid (TRIG BEAT): the current window fills over the same place in the previous bars, drawn as ghosts (GHOSTS 0, 1, 3, 7), a thin head shows where it is; nothing playing (or TRIG AUTO): the last rising edge at a quarter of the recent peak, the previous windows as ghosts. VIEW L/R (green, blue), MID, SIDE, XY (goniometer); KICK+ARP draws the kick in orange and the arpeggio in green. GAIN x1 to x16 with 0, -3, -6, -12 dBFS lines; a min/max envelope per pixel column, the samples themselves when there are fewer than 1.5 per pixel; 30 fps on the phone. Readouts (4 per second, on the last full window): window length in ms, BEAT or AUTO, PEAK dBFS, CORR (L against R; kick against ARP), DRIFT in ms: the cross-correlation lag between that window and the same place one bar earlier (first 4096 samples, +/- 5 ms, parabolic refinement), shown only when the two windows correlate at 0.6 or more. Checked muted (?mute=1, master 0, Chromium --mute-audio, no audio device): RYTM at 130 BPM, 1/4 window 461.5 ms, DRIFT +0.006 ms bar to bar, CORR +0.99; KICK+ARP CORR -0.02; ARP in XY CORR +0.30; desktop and phone screenshots; production build passes.
R14-100. View locked while a machine plays (2026-10-04, Mika: "quand le bouton play est lance dans une machine, qu'il ne soit pas possible de la deplacer en 3d a part de cliquer en dehors de la machine ; quand c'est play c'est lock sur la vue la plus adaptee pour utiliser la machine"). state/playLock.ts: machinePlaying (MM-RYTM: its clock runs; MM-ARP: its arpeggio runs; MM-DECKS: a deck plays, once its code is loaded) and onPlayStart (transitions only). scene/renderer.ts: a machine that starts playing comes to the front (focus moves to it, which resets the orbit; already focused: orbit.reset()), an open hood stays open. scene/orbit.ts lock(x, y): a gesture starting over a playing machine (hit.machineAt on the projected hulls) neither turns nor zooms the view (mouse drag, wheel, two fingers); its controls still answer (the dial gate runs first, taps still count); a gesture starting on the background turns and zooms as before. Checked (desktop, muted): background drag turns to az 315, RUN brings it back to 0, a drag and a wheel over the RYTM while it runs leave az 0 / zoom 1, a background drag and wheel still turn (313) and zoom (1.46), after STOP a drag over the machine turns again.
R14-101. Home Screen invitation (2026-10-04, Mika: "Il est important de proposer d'avoir mauditemachine.com en icone sur iPhone.. par defaut"). state/install.ts (imported from main.tsx so beforeinstallprompt is caught before the v4 chunk): standalone detection (navigator.standalone, display-mode), the iOS gesture to describe (Safari: Share; Safari 26 on iPhone: ... then Share; Chrome iOS: Share; Firefox, Edge iOS: the menu; nothing in Instagram, Facebook, TikTok and other in-app browsers, nor before iOS 16.4 outside Safari), beforeinstallprompt kept for an INSTALL button (no browser mini bar), a 30-day snooze (mm.v4.install). ui/InstallPrompt.tsx: a graphite card at the bottom, 4 s after the intro ends: iOS two steps with drawn icons (Share, then Add to Home Screen, the French label when the phone is French; a caret toward Safari's bottom bar up to iOS 18), Chromium a real INSTALL button (ink on orange, 6.8:1). Never once installed, nor over a section, the press kit, an editor, an open hood, the MM-DECKS or an open Dock; on the phone the machine is reframed above it (setInset). Close (44 px) or Escape snoozes it. index.html: manifest link with %BASE_URL%, theme-color #0c0b09; manifest background and theme #0c0b09, categories. Checked by the subagent on iOS 17 (390 x 844 and 390 x 664 in production preview), Safari 26, French, Chrome and Firefox iOS, Instagram (none), Android and desktop with a simulated beforeinstallprompt, standalone (none), reduced motion, light mode.
R14-102. KNOBS pages in the phone Docks (2026-10-04, Mika: "je voudrais m'assurer que ARP ainsi que toutes les autres machines fonctionnent correctement sur mobile ; tous les boutons, beau et design, accessible et pas tout petit ; nous avons la place sur un ecran en entier"). Measured at 390 x 844: the MM-ARP face knobs are 18 px on screen, the MM-RYTM encoders 27 to 31 px, the MM-DECKS keys 19 to 24 px high (their touch boxes are already 48 px). Each Dock gets two pages at the top (SEQUENCER or PLAY, then KNOBS, kept in mm.v4.dock.page / mm.v4.vdock.page). ui/KnobPanel.tsx: tabs per section (MM-ARP: OSC 1, OSC 2, MIX, FILTER, FILTER EG, AMP EG, MOD, FX, ARP, TWEAKS; MM-RYTM: GLOBAL FX, VOICE FX with the ten voices to pick, MASTER with TEMPO, KICK, VOICES), four 64 px knobs per row with their name above and value below (actions.ts dialValueText: SAW, 8', -8 CT, 52 HZ, 216 MS, 58%), an orange arc (from the centre for TONE and STRETCH), detents marked; drag up or right (150 px the full throw), a tap steps a selector, a double tap restores the default, role slider with arrows, Home, End. Same actions and stores as the machine (anyDial, new dialRange, dialSteps, dialReadout, subscribeDials). The KNOBS page has a fixed 392 px height (the tab sits on its edge, all sections fit) and lifts the machine above it (setInset) so its 3D knobs are seen turning. Checked at 390 x 844: a 60 px finger drag moves CUTOFF 50% -> 90%; screenshots of both Docks; production build passes.
R14-103. Crackling under load (2026-10-04, Mika: "Ola le son grish ! je pense que je suis en saturation de CPU, c'est le son qu'il y a quand je suis en low bitrate sur Ableton, et le CPU chauffe"). Measured in Chromium (CDP WebAudio.getRealtimeData, muted): render capacity 6.6% idle, 13% RYTM + ARP, 24% with two decks, five effects, RYTM and ARP, single callbacks up to 51-78%; the MM-ARP worklet alone 21% of a 2.1 GHz Xeon core at 4x oversampling (unchanged by OVERDRIVE). The context ran at latencyHint 'interactive', the smallest buffer of the sound card (128 frames, 2.7 ms on a Mac): any spike crackled. audio/drums.ts: latencyHint 'balanced' (about 10 ms, four times the margin). scene/renderer.ts FRAME_MIN_MS 12: 60 frames per second at most (a 120 Hz screen rendered every rAF while a machine played; 60 Hz screens unchanged). audio/scope.worklet.js: the chunk buffers come back from the main thread after reading ({ back }, transferred) and are reused (pool of 8): no allocation on the audio thread in steady state. ui/Scope.tsx paints at 60 fps at most on desktop (30 on the phone, as before). Checked: the scope still receives 44 032 samples per second at 44.1 kHz.
R14-104. MM-ARP SEMI and FINE take the plain black cap and white mark of the other knobs (2026-10-04, Mika: "les boutons sont d'une autre couleur, j'aimerais que ce soit la meme chose que les autres"): the chrome cap of R14-94 and its second InstancedMesh are gone (voyager/knobs.ts one mesh, isChromeCap removed).
R14-105. MM-DECKS waveforms in thin bars (2026-10-04, Mika: "c'est pas beau, pas forcement les couleurs, peut-etre juste mettre des barres fines"). dj/waveform.ts: the detail waveform is drawn as BARS = 150 bars per window (bar duration = window / 150), anchored to the track time so they scroll with the music without shimmering; the whole track as BARS_OV = 200 bars; each bar fills BAR_FILL = 0.6 of its pitch and takes the peak of its whole pitch (16 samples), which smooths the slice-to-slice grain; box-filtered coverage of the bar body (and of the next bar when the pixel straddles it); below BAR_MIN = 0.55 pitch per pixel (bars thinner than a pixel) the continuous outline returns. Same in 3BAND, RGB and MONO; played part, cues, loop and beat ticks unchanged.
R14-106. PLAY/STOP of the machines on the MM-DECKS mixer (2026-10-04, Mika: "un bouton playstop dans le mixer, bien place, pas trop imposant, et que ca se voie dans mobile"). A round aluminium key (the deck PLAY's, radius 0.36 against 0.5) under the master VU (MIX.play z 4.62; masterVu z1 4.3 -> 3.55), RYTM + ARP in orange above it (labelZ 3.98). actions.ts machinesToggle: if the MM-RYTM or the MM-ARP plays, both stop; otherwise the RYTM starts and the arpeggio joins its grid (F#m without a progression); a SoundCloud track pauses; the decks keep playing. Glows yellow while one plays, pale otherwise (rig syncLights on clock and arp). The play lock keeps the view on the table when a machine starts from it. Keyboard G (dj/keys.ts), twin "Play or stop the MM-RYTM and the MM-ARP together, key G". Phone: on the right mixer view, and in the MIXER Dock (R14-109). Checked muted: twin and G start and stop both, focus stays on the MM-DECKS; desktop and phone screenshots.
R14-107. REMOVE DECK next to ADD DECK (2026-10-04, Mika: "je ne sais toujours pas comment on fait pour supprimer un deck qu'on a rajoute"; the key of R14-96 was not live yet). dj/Twins.tsx: the right-edge zone (.dj-edge) holds + ADD DECK and, once a deck was added, - REMOVE DECK C (or D) under it, names on two lines; desktop on hover, right-aligned against the window edge (the zone is clamped to the viewport; the deck screen leans over its left part in perspective); phone: the end block, which now stays at four decks (DJ_VIEW_UNITS keeps 'add' when a deck can be removed). A playing deck needs two presses (PRESS AGAIN, orange). Checked: desktop 2 -> 3 -> 2 decks from the zone; phone three decks: both in the end block.
R14-108. Header logotype (2026-10-04, Mika: "le logotype en haut dans le header, a droite du logo Maudite Machine, dans la couleur jaune, de la meme taille que le logo"). ui/Header.tsx and MobileHeader.tsx: a span.v4-logotype in the home link, after the wordmark, 12 px (phone 10 px) to its right, the wordmark's height (26 px, phone 23 px), coloured by a CSS mask of public/logo/mauditemachine-logotype-mark.png (the white logotype trimmed, 112 x 156): #FFA600 like the gold wordmark, #434343 in light mode like the ink one.
R14-109. MM-DECKS Dock on the phone (2026-10-04, Mika: "en mobile tout doit etre disponible avec des knobs plus gros ! la plupart des gens qui vont utiliser mauditemachine.com ce sera en mobile"). dj/MixDock.tsx (lazy, mounted on the phone when the MM-DECKS is used): a MIXER tab at the bottom opens the KNOBS sheet (392 px): one tab per channel (1 RYTM, 2 ARP, 3 A, 4 B, then 5 C, 6 D: GAIN, HI, MID, LOW, FILTER, FADER), FX (the seven effects and TIME, six detents), MASTER (the volume and a PLAY / STOP RYTM + ARP button, yellow while they play). Kept: mm.v4.djdock (open), mm.v4.knobtab.dj. Checked at 390 x 844: a 60 px finger drag moves channel 1 HI 0 -> 0.8; MASTER PLAY starts both machines (the 3D key and the channel VUs light), STOP stops them; the ARP KNOBS page still moves CUTOFF 50% -> 90%.
R14-110. Home Screen invitation at every visit (2026-10-04, Mika: "il faut le proposer aux visiteurs a chaque fois"): closing it now lasts the visit only (sessionStorage mm.v4.install, reloads included); the old 30-day snooze in localStorage is removed at load.
R14-111. Search engine work (2026-10-04, Mika: "je veux que tu augmentes le referencement aussi, c'est important"), by a delegated agent, reviewed and merged: the static text of each machine page stays in the DOM after mount (next to #root, visually hidden, links tabindex -1; a router navigation removes it, App.tsx StaticContentGuard), so the 37 track pages and the sections no longer render the same DOM; /radar/ gets a static page (it answered 404), 404.html noindex; track pages with their cover as preview (JPEG via sharp), titles with the album or label, MusicRecording, the Limbos MusicAlbum, EPs as MusicAlbum; descriptions under 160 characters (EN, FR, ES), /shows/ with its next dates; real text on GOODIES, MERCH, STUDIO, MIXTAPES; JSON-LD linked by @id, official profiles in sameAs, booking ContactPoint, ContactPage, CollectionPage; /press/ with Open Graph and JSON-LD; sitemap lastmod per page from git (pages.yml fetch-depth 0, filter blob:none), images, /radar/. scripts/prerender-seo.mjs, scripts/seo-shared.mjs, scripts/generate-sitemap.mjs, index.html, src/data/seo-meta.json, state/sectionRoute.ts (the landing section keeps its static title).
R14-112. Oscilloscope readable (2026-10-04, Mika: "que les lignes soient grossies de 1px pour mieux voir les choses" ; "on a du mal a voir, ca bouge vraiment vite"). ui/Scope.tsx: every trace one CSS pixel thicker (min/max columns drawn as (1 + dpr)-wide stamps, dpr taller; sample paths +dpr; XY dots +dpr). POS (scopeSettings.at, kept): a window shorter than a bar keeps its place in the bar (POS 1, the downbeat, by default; POS 2..N), fills on its turn and holds until the next bar (at 1/16 it changed nine times a second, now once a bar); POS ALL follows the playhead as before; the button only shows in BEAT mode with more than one place. AUTO: a new capture at most every 250 ms (SCOPE_AUTO_MS), the image holds in between, the ghosts are the previous captures (each on its own rising edge) instead of the previous windows. Checked: 1/16 BEAT POS 1 on the RYTM, six screenshots 300 ms apart show the same window, DRIFT a few hundredths of a millisecond.
R14-113. MM-DECKS header lifted (2026-10-04, Mika: "remonte un peu le titre DECK A de 10px, c'est trop colle a l'ecran"): DECK.head.z, DECK.logo.z, DECK.remove.z and MIX.head.z -5.05 / -5.13 / -5.1 -> -5.19 (the title nearly touched the screen bezel at -4.92; the mixer follows so the titles stay aligned). The zoom field prints its window to the tenth under 10 s (2.5S; a pinched 2.49S overflowed onto its - and + keys).
R14-114. MM-SMPL, a fourth machine (2026-10-04, Mika, with a photo of his zoomed deck waveform: "j'aimerais que tu construises une autre machine de travail du sample avec une partie granulaire aussi ; propose-moi un nom et rajoute-la dans la liste des machines ; faire des samples, extraire des parties, changer la tonalite, slicer"). Name MM-SMPL (sample with the vowels dropped, like MM-RYTM). state/focus.ts: MachineId 'smpl', SMPL flag (on for everyone, ?smpl=0 removes it for the tab), MACHINES gets it after the MM-DECKS, ?m=smpl (smpl, sampler, mm-smpl, grain). One 8.4 x 11 block in the MM-DECKS family (its wedge, brushed top, screws, feet, back connectors, knobs, rubber keys and the deck's aluminium PLAY, from dj/body.ts and dj/controls.ts, now exported; dj/silk.ts DjSilk takes a SilkSpec), placed right of the MM-DECKS (smpl/theme.ts smplX, the same 2.6 gap). Top, back to front: header (MM-SMPL, SAMPLER / SLICER / GRANULAR, logotype), a 7.7 x 3.3 screen, a row of nine keys (GRAB A, GRAB B, FILE, REC | SLICES, MODE, REV, LOOP, SAVE), twelve knobs in three rows (SAMPLE: START, END, PITCH, LEVEL; SHAPE: ATTACK, RELEASE, FILTER, SPREAD; GRAIN: POSITION, SIZE, DENSITY, SPRAY) and PLAY on the left, sixteen thicker pads on the right (1 bottom left, like an MPC), lit pale orange when they have a slice, yellow while they sound.
R14-115. MM-SMPL sound. smpl/smpl.worklet.js holds the sample (both channels, its own rate) and plays up to 12 voices (Catmull-Rom Hermite interpolation, PITCH as speed, reverse, ATTACK ramp, RELEASE on letting go, 3 ms fade at the end of a slice, LOOP while held, the same pad chokes its previous voice, oldest stolen) and grain clouds (Hann-windowed grains of SIZE at DENSITY per second around a position, SPRAY of the region, PITCH without changing the duration, random pan up to SPREAD, level normalised by 1/sqrt(overlap)); it also records its input (the site output, after the limiter) for REC. No allocation in steady state. After it: a biquad (FILTER: centre off, low-pass 20 kHz to 200 Hz to the left, high-pass 20 Hz to 4 kHz to the right), LEVEL (squared), into the bus before the limiter. smpl/engine.ts keeps the main-thread copy (mono for the screen and onsets), sends params, receives positions 23 times a second, and stores the last sample in IndexedDB (mm-smpl) so it comes back next visit; 60 s at most. Checked offline (Node): PITCH 0 -> 438 Hz on a 440 Hz sample, +12 -> 875, grain -12 -> 217 Hz, a held loop sustains, a released cloud fades to 0, REC returns its frames; 8 voices and 2 clouds cost 7 % of a 2.1 GHz core.
R14-116. MM-SMPL controls. smpl/actions.ts: GRAB A / GRAB B take the deck's loupe (its loop if it has one, otherwise the detail window centred on the playhead, its zoom; dj/engine.ts DjPlayer.excerpt copies it); FILE opens a file (or drop an audio file on the page while on the MM-SMPL), decoded by the context; REC records the site output until pressed again; SLICES cycles 4, 8, 16 equal parts of the region and AUTO (smpl/slices.ts onsetSlices: 5 ms energy, positive log flux against the previous four frames, at least 6 dB and 3x its mean, 70 ms apart, the 16 strongest; on a synthetic beat it finds all nine hits 5 to 9 ms early); MODE SLICE (a pad plays its slice) or GRAIN (a pad plays a grain cloud at its slice start while held); REV; LOOP (held pads loop); PLAY: the whole region, or the cloud at POSITION; SAVE: the region as a 16-bit WAV at the PITCH (varispeed, reversed with REV), named after the sample. START and END re-slice. The screen (smpl/screen.ts): name and lengths, the settings or the message line (REC in red with its time), the whole sample in thin bars with the region light and the rest pale, START and END yellow, slices orange and numbered, POSITION cyan dashed in GRAIN; playheads are instanced lines (no redraw while playing). Gestures (smpl/gestures.ts): knobs drag (150 px, Shift fine), wheel, double tap; pads on press; keys on press (FILE and SAVE on release, a finished gesture); on the screen, near START or END moves that bound, elsewhere a drag selects a new region and a tap plays the slice under the finger (GRAIN: POSITION follows the finger). Keyboard (smpl/keys.ts): Z X C V / A S D F / Q W E R / 1 2 3 4 = pads 1-16, Space PLAY, M MODE, L LOOP, B REV. Twins (smpl/Twins.tsx): every key, pad and knob.
R14-117. MM-SMPL integration. state/smplload.ts loads its code apart (like the MM-DECKS); scene/renderer.ts attachSmpl, frameOf('smpl'), the overview now reaches its right edge, neighbours peek (the MM-SMPL at the right of the MM-DECKS, the MM-DECKS at the left of the MM-SMPL), visibility, thumbnail, insets; scene/floor.ts a fourth contact shadow (vec4 uniforms); scene/hit.ts kinds smplknob, smplkey, smplpad, smplscreen; ui/Hotspots.tsx routes them to SmplGestures (gesturesOf / holder, the same contract as the MM-DECKS); scene/quad.ts (quadToUnit, shared with the MM-DECKS). Machine lists: MachineNav (phone switch: MM-RYTM, MM-ARP, MM-DECKS, MM-SMPL fit in 385 px), MachineDrawer, the overview names (MM-SMPL, SAMPLER AND GRANULAR). playLock knows when it sounds. No hood (OPEN, the menu's hood items, the scope and the 808 keys skip it). Phone: smpl/Dock.tsx, a SMPL tab; PADS page (ten keys, a status line, sixteen big pads, 1 bottom left) and KNOBS page (SAMPLE, SHAPE, GRAIN, KnobView); KnobView only marks detents up to 16 (PITCH's 49 stay a fine knob). llms.txt names it. Checked muted in Chromium: GRAB A on a synthetic 124 BPM track (8.00 s around the playhead), SLICES, pad 1 lights and plays, GRAIN cloud on Z held, REC 4.12 s of the running MM-RYTM (peak 0.66), a drag on the screen from 30 % to 60 % sets the region (START 0.297), SAVE downloads a WAV; desktop overview with four machines, the MM-SMPL peeking right of the MM-DECKS, phone at 390 x 844 (nav, both Dock pages).
R14-118. Machine drawer thumbnails follow the hoods (2026-10-04, Mika: "j'ai ouvert et ensuite ferme une machine mais dans la colonne de gauche ca affiche comme si c'etait ouvert"). ui/MachineDrawer.tsx: the thumbnails were made once per scene, at the first opening; they are now made again when the drawer opens and a hood (MM-RYTM explode, MM-ARP voyExplode) is in another resting state than when they were made; an opening or closing animation waits for its end. Checked: MM-RYTM hood open, drawer: the board; hood closed, drawer again: the closed machine (new image).
R14-119. MM-RYTM MUTE in both orders (2026-10-04, Mika: "on clique sur une voix et ensuite sur MUTE, ca mute la voix, mais quand on reclique sur une autre voix ca ne la mute pas"). actions.ts: a voice touched just before MUTE (pad, key or Dock, at most 1.5 s, outside the modes) is muted with the mode (LCD 'BD MUTED: TAP VOICES'), and the mode stays on for the next voices; MUTE then a voice still works as before; MUTE again leaves the mode and brings every voice back. MUTE and SOLO are now exclusive: turning one on turns the other off (with SOLO lit, a voice tapped after MUTE used to go solo instead of muting). The phone KNOBS VOICES row picks the voice to tune without muting it in MUTE mode (tuneVoice). Checked muted: real clicks BD, MUTE (BD muted), SD, TOM (muted), BD again (back); SOLO + CP then MUTE: SOLO off, BD and SD mute; a voice touched 1.7 s before MUTE is not taken.
R14-120. MM-DECKS channels 1 and 2 show the machines (2026-10-04, Mika: "quand on joue sur les machines MM et qu'on arrive sur le mixer, on ne voit pas les pistes jouer sur 1 et 2"). The DJ engine (and with it the routing of the MM-RYTM and the MM-ARP into channels 1 and 2) was only made by a gesture on the table; dj/actions.ts djWake, called by the rig's animator as soon as the table is visible and the sound exists. Checked muted: RYTM running, then the MM-DECKS: channel 1 peaks 0.18 to 0.61, its VU lit.
R14-121. The logotype from Mika's vector file (2026-10-04, Logotype.svg, 1891 x 1612, the same drawing as mauditemachine-logotype.png): public/logo/mauditemachine-logotype.svg. The header mark (v4.css .v4-logotype, a mask, 26 px high, phone 23 px) uses it in place of the cropped PNG (mauditemachine-logotype-mark.png removed); the machines' silk ('mark', theme.ts SILK_LOGOS, the MM-RYTM, MM-ARP, MM-DECKS and MM-SMPL plates) draws it, sharp at any size.
R14-122. MM-RYTM GATE under the hood (2026-10-04, Mika: "trop de gate reverb sur le snare et le clap, je veux pouvoir l'activer ou pas dans OPEN"). audio/kit.ts: a TWEAK gate (0 or 1, a two-position switch, OFF by default, kept in mm.v4.kit.1; an older kit or preset reads OFF); it re-renders the snare and the clap; the sample key carries it. audio/shotsdsp.ts: GATE OFF, the MM snare is dry (no 8-line FDN gated room), the MM clap has no small room, the 909 clap no room (the 808 clap is unchanged, its tail is its room); GATE ON: the sounds of before. scene/rytmTweaks.ts: desktop plate in two rows of seven (KICK, TUNE, ATTACK, DECAY, DRIVE and the title over the last two cells; SNARE, SNAPPY, CLAP, GATE, HATS, TOMS, RIM), knobs 1.25 and switches 1.0 (they touched at seven columns); portrait four rows of three with the title at the top (KICK TUNE ATTACK / DECAY DRIVE HATS / SNARE SNAPPY GATE / CLAP TOMS RIM); scene/tweakplate.ts title width (w). Hotspot rk-gate, a tap steps it; LCD 'SNARE + CLAP GATE ON'; twins; phone KNOBS VOICES row. Checked: two real taps ON then OFF; offline (Node) the MM snare's room is -11.4 dB under its dry energy with GATE ON, absent OFF; the clap rooms -19 dB.
R14-123. MM-ARP SIDECHAIN (2026-10-04, Mika: "un knob sidechain automatique dans MM-ARP OPEN, un sidechain qui s'adapte parfaitement au kick et a sa longueur dans MM-RYTM"). voyager/params.ts: TWEAK duck (SIDECHAIN, 0 OFF to 1 = -24 dB at the hit, OFF under 0.02; LCD 'SIDECHAIN -12 DB'). audio/duck.ts: no detector: every kick scheduled by the MM-RYTM (audio/drums.ts trigger, sequencer or pad) brings what it plays: the envelope of its sample (peak per 5 ms, over 20 ms windows, made non-increasing), its playback rate, its voice DECAY (tau) and its velocity times its VOLUME (capped at 1). The gain reduction follows the kick level like a compressor glued to it: depth x 24 dB x ((level dB + 12) / 12)^1.5, nothing once the kick is 12 dB under its peak (the default 909 comes back in 0.38 s at -1 dB, a short DECAY in 0.19 s, the 808 boom later); it starts 2 ms before the hit and reaches the bottom 4 ms after it. Ducker: the curves of the kicks (points every 10 ms), the automation of the MM-ARP output gain (arpOut: dry, ping-pong and its reverb, so the scope and channel 2 see it) rewritten from the first instant touched (cancelScheduledValues, then linear ramps to the lowest of the overlapping curves); a voice cancelled by STOP removes its kick; OFF ramps back to 1 in 30 ms. audio/synth.ts sets the depth from the knob (setArpDuck). voyager/theme.ts: the TWEAKS plate in five columns (PHASE DRIFT WIDTH BASS MONO KEY TRACK / ACCENT SYNC SIDECHAIN and the title over two cells), portrait five rows of two (the title on the last), knobs 1.3; ends OFF and -24 DB. Phone KNOBS TWEAKS tab. Checked muted (master 0, never heard): RYTM at 130 BPM with SIDECHAIN 5: the ARP output gain reaches -11.7 dB on each kick, under -1 dB for 380 ms (DECAY 0.45) or 190 ms (DECAY 0.1), stays down with DECAY 10 (a 1.3 s boom); OFF and STOP: gain 1, no kick left.
R14-124. MM-DECKS FX TO (2026-10-04, Mika: "assigner avec un knob les FX vers une piste, un knob qui selectionne la piste de destination ou alors toutes les pistes"). dj/state.ts fxTo (-1 all, else the channel 0 to 5, kept in mm.v4.dj.2); dj/engine.ts: each channel leaves its fader through two gains, to the effects chain or straight to the master (a dry bus), switched in 20 ms (DjChannel.sendFx, DjMixer.setFxTo); the tails already in the delay and the reverb ring out. dj/actions.ts djSetFxTo, fxTarget (a target beyond the channels on the table reads ALL; REMOVE DECK of the targeted deck goes back to ALL), fxToText (ALL, 1 RYTM, 2 ARP, 3 A...). The table: an eighth knob at the end of the effects row (dj/theme.ts placeMix: eight places), FX TO, a selector with one tick per position (ALL, then each channel) and ALL printed at its first; drag, wheel (one step per 60 px of scroll), arrows (one step), double tap: ALL. The table screen says FX TO 2 ARP when it turns, EFFECTS 2 ARP at rest when one channel is targeted; the targeted channel number turns orange (dj/silk.ts setSilkFxTo, the table silk redrawn on change). Twin (aria-valuetext ALL / 2 ARP); phone MIXER Dock FX tab: FX TO after TIME (ALL then the channels). Checked muted: wheel up twice: fxTo 1, only channel 2 routed to the effects (gains 0 1 0 0), screen FX TO 2 ARP, 2 in orange; up to 4 B, down to ALL (1 1 1 1), a 40 px drag: 1 RYTM.
R14-125. MM-DECKS pitch of the last deck (2026-10-05, Mika: "le PITCH ne fonctionne pas sur DECK B et meme C"). The ADD / REMOVE DECK zone at the right edge (dj/Twins.tsx .dj-edge) was the bounding box of its projected quad: in perspective it reached over the last deck and swallowed its pitch fader. Its left edge is now the rightmost projection of its inner edge (64 px at least, against the window edge), it lets the pointer through except on its two buttons (pointer-events), and on desktop its buttons sit in its upper part (16 % down), away from the pitch fader. Checked: elementFromPoint over the C pitch fader is the scene again; a real drag moves C (0.103), A and B unchanged.
R14-126. MM-DECKS fine pitch (2026-10-05, Mika: "pouvoir le gerer finement, des fois c'est difficile d'atteindre la valeur souhaitee"). dj/gestures.ts: a vernier on the pitch fader: moving sideways away from the slot makes the drag finer (twice at 60 px, five times at 240 px, ten at most), on top of the slow-drag factor and Shift. dj/actions.ts djSetPitch: the pitch lands on the hundredth of a percent (what the screen reads is what plays). dj/screens.ts: the deck screen redraws on every hundredth of a percent (its cache key held the pitch to the percent, so a fine drag showed a stale value) and shows the BPM to the hundredth (124.03).
R14-127. MIXER EQ in dB (2026-10-05, Mika: "le 0, le milieu, a 0 dB ; apres on peut depasser, mais le 0 au milieu"). The law was already centred (dj/math.ts eqDb: -26 dB to +6 dB, kill at the far left, unity in the middle, the isolator bypassed there); now it shows and holds: dj/gestures.ts knobText (GAIN and EQ in dB, 0 DB in the middle, KILL; FILTER LP / OFF / HP; MASTER in dB; effects in %; FX TO its channel), a centre detent while dragging (within 4 % of the middle: exactly 0; not with Shift), float leftovers of wheel and arrow steps snap to 0; ui/KnobPanel.tsx KnobView: a centre detent for every bipolar knob while dragging (2 % of the throw). The table screen says the EQ just turned (2 ARP LOW -12.0 DB); phone readouts and twins in dB.
R14-128. Cursor and view on the machine in use (2026-10-05, Mika: "dans la vue par defaut je ne veux pas bouger la machine en 3D, mais au survol j'ai la main ; je veux le curseur normal ; je veux toujours pouvoir bouger en 3D en dehors de la machine"). scene/orbit.ts lock(x, y, mouse): with a mouse, a drag or a wheel that starts over the focused machine no longer turns or zooms the view (its controls still answer); from the background it does; a playing machine stays locked for every pointer (R14-100); touch unchanged (one finger never turned the view). ui/Hotspots.tsx: over the focused machine outside its controls, the default arrow; over the background the grab hand. Checked: a 200 px drag on the MM-RYTM body leaves az 0, the same drag on the background turns to 330; cursor 'default' over the body, grab outside.
R14-129. MM-DECKS WARM waveforms (2026-10-05, Mika: "des couleurs plus chaudes genre jaune et orange en light et dark mode ; les autres modes j'aime ca mais un truc mieux"). dj/state.ts DJ_WAVES: WARM first and default (stored as waveMode: a stored 3BAND, the old default, becomes WARM; RGB or MONO chosen stay), then 3BAND, RGB, MONO (V and the WAVE key cycle). dj/waveform.ts: the three bands stacked like 3BAND, lows deep orange, mids gold (#FFA600), highs pale yellow, brighter at the core (0.8 to 1), the played part blended toward brown; MONO fallback in gold while the bands compute. The deck screens are black in both themes, one palette fits both.
R14-130. LOOP > SMPL (2026-10-05, Mika: "comment on fait pour exporter une loop dans MM-SMPL ; un bouton Exporter situe sur MIXER vers SMPL, et la je peux editer mon sample"). A key in the mixer header, left of the logotype, its name LOOP > SMPL in orange (like REMOVE DECK on a deck), lit orange while any deck loops; key T; twin; the phone MIXER Dock MASTER tab (under PLAY RYTM + ARP). dj/actions.ts djExportToSmpl: the deck last looped (else another one looping, else the one heard most, else the first loaded) goes to smplGrab (now any deck A to D: its loop, else its zoomed window), the view moves to the MM-SMPL (its code loads if needed), whose screen says what it took. Checked muted: a 124 BPM test track on deck B, LOOP 4 (1.935 s), LOOP > SMPL: focus smpl, sample 1.936 s named after the track.
R14-131. MM-SMPL redrawn like a small Elektron Tonverk in Maudite Machine colours (2026-10-05, Mika: "arrange le MM-SMPL, je veux un petit Elektron Tonverk mais a mes couleurs, avec les fonctionnalites de samples"). smpl/theme.ts: a wide, shallow block (12.4 x 7.6 instead of 8.4 x 11; dj/body.ts wedge takes a depth and keeps the slope and the middle height, so TOP_M holds; the back connectors are moved onto its back face). Top, back to front: the header; the screen top left (6.1 x 2.62); twelve encoders on its right in three rows named in orange at their left like Elektron pages (SAMPLE: START END PITCH LEVEL, SHAPE: ATTACK RELEASE FILTER SPREAD, GRAIN: POSITION SIZE DENSITY SPRAY), names raised so the caps do not hide them; a row of eleven function keys in three groups split by rules (PLAY in gold, STOP | GRAB A, GRAB B, FILE, REC | SLICES, MODE, REV, LOOP, SAVE); sixteen trig keys in one row, 1 to 16 left to right, in groups of four (a mark above each group, numbers under, 1 5 9 13 brighter, the SLICES bracket), pale orange with a slice, gold while sounding. PLAY and STOP are function keys now (keyAction play / stop; the round PLAY key is gone; Space still plays). The screen's waveform: the region in gold, the rest dim gold, START and END in bone, slices orange. Phone Dock: eleven keys in two rows of six, the trigs in reading order (1 to 4 on top). Frame, thumbnail and contact shadow follow the new size. Checked muted: LOOP > SMPL from deck B, 8 slices lit on trigs 1 to 8, a held trig gold, PLAY gold; desktop focus and overview with the four machines; phone 390 x 844.
R14-132. Intro on one machine (2026-10-05, Mika: "a l'intro l'image flick un peu ; j'aimerais aleatoirement qu'une seule machine s'ouvre et se ferme et arrive en 3D zoom pour se mettre dans la vue par defaut"). scene/renderer.ts: the intro picks the machine of ?m= (MM-RYTM or MM-ARP), else one of the two at random; only that one starts open and closes (Explode.assemble / voy.assemble), the other waits closed; focus is on it from the first frame (no more overview during the intro, and no zoom from the overview to the MM-RYTM after it on the phone). The camera arrives in 3D over the intro (introCamera, easeInOutCubic over INTRO.ms): elevation 40 to 69 deg, azimuth from 38 deg on a random side back to 0, zoom 0.62 to 1, set directly (no orbit commit, RESET VIEW stays hidden); the end (time or first gesture) snaps to the default view. A machine without a hood asked by ?m= (dj, smpl): no intro, straight there. Against the flicker: the intro clock advances by INTRO.maxStepMs (50 ms) per frame at most (a slow frame, shaders compiling, slows the intro instead of making it jump), and the MM-DECKS and MM-SMPL that arrive during the intro (code loaded apart, a heavy build and new shaders, and in the overview a wider frame at once) are attached at its end (pendingDj, pendingSmpl). Checked on the production build (dev mode skips the intro: StrictMode builds the scene twice): intro pending, focus mm808 or voy, az 322, el 40, zoom 0.62, no DJ nor SMPL attached; after the first click: done, az 0, el 69, zoom 1, both attached; phone 390 x 844.
R14-133. Header logotype on the left, in yellow (2026-10-05, Mika: "le logotype devrait etre a gauche du logo Maudite Machine et en jaune"). ui/Header.tsx and ui/MobileHeader.tsx: the mark before the wordmark (12 px gap, 10 px on the phone); v4.css: yellow #F2C230 (the site's yellow), #E0A800 in the light theme (it was the wordmark's gold, and grey in light).
R14-134. Machine order RYTM, ARP, SMPL, DECKS (2026-10-05, Mika: "j'aimerais que MM-SMPL soit place a droite de MM-ARP"). state/focus.ts MACHINES: mm808, voy, smpl, dj. smpl/theme.ts smplX: right of the MM-ARP (the same gap as before); dj/theme.ts DJ_LEFT: right of the MM-SMPL when it exists (else of the MM-ARP). scene/renderer.ts: frameOf, the overview right edge (the MM-DECKS, else the MM-SMPL), neighbours that peek (ARP: RYTM left, SMPL right; SMPL: ARP left, DECKS right; DECKS: SMPL left) and setShown generic on the MACHINES order (a neighbour is the next index on either side). The machine drawer and the phone machine bar follow. Checked: centres RYTM 0, ARP 16.2, SMPL 31.3, DECKS 50.5; focus ARP shows the RYTM and the SMPL peeking.
R14-135. Machine selector at the top right (2026-10-05, Mika: "voir les machines en haut a droite pour les selectionner rapidement"). ui/Header.tsx HeaderMachines (desktop header, next to MENU): ALL then RYTM, ARP, SMPL, DECKS in the MACHINES order, aria-pressed on the focused one (bone pill, ink text), a click calls focusMachine; v4.css .v4-header-end, .v4-hmachines (a rounded group with a hairline border on a translucent ground, light theme variant), .v4-hmachines-btn (ALL dimmer, hover, focus-visible). The phone keeps its machine bar.
R14-136. Faster travel between machines (2026-10-05, Mika: "le voyage vers les machines doit etre rapide"). scene/renderer.ts FOCUS_MS 900 -> 450 ms and easeOutCubic (it starts at full speed and settles, instead of easing in); reduced motion still jumps.
R14-137. MM-ARP CHORD (2026-10-05, Mika: "je voudrais vraiment que ARP sonne mieux, je trouve les arpeges un peu grossiers, y a pas plus de notes qu'on peut mettre, ce sont les arpeges normaux ?"). They were: each chord a triad from its own root, so D and E jumped a sixth above F#m and the line climbed and fell with the progression, three notes an octave. voyager/params.ts: a TWEAK chord, a five-position selector BASIC, TRIAD, 7TH, 9TH, 11TH, 7TH by default (a stored state without it gets 7TH; a preset saved before it gets BASIC, state/presets.ts, so it plays as it did). voyager/chords.ts voicedDegrees: BASIC is the old arpeggio note for note; the others place every chord in the same octave window (F#3 to F4: common tones stay, the others move by a step at most, a keyboard player's voice leading), repeated per RANGE octave, 7TH adds the diatonic seventh (F#m7, Dmaj7, E7, C#m7, Bm7, Amaj7), 9TH and 11TH stack their extensions above the top note (a scale run of seconds in the upper octave sounded like an exercise); clashing extensions are avoided: the flat ninth of C#m becomes its eleventh, the natural eleventh of the major chords (A, E) their thirteenth (D gets its sharp eleventh). voyager/seq.ts: degrees now go from SEQ_MIN -7 (an octave under the root, the voiced D and E sit under their root) to SEQ_TOP 24 (the eleventh on top of three octaves); AUTO uses CHORD (extensions beyond 24 drop); EDIT suites stored before stay valid. ui/SeqLane.tsx: the lane spans -7 to 24, the guides mark the CHORD tones, extensions included. voyager/arp.ts: with DRIFT, each note a little more or less pressed (+-4 % at 5). TWEAKS plate: CHORD next to SIDECHAIN, the title on one cell (desktop row 2: ACCENT SYNC SIDECHAIN CHORD TWEAKS; portrait last row CHORD and the title), 7TH in orange; LCD CHORD 7TH; phone KNOBS TWEAKS tab. Checked (no sound): D with 7TH over 2 OCT plays F#3 A3 C#4 D4 F#4 A4 C#5 D5, BASIC 0 2 4 7 9 11; E 9TH G#3 B3 D4 E4 G#4 B4 D5 E5 F#5; the lane draws them under and over the root; plate screenshot.
R14-138. MM-SMPL knob hierarchy (2026-10-05, Mika: "MM-SMPL je voudrais que ce soit plus design, surement des knobs plus gros que d'autres"). smpl/theme.ts: the twelve knobs in three columns, the pages SAMPLE, SHAPE, GRAIN (SMPL_PAGES), each under an orange-named bracket with its ticks down (dj/silk.ts Bracket down, ink); at the head of each column a big knob, the one turned most (PITCH, FILTER, POSITION: 1.45 wide, 1.12 high, graduated 0 to 10 with 0, 5 and 10 longer), PITCH and POSITION with an aluminium cap and a black mark, FILTER with the orange cap of the mixer FILTER knobs; under it three small black knobs (0.62; START END LEVEL, ATTACK RELEASE SPREAD, SIZE DENSITY SPRAY) with their end ticks and a hit area 0.07 wider than their cap. smpl/rig.ts: three InstancedMesh by cap (black, aluminium, orange), per-knob scale, names placed from each knob's size and height so the caps do not hide them; dj/controls.ts knobGeometry takes a mark tone. The phone KNOBS pages keep their order. Checked: dark and light theme screenshots, phone 390 x 844, hotspots on every knob.
R14-139. Left and right arrows travel between machines (2026-10-05, Mika: "naviguer entre les machines avec les fleches gauche droite"). hooks/useKeys.ts: ArrowLeft / ArrowRight call actions.ts stepMachine (-1 / +1) in the header order (ALL, RYTM, ARP, SMPL, DECKS; ALL only on desktop), stopping at the ends; not when a control owns the arrows (slider, tab, radio, option, menu item, field), not with a section open, and the preset mode keeps its arrows.
R14-140. ADD DECK only from the mixer (2026-10-05, Mika: "quand on pose la souris sur le bord du deck B pour voir ce qu'il y a a droite, c'est trop fragile, on clique sans faire expres pour ajouter un deck"). The right-edge zone (dj/Twins.tsx .dj-edge, its CSS) and the phone end block ('add' in DJ_VIEW_UNITS) are gone. dj/layout.ts: an adddeck key in the mixer header, left of LOOP > SMPL (MIX.add, dx 4.2 from the right edge), while a deck can be added (none at four decks); its name ADD DECK in orange takes the place of the N CHANNEL DJ MIXER subtitle (as REMOVE DECK does on a deck); dj/gestures.ts djAddDeck; twin. Phone MIXER Dock MASTER tab: ADD DECK and REMOVE DECK C / D (PRESS AGAIN while it plays). REMOVE stays on the last deck's header. Checked: 2 -> 3 -> 4 decks from the key, then the key leaves.
R14-141. CUE and PLAY in a club-player style (2026-10-05, Mika: "les boutons CUE et PLAY font un peu trop jouets"). dj/controls.ts roundGeometry: no more bulging orange and aluminium discs; a low flat cap of dark rubber (DJ_ROUND h 0.16 -> 0.1, cap radius 0.8), a slit, then a thin LED ring (0.86 to 1, 0.045 high) that lights: orange on CUE (loaded and paused, or held), yellow on PLAY (playing; pale when loaded), the mixer PLAY/STOP too; the ring's emissive is 2.2 times a key top (DJ_ROUND.glow). labelTexture: CUE printed small and spaced in orange, the play / pause glyph small in bone. Same in light theme (dark caps on the cream body).
R14-142. MM-ARP knobs a bit bigger on desktop (2026-10-05, Mika: "j'aimerais que les knobs soient un peu plus gros en desktop"). voyager/theme.ts SIZE_SCALE desktop: xl 1.5 -> 1.6, l 1.18 -> 1.28, m 1 -> 1.1, s 0.8 -> 0.9, sw 0.62 -> 0.68, sel 0.85 -> 0.92 (the portrait sizes do not change); labels and scales follow; checked on a 1800 x 1100 screenshot, no collision.
R14-143. Mika's samples in the MM-RYTM sound selector (2026-10-05, Mika: "je voudrais que tu mettes ces samples dans le selecteur de samples, pour BD et SD", six kicks and four snares from his library). Any audio file in public/samples/rytm/<family>/ (bd, sd, cp, hh, tom, rs) becomes one more position of its family's sound switch, after 909, 808 and MM, in file name order. vite.config.ts: a plugin lists them as virtual:rytm-samples at each build and, in dev, at each file added or removed (no script; any file name: a # or spaces, which an import cannot carry, public/ serves them as they are). audio/samples.ts: the list, a short name (01 AT BluePrint - Kick F.wav: BLUEPRINT F; Psy_Snares02.wav: PSY 02), download and decode once chosen (OfflineAudioContext at 48 kHz). audio/sampledsp.ts (pure): the file read at the render rate (Hermite), with the family's TWEAKS: KICK TUNE -12 to +12 semitones (one per detent of 1/24), ATTACK (5: the file; above, up to +6 dB over its first 4 ms; below, a rise up to 6 ms), DECAY (4.5 and above: its length; below, an earlier cosine fade, down to 12 %), DRIVE (2.5 and below: clean; above, tanh); SNARE SNAPPY (a tilt around 2 kHz). shotsdsp.ts renderSampleShot: STRETCH like the other sounds and its peak set like its voice's (BD 1.5 dB under, as the 909). shots.ts: a sample shot is rendered on the main thread once its file is in (the worker does not have it), one variant only; before that the voice plays the nearest computed sound, then the right one. audio/kit.ts: Kit.sample (the chosen file per family, kept in mm.v4.kit.1; a file gone from the folder: the model again), kitSteps 3 + files, kit.sound / setSound, readouts KICK BLUEPRINT F, KICK TUNE +3 ST, KICK DECAY 51 %. Presets keep each family's sound by name (sounds); an older preset reads its value on three positions. The plate prints 909 808 MM then 1 2 3... (scene/rytmTweaks.ts kitStepLabels). The files are not in the repository yet: they are on Mika's Mac (see the session report). Checked with ten synthetic files named like Mika's, removed before the commit: G# loads (%23), 9 positions for the kick, 7 for the snare, TUNE +6 ST shortens 0.5 s to 0.354 s, DECAY 0.2 to 0.181 s, peak -4 dB.
R14-144. Selectors of five positions and more turn 240 deg (2026-10-05): voyager/theme.ts switchThrowDeg 2: 90, 3 to 4: 150, 5 and more: 240 (CHORD and the sound selectors with samples): their names no longer touch.
R14-145. MIDI for every machine (2026-10-05, Mika: "toutes les actions, tous les parametres de toutes les machines assignables en midi ; j'ai un Roto-Control, je voudrais selectionner un preset de mon Roto-Control, choisir la machine que je veux et avoir les assignations"). midi/targets.ts: every assignable target with a stable id: MM-RYTM (rytm:enc:<encoder>, rytm:voice:<voice>:<param> for a voice without selecting it, rytm:kit:<tweak>, pads, 16 steps, RUN/STOP, CLEAR, RANDOM, MUTE, SOLO, EDIT, OPEN), MM-ARP (every knob and tweak, the 8 chord pads, RUN/STOP, CLEAR, RANDOM, EDIT, OPEN), everywhere (go to ALL or a machine, previous, next, PLAY/STOP RYTM + ARP); MM-DECKS (dj/midi.ts: every knob, fader and key of the decks in place, keys held like the keyboard) and MM-SMPL (smpl/midi.ts: 12 knobs, 11 keys, 16 trigs held) register when their code loads (state/djload.ts, smplload.ts), an assignment to them loads it. midi/midi.ts: Web MIDI (Chrome, Edge, Opera, Firefox), asked at the first CONNECT then reconnected at each visit; CC, notes and pitch bend per channel; MIDI LEARN: touch a control on the machine (ui/Hotspots.tsx: in learn mode the control does not play, it waits) or pick it in the panel list, then move the controller; assignments are kept per machine (the machine of the target): a message goes first to the machine in view, then to the global ones, then to another machine that has it (one Roto-Control preset per machine with its own CCs works without changing view; the same CCs on every machine work once the machine is chosen). A value follows 0 to 127 over its course (its detents if any; a note toggles it); an action fires on press (a note, or a CC rising above 63), held targets release with it. Feedback for motorised knobs: every assigned value is sent back when it changes (mouse, preset, RANDOM) and all of them when the machine changes, only to the outputs of the devices used to learn (never to a synth next to it), never within 300 ms of a move on that knob. ui/MidiPanel.tsx: a MIDI button in the desktop header (yellow dot when connected, orange blinking in learn), its panel (status and inputs, CONNECT / DISCONNECT, MIDI LEARN, the control list of the machine in view and the global actions, what is waiting, the last message, the assignments of the machine in view then everywhere then the others, each removable, CLEAR per machine, SEND VALUES BACK, EXPORT and IMPORT as JSON); in learn mode each assigned control in view carries its message (CC21) and the one waiting blinks in an orange frame (MidiLearnLayer, over the hit layer). Kept: mm.v4.midi.1. dj/names.ts: the MM-DECKS control names, shared by its twins and the MIDI panel; scene/hit.ts views carry dj and smpl. Checked in Chromium with a fake Roto-Control (and a fake minilogue xd output): learn by clicking CUTOFF then CC 21; CC 21 at 127, 0, 90: CUTOFF 1, 0, 0.709; no echo while turning; the mouse moves CUTOFF to 0.25: CC 21 = 32 sent to the Roto-Control only; note 36 learned on NEXT MACHINE: MM-ARP to MM-SMPL; CC 21 learned again on the MM-SMPL PITCH: it sets PITCH there and CUTOFF on the MM-ARP, and the knob receives 64 then 127 when the view changes; 74 MM-DECKS targets, CC 30 on channel 1 HI: 0 then 1.
R14-146. Header logotype bigger (2026-10-05, Mika: "le logotype plus gros de 20 px, en light et en dark ; en light, gris, un peu plus clair que le logo normal"). v4.css: the mark 26 -> 46 px high on desktop, 23 -> 43 px on the phone (the wordmark does not change, the home link 48 px high); light theme #737373 (the ink wordmark is #434343), dark theme still #F2C230.
R14-147. MM-RYTM voices at the same loudness (2026-10-05, Mika: "il y a des voices vraiment plus fortes que d'autres alors que le knob volume est pareil"). The peak normalisation (SHOT_PEAK) left 11 dB between voices at the same VOLUME (K-weighted 100 ms loudness: TOM -1.1, HT -1.4, SD -2.9, PC -3.4 against BD -7.5, CP -12.5, CH -12.4). audio/shotsdsp.ts: every hit (computed sounds, 909, 808, MM, and Mika's samples) is set to the loudness of its voice (SHOT_LOUD, the loudest 100 ms window of the BS.1770 K-weighted energy, the filters designed for the render rate): BD -7.5, SD -7.5, TOM -9, HT -9.5, CP -9.5, PC -10, RS -10.5, CH -12, CHopen and OH -11.5, CY -12.5 (a drum balance, hats and cymbal a little under), its peak at +6 dB at most; SHOT_PEAK is gone. Checked offline (Node): every voice of every model lands on its target, peaks from -7.6 to +2.4 dB.
R14-148. Less aliasing (2026-10-05, Mika: "des petits glitchs graphiques genre aliasing quand on start et que ca fait la jolie 3D"). theme.ts DPR_MIN_DESKTOP 1.5: on a 1 dppx screen the desktop renders at 1.5 (supersampled, then reduced by the browser), the knob flutes, rules and edges stop crawling while the camera moves; ORBIT.near 0.1 -> 1: from far (the intro, the overview) the depth buffer was too coarse for the silk, labels and LEDs laid a few thousandths above their surface, which flickered (the camera never comes closer than 8).
R14-149. How to use each machine, in the overview (2026-10-05, Mika: "quand on hover sur une machine avant de cliquer, une description de comment l'utiliser, en vraiment court"). state/overviewHover.ts (ui/Hotspots.tsx sets the machine under the mouse while the view is ALL), ui/OverviewHelp.tsx: a card under the machine's name (above if it would leave the screen), following the view: its name, its kind, two short paragraphs (MACHINE_HELP) and CLICK TO PLAY IT; gone when a machine is chosen or the mouse leaves; not on touch screens.
R14-150. MM-RYTM square steps, beat frames, velocity by dragging (2026-10-05, Mika, a photo of the Digitakt II: "je voudrais que les steps soient carres finalement ; on remarque que les steps ont des strokes 1 5 9 13 ; quand je clique et que je reste appuye, je peux changer la velocite"). theme.ts KEYS: 0.5 x 0.9 -> 0.6 x 0.6 (portrait 0.7 x 0.7), radius 0.05, the row 0.09 higher, the numbers and brackets follow. scene/sequencer3d.ts: a frame printed on top of keys 1, 5, 9 and 13 (a rounded square outline in the silk ink, 0.62 opacity), going down with its key. ui/Hotspots.tsx: a drag that starts on a step sets its velocity (up louder, one level every 12 px with a mouse, 16 px with a finger; an empty step starts from MID), never orbits; the screen reads STEP 03 SD HIGH; releasing keeps it; held still for 400 ms, the screen says its velocity and DRAG (the long press used to clear the step: a tap still cycles HIGH, MID, LOW, OFF). actions.ts stepVelocity, stepVelocityOf, stepHoldHint. Twins: up and down arrows change a step's velocity. Checked: an empty step dragged up 40 px: 9, then 40 px under its start: 3, kept on release; a tap on a HIGH step: MID.
R14-151. MM-SMPL redesign: balanced knobs, rubber LED keys, a slice sequence (RANDOM, CLEAR, EDIT), no GRAB (2026-10-05, Mika, a photo of the machine drawer and one of the Digitakt II: "les trois boutons sont vraiment trop gros et les autres trop petits ! travaille sur quelque chose d'ergonomique et attention au mobile je veux que ce soit parfait ; fais un gros effort pour MM-SMPL, c'est bof le design ; les boutons poussoirs genre caoutchouc avec une LED interieure, la diffusion doit etre de cette maniere ; il manque random, edit, clear ; GRAB je ne veux pas, c'est moi qui envoie quelque chose a la machine"). smpl/theme.ts: the screen in the middle of the top row (x -1.62, 5.0 x 2.2); left of it LEVEL and PITCH (aluminium caps, scale 1.1, graduated 0 to 10); right of it ten knobs of one size (0.92) in two rows, SAMPLE (START, END, ATTACK, RELEASE, FILTER in orange) and GRAIN (POSITION, SIZE, DENSITY, SPRAY, SPREAD), their page names in orange above and below the grid; twelve function keys REC, PLAY, STOP | FILE, SLICES, MODE, REV, LOOP | RANDOM, CLEAR, EDIT, SAVE (GRAB A and GRAB B removed: the mixer's LOOP > SMPL still sends a deck's loop); sixteen trigs in two rows of eight (1.08 x 0.8, a gap in the middle of each row; 1, 5, 9, 13 printed brighter). scene/materials.ts withRubberLed: the keys and trigs are lit like a LED under the rubber (brightest in the middle of the top, spreading to the edges, the sides barely; where the LED shines the rubber takes its colour, so it reads on the light rubber too; Light mode pushes it 1.6x). smpl/seq.ts: a sixteen step sequence, each step empty or a slice, kept in mm.v4.smpl.seq.1; scheduled 100 ms ahead on the audio clock (the voyager/arp.ts technique) on the MM-RYTM grid when it runs, else the MM-ARP grid, else its own at the pattern tempo, with the kit's SWING; each step plays its slice until the next step (one voice: the next cuts it at its own time, smpl.worklet.js `at`), in GRAIN a cloud at the slice start for the length of the step (`at`, `dur`). PLAY runs the sequence when it has steps (else the region, as before); RANDOM writes a musical one (step 1 on slice 1, beats likely, steps tend to their own slice, some repeats) and starts it; CLEAR empties it (it keeps running); EDIT turns the trigs into the steps: tap places or clears (an empty step takes the slice of its rank in the current slicing), drag up or down picks its slice (12 px per slice with a mouse, 16 with a finger; heard while stopped), orange a full step, gold the playhead; out of EDIT the slice being played lights like a struck pad. The screen shows the steps under the waveform (sixteen cells, the slice number in orange, a gold bar under the step that plays). Keyboard, MIDI and twins place steps in EDIT (twins: up and down change the slice). Phone Dock (smpl/Dock.tsx): one row of tabs PADS | SAMPLE | GRAIN (no second level), the twelve keys in two rows of six, a small waveform (region, slices, playheads: the machine's screen is too small on a phone), the status line, the sixteen trigs; in EDIT the trigs are the steps with their slice number, tap and drag as on the machine. Checked (sound muted): EDIT, taps on 1 5 9 13 16 with eight slices: 1 5 1 5 8; step 3 dragged up 40 px: slice 6; PLAY runs (the playhead moves over the steps), STOP, RANDOM, CLEAR; the same on a phone with a finger.
R14-152. MM-RYTM pixel screen (2026-10-05, Mika: "l'ecran de RYTM dans le design de pixel comme la Digitakt 2, mais attention de ne pas copier, quelque chose de bien plus simple ; des fois des petites animations ; l'ecran est important dans RYTM, on devrait avoir plus d'informations ; le meilleur de toutes les machines : Digitakt 2, Analog Rytm, EMX1, Impulse"). scene/pixels.ts: a 160 x 60 dot image with three levels (off, half, full), two bitmap fonts drawn here (5 x 7, 3 x 5, and the 5 x 7 doubled), blown up 4x into the 640 x 240 texture (each dot a square, its last row and column at 82 %: an OLED raster up close). scene/screen.ts, top to bottom: the section (or MM-RYTM), the pattern slot in negative (A01, it blinks when it changes), four beat dots (the beat lit while running), the tempo; a dotted rule; the chosen voice in big letters (it slides in when picked) and its sound (909, 808, MM or the sample; MUTE when muted), its sixteen steps with the hit height as velocity and the playhead underlined (no voice: the loudest hit of each step, dimmed); ten voice meters that jump on each hit and fall back (half-life 120 ms, Impulse style); the two bottom lines from state/lcd.ts (READY, RUN, the scrolling title, the PRESETS tag; the passing message, a knob value, or the track time with its clickable bar: bar keeps its texture coordinates for seekAt). EDIT: the pattern in big letters, the sixteen slots (the current one in negative, the chain framed, the one waiting for the bar blinking, an underline under the chain position), the chain and a hint on the bottom lines. MIX: five level bars, the voice being set in negative, VOLUME SD 80 under them. Presets mode: the name between its arrows, four keys at the bottom. Redrawn when a store changes (lcd, pattern, patterns, editor, voices, clock, playhead), at most every 60 ms; short animations at 50 ms; on a desktop, when nothing has happened for 15 s on the MM-RYTM, a slow wave runs over the meters for 7 s. The twin keeps reading the three text lines.
R14-153. MM-RYTM patterns and chains, EDIT on the machine (2026-10-05, Mika: "pour les boutons EDIT, j'aimerais que le contenu d'EDIT s'ouvre a l'interieur de la machine, pas en dessous, dans la partie des steps ; pour la RYTM on peut placer des patterns et d'ailleurs on peut rajouter des sequences comme le fait la Elektron Digitakt"). state/patterns.ts: sixteen slots A01 to A16, each a whole pattern (ten voices and their velocities; tempo, effects and kit stay), kept in mm.v4.patterns.1; the playing pattern is the current slot's, every edit (steps, RANDOM, CLEAR, a preset) is kept in it. EDIT no longer opens the grid under the machine on a desktop: the sixteen steps become the slots (LED bars: the current one three yellow bars, the chain two orange, a filled slot one, the one waiting for the bar in hover grey). A tap chooses the pattern (stopped: at once; running: at the end of the bar, NEXT A03); more taps within 2 s add to a chain (A01 > A03 > A02, one bar each, looping, Digitakt chain mode); holding an empty slot copies the current pattern into it. audio/clock.ts onBar: called just before the first step of each bar is scheduled, so the next pattern of the chain is in place before its hits are scheduled (first bar of a run: the chain starts from its first pattern). Twins describe the slots; MIDI targets PATTERN A01 to A16. On a phone the panel stays (everything is under the machine there), the sixteen slots on top of the grid. Checked (sound muted): A01 held on A02 copies, quick taps on 1 and 2 while running: the current pattern alternates A02, A01, A02 at each bar.

MM-ARP note screen in the machine (2026-10-05, same request: "dans un design sur la machine ou je peux mettre des notes"). voyager/seqscreen.ts: on a desktop (landscape layout), EDIT puts the eight chord pads away (hidden, their halos off, their hotspots off) and raises a screen in their place (a black bezel, a canvas glass, 240 ms, from under the deck), as wide as the pads, from under the ARPEGGIATOR bracket to the deck edge: the eight chords on top (the one read in negative, the playing one underlined in yellow, those in the progression with an orange dot; tapping one picks the chord whose notes are read, tapping it again follows the playing chord), AUTO and EDIT, STEPS - n +; one column per step, a bar whose height is its note (an octave under the root to three above), chord tones in gold, others in bone, guide lines on the chord tones (roots stronger), the playing column lit, dragging draws the notes (steps crossed follow the line, as in ui/SeqLane.tsx); the note names at the bottom (a tap makes a rest, or brings the note back). ui/Hotspots.tsx: a 'vseq' zone; the touched point is mapped onto the glass through its projected quad (scene/quad.ts), a drag follows its pointer and never orbits. The panel under the machine (ui/SeqLane.tsx SeqPanel) stays on a phone and in the portrait layout only. Checked: a drag from step 1 to 6 draws 19 13 11 6 3 -2; a tap on the second name: a rest; STEPS -: 7; the D tab: its notes read; EDIT again: the screen goes down, the pads are back.
R14-155. Roto-Control set up in full, automatically (2026-10-05, Mika: "je voudrais que ce soit automatiquement parametre en entier sur le Roto-Control ; les machines sur le Roto devraient s'appeler RYTM ARP DECK MIXER SMPL ; un knob ne fait pas 0 a 127 avec le Roto"). midi/roto.ts: five ROTO-SETUP files (JSON, File > Import), one per machine, named RYTM, ARP, DECK, MIXER, SMPL, for setups 11 to 15 (Mika's 1 to 10 untouched); knobs on MIDI channels 1 to 5, buttons on channels 9 to 13; 32 knobs and 32 buttons each, CC 14 to 31 then 102 onwards; every control named (12 characters at most), coloured by role (orange the hero, gold and yellow the tone, cyan the time, red the transport...), 0 to 127; stepped parameters (waves, modes, ranges, FX TO) get the haptic detents and their step names; toggles latch. midi/midi.ts: with the Roto table on (the default, saved), an unassigned message on those channels reaches its parameter, and the motorised knobs follow every change made on the site (feedback to any output whose name contains "roto"). ui/MidiPanel.tsx: a ROTO-CONTROL section: the table on or off, DOWNLOAD THE 5 SETUPS (one zip) or one file per machine, and the four steps (update ROTO-SETUP and Export All first). R14-163. MM-RYTM screen, finer and full (2026-10-05, Mika: "arrange l'ecran de RYTM, fais les choses plus fines, meilleur graphisme et occupe tout l'ecran, la il y a de l'espace en dessous"). scene/pixels.ts: the layout still counts in units of 160 x 60, but the image has two points per unit (320 x 120), each point three texels (canvas 960 x 360, PIX_CANVAS; the rest of the site keeps the 640 x 240 texture space, PIX_TEX 4 per unit, so the seek bar and the presets keys did not move). The fonts keep their size and design, drawn with a stroke half as thick: each lit point of the 5 x 7 (or 3 x 5) glyph becomes a node every two points, neighbours joined by a one-point line, diagonals only where no corner exists (thin(); the big font: nodes every four points, two-point lines); M, N, W, S of the small font are hand-drawn at 5 x 9 (MINI_FINE). Frames and rules are one point. scene/screen.ts: two-unit margins; a play triangle (a square when stopped) before the four beat boxes; the sixteen steps in three segments (soft, medium, strong; half tone when no voice is chosen), a dot over each downbeat; the ten meters in six one-point segments, the chosen voice's name in negative, a muted voice's name struck; the bottom line is never empty: the message, the track and its bar, EDIT help, else three gauges (the chosen voice: VOL, TONE, DECAY; none: SWING, STRETCH, MASTER) with their value, re-drawn when voiceFx or the mix change. EDIT help shortened to fit (TAP: PLAY  TAP TAP: CHAIN).
R14-164. Your samples in the MM-RYTM (2026-10-05, Mika: "je ne peux toujours pas selectionner les samples que je t'ai donnes, les kicks, ou explique-moi comment aller les chercher"; the files were only paths on his Mac). audio/usersamples.ts: audio files picked on the device stay in the browser (the list in localStorage mm.v4.usersamples.1, read at once so the sound selector knows its steps from the start; the bytes in IndexedDB mm-v4-rytm-samples, read on demand; in memory for the visit when IndexedDB is refused); checked by decoding, 20 MB and 32 per family at most, nothing uploaded. audio/samples.ts: samplesOf = the site's samples then yours (key my:<family>/<id>), loadSample reads yours from IndexedDB. audio/kit.ts: when your samples change, the steps follow (a voice that played a removed sample goes back to its model); kitStepLabels names the first, the last and one in five past nine. scene/rytmTweaks.ts: the TWEAKS sound selectors relabel and redraw (TweakPlate items' steps). midi/targets.ts: the kit targets' steps are read live. ui/RytmSamples.tsx: with the MM-RYTM hood open, a SAMPLES panel on the left (the SCOPE is on the right; on the phone a SAMPLES pill at the top left): the voice (KICK, SNARE, CLAP, HATS, TOMS, RIM), its sounds (909, 808, MM, the site's, yours with a remove x), + ADD <VOICE> FILES (the voice plays the first one; the screen says 2 KICKS ADDED); on desktop, an audio file dropped on the machine goes to the pad under the pointer (else the chosen voice, else the kick). Light theme variant.
R14-165. Live Roto-Control map (2026-10-05, Mika: "comment je fais pour mapper tout le Roto-Control ? j'ai ROTO-SETUP 3.3.0, j'aimerais que tout soit mappe de la meilleure maniere possible et la plus efficace pour le jeu en live, je veux controler toutes les machines"). The import format was checked in the ROTO-SETUP 3.3.0 code (File > Import, one file, onto the setup chosen with SEL, the setup takes the file's name field; every field written). midi/roto.ts: six setups, a page of eight knobs and the eight buttons under them going together: RYTM (global FX + RUN RANDOM CLEAR EDIT OPEN RYTM+ARP PREV NEXT; the chosen voice's FX + voice select; the voice volumes + their mutes; CY PC and the kit + their mutes, select and patterns A01 to A04), ARP (the arpeggiator + transport; the filter + the eight chords; envelopes + OSC ON; mod and effects), DECK (deck A, deck B, the table's effects, the RYTM and ARP channels), MIXER (faders and filters + what plays; RYTM/ARP EQ + RYTM mutes; decks EQ + SYNC, LOOP 4, ADD DECK, navigation; effects + FX TIME), SMPL, and LIVE (setup 16, channel 6, buttons 14: the four faders and filters with RUN RYTM, RUN ARP, PLAY and CUE A/B, RYTM+ARP, LOOP > SMPL; SWING, STRETCH, RYTM FX and the kick with the RYTM mutes; the ARP filter, gate, rate, sends with the eight chords; the table's effects with FX TIME, SMPL PLAY/STOP, PREV/NEXT). A state (RUN, a mute, OSC ON) is a TOGGLE button whose LED follows the site; a bipolar knob (EQ, FILTER, GAIN, PITCH, TONE, STRETCH, TUNE) gets a centre indent (hapticIndent1 64); KICK SOUND and SNARE SOUND are step knobs named after their sounds (yours included) when there are 16 at most, the files being built at download (rotoSetups).
R14-166. MIDI states and FOLLOW (2026-10-05, for the live map). midi/targets.ts: rytm:voice:<voice>:mute (1 mutes, 0 brings back, without the MUTE mode: actions.ts voiceMute), rytm:running and voy:running (0 or 1). midi/midi.ts: CC 64 is the exact neutral (0.5) of a value; an echo of a value just sent to the motor knobs (same value within 250 ms) is neither played nor followed; FOLLOW (on by default, saved): a control of a machine setup (its map, nothing learned) shows its machine (RYTM, ARP, SMPL; DECK and MIXER: the MM-DECKS; LIVE: none). ui/MidiPanel.tsx: six setups to download (their setup number), FOLLOW, the import steps for ROTO-SETUP 3.3.0 (accept its firmware update, MIDI mode, SEL then File > Import).
