The note editor back on the MM-RYTM (2026-10-05, Mika: "avant le Edit affichait un ecran en bas ou je pouvais ecrire les notes, voir les velocites, le faire a la souris ; je voulais le voir a la place des Steps du bas ; je veux garder aussi l'enchainement de pattern et changement de pattern la-dedans"). ui/BeatEditor.tsx, desk variant: the PTN row (the sixteen pattern slots: tap plays, tap tap chains, holding an empty one copies), the ten voice rows and the VEL lane drawn with the mouse; useOnMachine lays the panel on the machine from the step-1 and step-16 hotspot rects (the grid exactly over the steps, rows 10 to 17 px from the room left), following onView, onIdle and resize. ui/editorPanel.ts: no inset on desktop (the machine stays), still on the phone.
R14-171. MM-SMPL INFO and its user guide (2026-10-05, Mika: "un tout petit bouton INFO ; on tombe sur un PDF quand meme complet, avec des captures d'ecran aussi, et en FR"). ui/SmplInfo.tsx: an INFO key (a round i) on the MM-SMPL head, left of the logo, opening public/docs/MM-SMPL-mode-emploi.pdf in a new tab. scripts/smpl-manual (npm run manual:smpl): the site in dev (Vite), headless Chromium with the sound cut, a two-bar loop computed in memory (loop.mjs; nothing recorded or published), the machine set through its modules without playing anything, captures on desktop (1440 x 900 x 2) and on a 390 x 844 x 3 phone cropped and encoded by sharp, numbered badges placed from the hotspot rects; content.mjs: fourteen A4 pages in French (Inter and Space Mono, OFL, embedded) printed by page.pdf.
R14-172. MM-SMPL knobs that work (2026-10-05, Mika: "je pense qu'il y a plein de choses qui ne fonctionnent pas quand on tourne les knobs"; offline audit in Node with the real worklet, twelve knobs in seven contexts). smpl.worklet.js: a released pad or PLAY fades on RELEASE (a plain slice ignored it, a second PLAY did not stop a region); a loop keeps looping through its release with a 5 ms crossfade at the loop point (it clicked); a fading cloud keeps its grains, STOP is quick; a cloud taken again does not burst; DENSITY reacts at once; 32 grains per cloud at most, 128 in all; a grain never reads past its bounds; REV and LOOP follow live. engine.ts: FILTER is two biquads in series (low-pass, high-pass, never a type change: no 70 ms hole across the middle); a message sent while the worklet loads waits for it (no stuck note on a first quick tap). actions.ts: a pad's light goes off on release (a short slice's light no longer stays on). params.ts: RELEASE 0.5 (122 ms), SIZE 0.55 (86 ms), DENSITY 0.7 (26/s) by default (the default cloud was choppy and 6 dB lower; its level now counts the Hann window power), stored under mm.v4.smpl.2.
R14-173. MM-SMPL granular (2026-10-05, Mika: "implemente la fonction granulaire"). SCAN replaces SPREAD (params.ts scanSpeed: -2x to +2x, squared for slow speeds, FREEZE within 2 % of the middle, 1.00X near 0.854): the cloud's head moves at that speed and loops within the cloud's bounds. A pad's cloud lives in its slice, at POSITION inside it (it always started at the slice start), a sequence step's too; PLAY's in the region. SPRAY also opens the stereo image (0.2 + 1.6 x spray) and detunes each grain (spray x 0.25 semitone at most). GRAIN, the screen: touching the waveform makes a cloud there, it follows the finger and fades on RELEASE (actions.ts smplTouch, gestures.ts); the dashed POSITION line is PLAY's and the finger's, a cyan tick in each slice is its pad's, SCAN replaces LOOP in the status line. SLICE: turning a GRAIN knob says GRAIN ONLY: PRESS MODE. theme.ts: the GRAIN row POSITION SCAN SIZE DENSITY SPRAY (phone: SPRAY next to FILTER); midi/roto.ts SMPL: page 1 the sound, page 2 the grains, SCAN with a centre indent.
R14-174. Schedulers that do not cut (2026-10-05, Mika: "je lance le site et des que je fais un petit truc le son se coupe ; faut arranger ca une bonne fois pour toutes, optimise le tout"; measured: main-thread tasks of 0.1 to 5 s beat the 100 ms horizon: the first RUN, the first MM-ARP OPEN, a theme change, ADD DECK, a first visit on the phone, the first REVERB, a section render). audio/sched.ts: a 300 ms horizon (450 ms on a coarse pointer), GUARD_S 30 ms, reserve(s) (s seconds scheduled at once before known heavy work), grouped reschedules (12 ms, the MM-RYTM first). audio/clock.ts: each scheduled step keeps its voices, grid and pattern; a change (pattern, mute or solo, kit, voice settings, TONE, STRETCH, tempo, swing) cancels the steps not started yet (beyond 30 ms, newest first) and schedules them again; a tempo starts from the first cancelled step; a step of a finished bar keeps its pattern; onBar fires once per bar, rescheduled or dropped. drums.ts: a cancelled hat gives the open hat it choked back. voyager/arp.ts: a snapshot per step (grid, bar, chord, note index, last pitch), the worklet forgets cancelled notes (moog.worklet.js 'cancel', synth.ts synthCancel), rescheduled on RATE, GATE, OCTAVE, MODE, RANGE, NOTES, CHORD, ACCENT, DRIFT, the note sequence, a chord added or removed, tempo, swing; a dropped step still moves the bar and the notes. smpl/seq.ts: the same, the worklet's 'unseq'.
R14-175. Less main-thread work under the music (2026-10-05). audio/shots.ts: no sound computed on the main thread during playback (nothing ready: the hit is skipped, its sound first in the worker's queue); the prewarm starts at page load (index.tsx). scene/pcb.ts: prepare reserves the music; scene/renderer.ts: on desktop, both PCBs prepared after the intro in idle time and their big textures uploaded (initTexture); OPEN reserves 1.2 s (voyager/rig.ts too); the MM-DECKS and MM-SMPL programs compiled in parallel on arrival (compileAsync); no shader error check in production. audio/sends.ts warm and drums.ts: the reverbs built in idle time after the context; iOS audioSession playback; routeMachines connects before it disconnects. index.tsx: a hidden page (another window, another app) suspends the audio only when nothing plays (state/playLock.ts anyPlaying, the MM-SMPL sequence included); no reload on a width change while something plays; the scene rebuild reserves 3 s. dj/actions.ts: the crate's background analyses wait while anything plays; dj/engine.ts load reserves around the decoding and the waveforms.
R14-176. Fast OLED blit (2026-10-05). scene/pixels.ts: the points go into a small canvas (one pixel each, putImageData), scaled up without smoothing in one drawImage, then a multiply pattern of the dot grid (a 3 x 3 cell whose last row and column are 219): the same look without the per-texel loop; scene/screen.ts keeps no full-size ImageData.
R14-177. Checks (2026-10-05). Offline (Node, the real worklet): every knob in the seven contexts (GRAIN: POSITION, SCAN, SIZE, DENSITY, SPRAY live on pads, PLAY and the sequence; RELEASE on pads and PLAY), SCAN at freeze, 1x, 2x and -1x, a pad's grains inside its slice, the screen cloud. Browser with the sound cut (?mute=1, --mute-audio): RUN through a 250 ms freeze, no late or dropped step (before: late from 75 to 100 ms); 600 ms, late beyond the horizon as expected; reserve(2) then 1.5 s, none; a mute takes the coming kicks away at once; a tempo change spaces the next steps at the new tempo at once; a two-pattern chain under 207 reschedules switches once per bar, every step with its bar's pattern; the MM-ARP and the MM-SMPL sequence through a 250 ms freeze without a gap; the smoke run on desktop and phone without errors. No audio played on a speaker.

Pareil pour Sd et enleve moi la fenetre Samples on this device !"). ui/RytmSamples.tsx, audio/usersamples.ts and their CSS are removed (this supersedes the device-files part of R14-164): only the site folder public/samples/rytm/<family>/ feeds the selector, and what the old panel kept in the browser (localStorage mm.v4.usersamples.1, IndexedDB mm-v4-rytm-samples) is purged at load. audio/kit.ts kitStepLabels writes each sample's name at its stop (up to eight per family, their numbers beyond, as before); scene/tweakplate.ts draws a switch of more than five stops with a name longer than three characters radially (each name leaves its tick along the radius, the left half flipped so none reads upside down, its length capped by the cell so the type shrinks to fit) and lifts the knob's own name above them. scripts/import-rytm-samples.mjs (npm run samples:import) copies Mika's ten files from his Mac, where they are, into the site folder under clean names that give the stop order: KICK BLUEPRINT, VNTM, ENGELHARDT, CARASSI, STEIN, AFFKT; SNARE PSY 02, PSY 12, PSY 26, 707. R14-179. MIDI reference for the Roto-Control (2026-10-05, Mika: "pourrais tu me dire tout le midi comment c'est fait ?? avoir une liste pour que je puisse faire mon fichier json pour le roto"). scripts/midi-reference.mjs (npm run docs:midi) reads the real code in the dev site (midi/targets.ts, midi/roto.ts, the MM-DECKS and MM-SMPL targets loaded on demand) and writes docs/midi/MIDI-roto-reference.md (the rules: message key type:channel:number, learned assignments before the Roto map, channel N for knobs and N + 8 for buttons, CC 14 + n then 102 + n - 18, 0 to 127, 64 as the bipolar neutral, steps, toggles and holds, feedback, FOLLOW; the ROTO-SETUP JSON fields; the six setups control by control; the 345 targets by machine; how to write one's own Roto file plus the site's assignments file), MIDI-roto-setups.csv (one Roto control per line) and MIDI-targets.csv (one target per line). It is regenerated, not written by hand, so it follows the code (KICK SOUND has nine stops with the samples, SNARE SOUND seven).
R14-180. OPEN frames the inside (2026-10-05, Mika: "Quand on OPEN une machine j'aimerais que ca puisse zoom vers le contenu de l'interieur.. quand on ferme on reviens dans la vue reset view"). Desktop, MM-RYTM and MM-ARP: open, the camera no longer fits the whole exploded stack (hood and board) but the TWEAKS plate and its board (theme.ts OPEN_VIEW: the plate width plus 0.6 fills 82 % of the width); the pivot goes to the plate's centre in the world (y and z, Frame.openY and openZ), so turning the view turns around it; the framing follows the opening progress as before. OPEN and CLOSE of the machine one looks at bring the orbit back to the default view (renderer.ts openView, 500 ms): open always starts from the reset view, close returns to it whatever the user turned meanwhile. Phone: the previous framing (the upright plate already fills the screen), the reset still applies. Not during the intro.
R14-181. SAMPLE knob and BD by default (2026-10-05, Mika: "dans les parametres des voices a droite de Volume je veux un knob samples selection.. quand on commemnce a le tourner on voir sur l'ecran la liste des echantillons, on a appuyer sur un voice et la on choisi le sample. Par defaut je veux toujours que le BD soit selectionne pour les Fx Voices"). theme.ts: encoder vsound (SAMPLE), the eighth of the VOICE FX row, right of VOLUME; the desktop row now spans from the BD column to the OPEN column (five pitches) so the eight names stay apart; phone: one knob per step column. actions.ts soundDial: it sets the sound of the selected voice's family (audio/kit.ts: 909, 808, MM, then its samples; CH and OH share HATS, TOM and HT share TOMS; CY and PC have one sound, the screen says so), its stops follow the voice (BD 9, SD 7, the others 3). state/lcdSamples.ts and state/lcd.ts: turning it opens the SAMPLES page for 2.4 s; scene/screen.ts paints three rows (the previous sound, the current one in negative, the next), its rank and a rail of one dot per sound, then KICK SOUND with the voice tag and PRESS A VOICE: ITS SOUNDS; pressing another voice meanwhile switches the list to it. The 3D knob, the twin slider (one stop per arrow), the wheel, the phone KNOBS > VOICE FX page and the MIDI target rytm:enc:vsound (its stops read each time) follow. audio/pattern.ts: a visit starts with BD selected.
R14-182. Roto-Control files (2026-10-05, Mika: "donne moi un json parfait pour mon roto-control"). midi/roto.ts RYTM: page 2 is the VOICE FX row in the machine's order (VOLUME, SAMPLE, TONE, DECAY, V DIST, V CHORUS, V DELAY, V REVERB), SAMPLE continuous on the Roto (its stop count changes with the voice, the site takes the nearest); KICK TUNE moves to page 4 in place of KICK SOUND (SAMPLE with BD, the default, picks the kick). scripts/midi-reference.mjs also writes the six ROTO-SETUP files to docs/midi/roto/ (the same as DOWNLOAD THE 6 SETUPS) and reads the page's own module copies (an edited module served under a new address is another copy).

It appears once the machine is open and leaves as closing starts; a click closes (openToggle). Phone: the existing ui/PcbClose.tsx.
R14-184. Eight voices (2026-10-05, Mika: "je veux finalement 8 VOICES dans RYTM enleve RS et PC"). The rimshot and the percussion are gone everywhere: theme.ts Inst, PADS (two rows of four: BD SD CH OH on keys A S D F, CP TOM HT CY on Z X C V; EDIT and OPEN in the fifth column, VOICE_COLS 4; the pitch grows to 1.25 desktop, 1.4 phone, the pads to 0.98, so the block keeps its width and EDIT and OPEN their place; the VOICE FX row spans VOICE_COLS pitches as before), audio/pattern.ts INSTRUMENTS in the pads' order and the default pattern, audio/voicefx.ts, the generators (house.ts, beats.ts), the shot DSP (shotsdsp.ts rs, rsModel, pc removed; shots.ts no longer prepares them), the TWEAKS plate (no RIM: the six of the lower row spread over its seven columns, desktop; CLAP and TOMS centred in the last row, phone), the OLED meters (eight, spread over the same width), the MIX page (two rows of four), the editor and the phone docks (eight rows, four per row). Stored patterns, slots and presets from ten voices load their eight (RS and PC ignored, a preset's RS or PC voice settings skipped). Roto RYTM: page 3 the eight volumes, page 4 KICK SOUND, KICK TUNE, KICK ATTACK, KICK DECAY, KICK DRIVE, SNARE SOUND, SNAPPY, HATS SOUND; buttons page 2 the eight voices, page 3 their mutes, page 4 patterns A01 to A08; MIXER and LIVE mutes the eight voices. 327 MIDI targets.
R14-185. MM-SMPL OPEN, INFO inside (2026-10-05, Mika: "Le bouton Info doit etre a l'interieur OPEN de la machine SMPL"). The MM-SMPL had no hood; it gets one. dj/body.ts: wedge() takes a drop (the top comes down by the lid's thickness, its face painted as the tray floor) and lidSlab() builds the lid, a rounded slab in the top frame. smpl/rig.ts: top is the lid (the slab, the screen bezel and the four corner screws moved there from the body, every control); inner is the tray floor frame, holding the MM-RYTM board (scene/pcb.ts, model MM-SMPL R1.0, cleared under the plate) and a plate with no setting (scene/tweakplate.ts, its title head now configurable: MM-SMPL, SAMPLER / SLICER / GRANULAR, R1.0 VRSTL 2026). state/explode.ts smplExplode; scene/explode.ts drives it like the MM-ARP's (smpl/theme.ts SMPL_EXPLODE: desktop lift 4.4, back 3.6, -26 deg; portrait 6.2, 4.6, -58 deg; the board rises 0.45 out of the floor); occluders split between the tray and the lid. The renderer frames the inside when open, phone included (SMPL_OPEN_FRAME: the plate's width, its top's height; desktop aims at the whole board, the plate in its lower half under the MAUDITE MACHINE print), and OPEN or CLOSE resets the view (openView). ui/SmplInfo.tsx: the header key (where INFO was, left of the logo) is now OPEN, orange like the MM-RYTM OPEN pad with dark ink (6.4:1), bigger in portrait (1.15 x 0.42); open, INFO sits on the plate, left of CLOSE (ui/HoodClose.tsx) on desktop, alone and centred on the phone, where ui/PcbClose.tsx is the CLOSE. actions.ts: hoodOf('smpl') and openToggle take the MM-SMPL (the MM-DECKS still has no hood); the O key, the logo (goHome), Escape, the machine drawer thumbnails and the /v4 unmount follow; MIDI target smpl:open (328 targets; the Roto setups do not change). The user guide (npm run manual:smpl) shows OPEN as item 9 of the tour (page 3), O in the keyboard table, and where to find INFO in the troubleshooting with a shot of the open machine (page 14); the shots wait for the DOM keys to be placed.
R14-186. Pad names follow the eight-voice keys (2026-10-05). theme.ts PAD_ARIA: TOM on X, CH on D, OH on F; the twins and screen readers still named the ten-voice keys (TOM D, CH F, OH G).
R14-187. A sampler in each deck (2026-10-07, Mika: "je voudrais deplacer le contenu de MM-SMPL dans un DECK, chaque Deck doit avoir les memes choses pour sampler ce qui est en cours ... ou alors sampler le mixer ; tout doit etre disponible en mobile ; profitons d'avoir un grand ecran pour voir les choses et les boutons de CUE que je n'ai pas besoin pour l'instant transforme-les en boutons pour le sampler ; le design de l'interieur de l'ecran me plait quand meme quoiqu'un peu grossier donc ne change pas trop, mets les choses en valeur surtout"). sampler/sampler.ts: one Sampler per deck (samplerOf), everything the MM-SMPL did (slices 4/8/16/AUTO, SLICE or GRAIN, REV, LOOP, twelve settings, sixteen pads, a sixteen step sequence, SAVE as WAV, FILE), its worklet (sampler/sampler.worklet.js, moved) feeding the deck's mixer channel (EQ, filter, fader and effects apply). The four HOT CUE keys are now SMPL (the sampler page on the deck screen, or the track), REC DECK, REC MIX and PLAY (dj/layout.ts DJ_SMPL_KEYS, ids dj-a-smpl-open, -recdeck, -recmix, -play; keys 1 2 3 4 and 7 8 9 0; silk SAMPLER, the REC names in orange; LEDs: SMPL orange while its page shows, REC a moment after a take, PLAY yellow while it sounds and pale with a sample). REC DECK cuts from the deck's own buffer at once: its loop if it loops, else while playing the last LEN beats up to the last bar line (of LEN beats under four), paused LEN beats from the nearest beat; REC MIX takes the last LEN beats of what the mixer output (sampler/ring.ts, a 34 s ring worklet on the site output after the limiter, started with the DJ engine) up to the last bar of what you hear (dj/actions.ts mixBeat: the most heard deck's grid, else the MM-RYTM or MM-ARP clock); measured aligned within 3 ms. LEN 1/2/4/8/16 beats (default 4), retained per deck with the slicing, mode and page (mm.v4.dj.smpl.<deck>); the last sample of each deck returns from IndexedDB (the MM-SMPL's goes to deck A). The sequence follows the deck's own 16th grid while it plays (dj/actions.ts deckGridAfter), else the MM-RYTM, the MM-ARP or the sample's tempo; a step takes the slice that falls at its time (sampler/seq.ts sliceForStep), RANDOM too; PLAY without steps loops the region from the next beat. dj/SamplerScreen.tsx: the page laid on the deck screen like the track browser (same homography): header (deck, SMPL, the source DECK/MIX/FILE, name, beats and seconds, TRACK), the playing chips (SLICE/GRAIN, SLICES, LEN, REV, LOOP, PLAY/STOP), the waveform in the MM-SMPL screen colours, bigger (gold bars, orange numbered slices, bone region bounds, cyan POSITION, yellow and cyan heads; tap a slice, drag a region or a bound, GRAIN clouds under the finger), then a page: PADS (one big cell per slice, two rows of eight at 16), SAMPLE and GRAIN (drag cells, double tap resets, wheel, arrows), SEQ (sixteen steps, RANDOM, CLEAR), the tabs, FILE and SAVE. Escape closes it; E and I give the screen back to the browser. MIDI: dj:smpl:<deck>:knob:<id>, :pad:<0-15> (held), :mode, :slices, :len, :rev, :loop, :random, :clear, :save, :stop (dj/midi.ts; 359 targets).
R14-188. MM-SMPL removed (2026-10-07, Mika: "suite a ca supprime MM-SMPL ca ne sert a rien"). The machine, its rig, screen, gestures, twins, Dock, keys, hood (smplExplode), INFO and its PDF user guide (public/docs, scripts/smpl-manual, npm run manual:smpl) are gone; state/focus.ts MACHINES is MM-RYTM, MM-ARP, MM-DECKS again, ?m=smpl (sampler, grain) lands on the MM-DECKS; the MM-DECKS sits right of the MM-ARP. The mixer's LOOP > SMPL key and the T key are gone (ADD DECK moves next to the logo, the mixer subtitle returns). The play lock and the hidden-tab keep-alive count the deck samplers (state/djload.ts samplersSounding). Roto-Control (midi/roto.ts): setup 15 (SMPL) is gone, LIVE stays on 16; DECK (13): the hot cue buttons become SMPL, REC DECK, REC MIX, SMPL PLAY per deck, BEND's page ends with SMPL MODE, knob page 4 is each deck's sampler (LEVEL, PITCH, FILTER, POSITION; RYTM and ARP stay on the MIXER); MIXER (14) and LIVE (16): REC MIX A in place of LOOP > SMPL, LIVE's SMPL PLAY and STOP become SMPL PLAY A and B. docs/midi regenerated.
R14-189. MM-RYTM screen, OP-1 style (2026-10-07, Mika: "l'ecran du MM-RYTM, je le trouve brouillon, il y a des infos que je n'aime pas, que je ne veux pas voir, c'est trop vieille machine ; je ne veux pas de couleurs dessus, mais j'aimerais bien un design a la Teenage Engineering OP-1"). scene/screen.ts redrawn in vector (Canvas 2D, 1280 x 480, 1024 x 384 on a phone; scene/pixels.ts and its bitmap fonts are gone), black and bone only in three intensities, few words, layout in 320 x 120 units. Left: a ring of the sixteen steps of the chosen voice (no voice: the whole pattern half lit, ALL in the middle), one dot per step growing with velocity, beats 1 5 9 13 marked, the playhead a circle around the playing step and a tick toward it, a hit pulses 260 ms; in the middle the voice in large light type and its sound (909, 808, MM, a sample) or a MUTE or SOLO pill. Right: play triangle or stop square, the pattern (blinking when it changes), the tempo; then three settings drawn like the OP-1's, each its own picture: the voice's VOLUME (rising bars), TONE (a tilting filter curve), DECAY (an envelope whose tail lengthens), or with no voice the machine's SWING (pairs of dots, the second shifted), STRETCH (a wave that stretches), MASTER; bottom right the message of the moment, or the playing track with a thin bar and its dot (still clickable, bar in OLED.tex pixels), else only a faint TOUCH: PRESETS. EDIT puts the sixteen patterns on the ring (the current one full, the chain linked through the middle, the waiting one blinking, 1 5 9 13 numbered) with the pattern and an EDIT pill in the middle and the chain on the right; MIX draws the row's volumes as faders; SAMPLES the sound list with the current one in a pill and its rank; PRESETS the name large between arrows and the keys as outlined pills. Gone: the eight meters and their labels, READY and RUN, the section name, the beat boxes, the pixel gauges. The idle wave now ripples the ring. The LCD text model (state/lcd.ts, the twin) does not change.
R14-190. MM-BASS, a bassline generator (2026-10-07, Mika: "commence a travailler sur un prototype de generateur de bassline ; nous avons MM-ARP qui peut faire des melodies, mais je veux un generateur de bassline qui peut descendre super bas pour faire de beaux SUBs ; la meme taille que MM-RYTM et bien sur completement adapte en mobile ; inspire-toi de ces synthes, trouve le meilleur de chacun : Moog Minitaur, Norand Mono, Torso T-1, TB-303, Syntakt, Roland SE-02"). A fourth machine, src/v4/bass/, between the MM-ARP and the MM-DECKS (state/focus.ts MACHINES: mm808, voy, bass, dj; ?bass=0 removes it for the tab, ?m=bass, mm-bass or acid lands on it; the MM-DECKS now starts right of it). The size of the MM-RYTM (BODY.w x BODY.d, standing at 8.2 x 14.4 on a phone), built from the MM-DECKS parts (dj/body wedge, bezel, screws, feet, back connectors; dj/controls knobs and rubber LED keys; dj/silk). Desktop top, back to front: header MM-BASS, BASSLINE GENERATOR / ACID / SUB and the logotype; the screen on the left, the large aluminium CUTOFF (the Minitaur's) graduated 0 to 10, then two rows, FILTER (RESO, ENV MOD, DECAY, ACCENT in orange, VOLUME) and VOICE (WAVE, SUB, DRIVE, GLIDE, OCTAVE); the GENERATOR row of seven black knobs with orange names (the Torso T-1: STYLE, DENSITY, SLIDES, ACCENTS, RANGE, ROOT, SCALE); ten keys RUN | GEN, MUTATE, CLEAR | ACCENT, SLIDE | NOTE -, NOTE +, OCT -, OCT +; sixteen steps in one row by groups of four (the TB-303, the Norand Mono), numbered, 1 5 9 13 stronger. Phone: the screen full width, knobs in rows of four (CUTOFF larger), keys in two rows of five, steps in two rows of eight. Step LEDs: orange a note (brighter with accent), pale a tie, yellow the playing step, the chosen step lighter; RUN gold while it plays, ACCENT and SLIDE lit when the chosen step has them. The rig, its gestures, twins, keyboard and MIDI targets load apart (state/bassload.ts), like the MM-DECKS; scene/renderer.ts attaches it after the intro, frames it face on like a deck, peeks its neighbours (MM-ARP left, MM-DECKS right), gives it a contact shadow (scene/floor.ts uX.w) and a drawer thumbnail. No hood. The play lock and the hidden-tab keep-alive count its sequence (state/playLock.ts). A SoundCloud track of the site that starts stops it. The sound goes to the master bus (synthPort().input), not to a mixer channel yet.
R14-191. MM-BASS voice and TB-303 filter (2026-10-07, Mika: "j'aimerais que MM-BASS puisse avoir la meme qualite de filtre que la TB-303 pour faire de l'acid, voire exactement la meme"). AudioWorklet mm-bass (bass/bass.worklet.js), monophonic, run at twice the context rate, pitch, envelopes and cutoff at control rate every 4 samples. Oscillator: band limited saw and square (polyBLEP), WAVE crossfades them. Filter: the TB-303 model of Open303 (TeeBee: four coupled poles like the diode ladder, a 150 Hz one pole highpass in the feedback path so the bass stays when resonance rises, Open303's polynomial fits for tuning b0 and feedback k, MIT notice in the file), RESO up to the edge of self oscillation; measured offline: the resonant peak sits on the cutoff, stable at full resonance, no NaN. MEG: instant attack, DECAY 120 ms to 2.5 s, ENV MOD up to 5 octaves; an accented note gets the 303's short fixed decay (200 ms), more depth and the accent sweep circuit (a charge that builds from accent to accent, slower with resonance), and a louder VCA. Slides (legato) glide the pitch over GLIDE (12 to 350 ms) without retriggering the envelopes. DRIVE (tanh) after the filter, a DC blocker; SUB, a sine one octave below the note, is added clean after the filter and the drive, so subs stay full at any resonance. CUTOFF 60 Hz to 6 kHz; OCTAVE -2 to +1 lets the lowest notes reach about 20 Hz (MIDI 16). About 1.7 % of one core. The worklet reports the sounding cutoff, envelope, gate and note about 25 times a second for the screen.
R14-192. MM-BASS sequencer, generator and screen (2026-10-07, Mika: "j'adore reellement la facon de travailler sur les machines Elektron, principalement le Syntakt"). Sixteen steps (bass/state.ts, kept under mm.v4.bass.state): off, note (a scale degree, an octave offset, ACCENT, SLIDE) or tie (the TB-303's TIME mode: the previous note goes on). Tapping a step picks it, a second tap cycles off, note, tie (after a note), off; dragging a step up or down sets its note in the scale (14 px per degree with a mouse, 18 by touch, the wheel too); ACCENT, SLIDE, NOTE - +, OCT - + act on the chosen step; stopped, a touched note sounds 220 ms. bass/seq.ts schedules on the audio clock like the arpeggiator (audio/sched.ts, order 3, 25 ms wake ups, reschedules what has not left on any change of steps, ROOT, SCALE, OCTAVE, STYLE, tempo or swing): on the MM-RYTM grid when it runs, else the MM-ARP grid, else its own at the pattern tempo, with the MM-RYTM swing; gate per style (ACID .52, DISCO .45, ROLL .38, SUB .92 of a step). Pitch: F#2 base, ROOT (F# to F, or ARP: it follows the root of the chord the MM-ARP plays), SCALE (MINOR, DORIAN, PHRYGIAN, HARMONIC, PENTA). bass/gen.ts, rules rather than notes like the Torso T-1: ACID (303 sixteenths, root, fifth, minor third, seventh, octave jumps, off-beat accents, slides), DISCO (octave bounce, low on the beat, high on the and), ROLL (rolling between the kicks, the beat left empty), SUB (long tied low notes changing one to four times a bar); DENSITY, SLIDES, ACCENTS, RANGE (1 to 3 octaves); GEN writes a line and starts it, MUTATE changes a few steps, CLEAR empties. The screen (bass/screen.ts) speaks the language of the new MM-RYTM screen: black and bone, no colour; play triangle or stop square and the STYLE, root, scale and tempo; a piano roll of the sixteen steps (bars at their pitch, full when accented, ties extend them, a short stroke for a slide, the chosen step in a faint band, the playhead underlined and circled); on the right the filter curve with a dot on the cutoff that sounds; the message of the moment or the chosen step at the bottom.
R14-193. MM-BASS controls everywhere (2026-10-07). Keyboard while it is in use (bass/keys.ts, physical keys): 1 to 8 and Q to I the steps, Space RUN, G GEN, M MUTATE, A ACCENT, S SLIDE, Up and Down NOTE + -, Z and X OCT - +; the RYTM pad keys, Space and O no longer reach the MM-RYTM there. HTML twins (bass/Twins.tsx): knobs as sliders (one notch per arrow on the selectors), keys and steps as buttons named with their note. MIDI targets (bass/midi.ts): bass:knob:<id> (18), bass:key:<key> (10), bass:running (a toggle whose LED follows), bass:trig:<0-15>; scope bass in the MIDI panel, learn on the 3D controls. Roto-Control setup BASS on slot 15, knobs on channel 5, buttons on 13 (midi/roto.ts, the old SMPL slot): page 1 CUTOFF, RESO, ENV MOD, DECAY, ACCENT, DRIVE, SUB, VOLUME with RUN, GEN, MUTATE, CLEAR, ACCENT, SLIDE, PREV and NEXT MACHINE; page 2 WAVE, GLIDE, OCTAVE, STYLE, DENSITY, SLIDES, ACCENTS, RANGE with steps 1 to 8; page 3 ROOT, SCALE, SWING with steps 9 to 16; page 4 NOTE - +, OCT - +, RYTM + ARP, RUN RYTM, RUN ARP, MM-STUDIO; selectors are stepped knobs with their names; FOLLOW shows the MM-BASS. Header selector, machine drawer, mobile selector and overview help know it. docs/midi regenerated (405 targets, 6 setups).
R14-194. Closer desktop framing, firmware labels (2026-10-07, Mika: "quand nous sommes en desktop, pour cette taille de machine comme la MM-RYTM, tu pourrais arriver plus zoome, tout en voyant la machine de droite bien sur pour qu'on puisse cliquer dessus et switcher rapidement ; tu pourrais mettre V3 sur RYTM, V2.3 sur ARP et V1 sur BASS"). scene/renderer.ts frameOf: a single machine on desktop (MM-RYTM, MM-ARP, MM-BASS) is framed face on, its real width over 80 % of the canvas (SINGLE_FILL, instead of the azimuth 45 projected width over 78 %) and, for the MM-RYTM, its front height (BODY.d + 0.5 instead of MACHINE_H): about a third larger on arrival; turned with the orbit it may overflow a little, as on a phone. The overview and the opened views keep their frames. The neighbour's peek is 52 px (was 36) with a 0.6 gap (was 0.8), so the next machine stays visible and clickable at the screen edge. Firmware: MM-RYTM header and back plate FIRMWARE V.3.0 / 2026, MM-ARP header (desktop, in place of ARPEGGIATOR SYNTHESIZER) and back plate V.2.3, MM-BASS header V.1.0 left of its logotype (its subtitle is now BASSLINE GENERATOR).
R14-195. Factory presets, electronic styles (2026-10-07, Mika: "je voudrais qu'il y ait deja des presets de styles de musique electro differents, et pas juste Disco"). state/factory.ts: the same ten styles on the MM-RYTM, the MM-ARP and the MM-BASS, so loading one style on all three gives a coherent track: ACID, DARK DISCO, INDIE DANCE, MINIMAL, PSY PROG, TECHNO, HOUSE, ELECTRO, EBM, ITALO, plus DEEP SUB on the bass. MM-RYTM: the pattern and velocities, tempo (118 to 138), SWING and effects, the kit sound (909, 808, MM; voice levels back to default, kit knobs untouched). MM-ARP: rate, mode, range, notes, gate, filter, overdrive and effects, chord progression (other knobs at default, AUTO sequence). MM-BASS: sound, generator settings (style, scale, octave, range) and a line in that style drawn once with a seeded generator (the same at each load). state/presets.ts: presets.list(m) is Mika's presets then the factory ones (ids factory-<machine>-<n>, factory: true), load() finds both; the MM-BASS is a preset machine too (all its knobs and its line with locks; loading neither starts nor stops it). state/presetMode.ts browses the list: a factory preset is titled FACTORY, its NAME and DEL keys are blank (pressed: FACTORY), SAVE makes one's own copy. The MM-BASS screen takes the presets like the other two: touching it opens them (TOUCH: PRESETS faint at its bottom right), then left and right halves of its top for previous and next, its bottom band for SAVE NAME DEL EXIT (hotspots basslcd, twins placed as soon as the mode opens).
R14-196. MM-BASS styles (2026-10-07). STYLE has eleven detents (bass/params.ts BASS_STYLES): ACID (303 sixteenths, octave jumps, off-beat accents, slides), DARK DISCO (octave bounce, the line moving to the sixth, fourth or seventh at mid-bar), INDIE DANCE (driving eighths, off-beat octave, ghost sixteenths, fifth at the bar end), MINIMAL (one to four notes off the beat, an eight-step figure repeated, one note moving in the second half), PSY PROG (the roll K B B B, or K . B B when sparser), TECHNO (bass on the off-beat with a rumble of sixteenths around it), HOUSE (four swung rhythms, tied notes, octave and fifth, more slides), ELECTRO (3 + 3 + 2 syncopation, robotic octave jumps), EBM (hammered sixteenths, an accent per beat), ITALO (octave sixteenths, chord change at mid-bar), SUB (long tied low notes; lengths now always fill sixteen steps). Gate per style from .32 (MINIMAL) to .92 (SUB); slide chances weighted per style.
R14-197. MM-BASS EDIT, sixteen patterns (2026-10-07, Mika: "sur BASS il n'y a pas de bouton EDIT, j'aimerais en avoir un"). An EDIT key after RUN (eleven keys: RUN, EDIT | GEN, MUTATE, CLEAR | ACCENT, SLIDE | NOTE - +, OCT - +; on a phone two rows of five and six), key E, MIDI bass:key:edit, state/editor.ts id 'bass'. As on the MM-RYTM (bass/patterns.ts, kept under mm.v4.bass.patterns): the sixteen steps become slots A01 to A16, each a whole line with its locks; tap one to play it (stopped: at once; running: at the next bar), tap others within two seconds to chain them (one bar each, in a loop), hold an empty one 0.5 s to copy the current line into it. The sequencer calls bassPatterns.bar(time, first) as it schedules the first step of every bar (a rescheduled bar is not replayed, the pattern change itself does not trigger a reschedule); stopping resets the chain to its start. Step LEDs in EDIT: current bright orange, chain half, waiting slot gold, filled faint; the LOCK row dims. The screen shows EDIT PATTERNS, the slots in two rows of eight (current filled, waiting one outlined, chain positions numbered), the current slot large and the chain under it.
R14-198. MM-BASS parameter locks (2026-10-07, Mika: "sur BASS je veux des boutons au-dessus de chaque step du sequenceur ; quand j'appuie sur ce bouton je peux parametrer tout ce que je veux sur CE step que j'ai selectionne uniquement, et donc les parametres changent au passage de ce step"). Sixteen LOCK keys, one above each step (desktop a row at z 2.13; phone above each row of eight, the steps a little shallower), hotspots basslock, MIDI bass:lock:<0-15> and bass:lock (the chosen step), key L, Roto BASS page 4 LOCK and EDIT. Tapping one makes its step the lock target (and the chosen step); the ten sound knobs (CUTOFF, RESO, ENV MOD, DECAY, ACCENT, WAVE, SUB, DRIVE, GLIDE, VOLUME) then write that step's locks instead of the base values and show the locked value; generator knobs and OCTAVE stay global; double tap (or Delete on its twin) removes a knob's lock, CLEAR clears the step's locks; the same LOCK key, Escape, EDIT or another machine end it. bass/state.ts BassStep.locks (cleaned on load, saved with the line, the patterns and presets; the save is debounced 300 ms). Sound: each note-on carries its step's locks and a locked tie sends its own; the worklet keeps the knobs as a base and applies the locks of the sounding step over it before the note (its decay and accent depend on them), back to the base at the next unlocked note (measured offline: cutoff 151 Hz, 3.8 kHz on the locked step, back to 151 Hz). LEDs: LOCK pale orange when its step has locks, gold for the target. Screen: a LOCK 05 pill in place of the style, the step's column brighter, the filter curve drawn with its locks, the locks listed at the bottom (CUT 1.7 KHZ  RES 35), a small tick above every locked step's column.
R14-199. MM-BASS before MM-ARP (2026-10-07, Mika: "MM-BASS devrait se situer avant MM-ARP, fais ce petit changement"). The machines on the table, left to right, are now MM-RYTM, MM-BASS, MM-ARP, MM-DECKS (state/focus.ts MACHINES), so the two rhythm machines sit side by side and the MM-ARP follows the bass. bass/theme.ts bassX() is right of the MM-RYTM (BODY.w / 2 + gap + BASS_W / 2), no longer depends on the MM-ARP; voyager/theme.ts VOY_X is right of the MM-BASS when it is on the table (the MM-RYTM alone: unchanged); dj/theme.ts DJ_LEFT is right of the MM-ARP again. scene/renderer.ts placeNeighbors is now generic over MACHINES: in use on desktop, the machine on its left and the one on its right slide to the screen edge (PEEK.px), each at least a gap from the used machine; the overview frame ends on the MM-DECKS (or the MM-ARP without it). The header selector, the machine drawer, the arrow keys, the phone swipe order and the Roto prev / next follow MACHINES: RYTM, BASS, ARP, DECKS. The Roto setup numbers do not change (RYTM 11, ARP 12, DECK 13, MIXER 14, BASS 15, LIVE 16), nor the MIDI ids or the old links (?m=bass, ?m=arp). llms.txt lists the machines in the new order.
R14-200. MUTE and SOLO, one voice or several (2026-10-07, Mika: "quand j'appuie sur MUTE je clique sur une voix, ca la mute, mais une autre voix juste apres ne doit pas se muter ; MUTE de nouveau demute la voix ; DEUX fois MUTE et je peux muter plusieurs voix ; l'ecran affiche la difference et le tip du double MUTE ; pareil pour SOLO"). state/voices.ts: solo becomes a list (several voices in solo), muteMulti / soloMulti, arm / disarm / release, lit(). actions.ts modeTap: one press arms the mode for one voice (the voice tapped next is muted or soloed, then the mode drops and the pads play again; the voice stays muted, the key lit); a second press within MODE_DOUBLE_MS (420 ms) makes it MULTI (every voice tapped toggles, as many as wanted); a press while armed, in MULTI or with a voice still muted (soloed) brings every voice back. The voice touched just before MUTE (1.5 s) is still muted at once (2026-10-04), and a quick second press then keeps it in MULTI. MUTE and SOLO stay exclusive. Screen (scene/screen.ts paintMode): when no message shows, the bottom line holds the mode and its tip: MUTE 1 VOICE / 2X MUTE: SEVERAL, MULTI MUTE / TAP VOICES / MUTE: OFF, MUTED BD SD / MUTE: ALL ON, and the same for SOLO. Roto voice mutes (voiceMute) light MUTE too; MUTE then brings them back. Twins, Dock and aria labels follow.
R14-201. Muted voices in red, the light machine's flash in orange (2026-10-07, Mika: "je n'aime pas la couleur du Mute ; Solo en bleu d'accord, mais Mute en rouge, type LED a travers un caoutchouc" and "en light mode, le petit eclairage quand les voix jouent est jaunatre, difficile a voir"). theme.ts VOICE_TINT.mute: dark #4A0F0C, light #C8372C, plus PAD_GLOW.muted (a red emissive, the top brighter than the flanks) and PAD_HALO.muted (a red halo): scene/pads.ts restGlow gives a muted voice pad the MUTED glow. FLASH_TINT (light only, [1.32, 0.21, 0.014]): during a voice flash the light rubber turns orange (like OPEN), the emissive flash lowered to [0.3, 0.045, 0]; the dark machine keeps its yellow flash. scene/sequencer3d.ts: the MUTE key LED is red (#FF2A1F) and SOLO blue (#5B9BFF).
R14-202. Velocity by holding a cell in the MM-RYTM EDIT grid (2026-10-07, Mika: "en Edit, j'aimais bien le principe de changer la velocite en maintenant le clic de la souris appuye"). ui/BeatEditor.tsx: a grid gesture waits; moving to another column paints the row (as before, a filled start cell erases); holding 350 ms, or a vertical drag of 6 px on the cell, enters velocity mode: up and down change that cell's velocity (12 px per level with a mouse, 16 with a finger, 1 to 9), an empty cell is filled first; the level is written in the cell (v4-beat-velnum) and the cursor is ns-resize; a quick tap on a filled cell still removes it. The VEL lane stays. Hint text updated.
R14-203. MM-BASS shaped like the MM-RYTM (2026-10-07, Mika: "MM-BASS devrait avoir la meme forme de boitier que MM-RYTM, un peu arrondi sur les bordures mais pas trop"). dj/body.ts roundSlab: the MM-RYTM panel slab (a rounded rectangle, radius 0.2 plus a 0.1 bevel, 0.14 thick, top at y 0 of the top frame); bass/rig.ts buildBody puts it on the wedge lowered by its thickness (wedge drop) and drops the four screws (the MM-RYTM has none). Desktop and phone.
R14-204. Gain staging: the kick is the reference (2026-10-07, Mika: "quand je fais du son, le kick est la reference ; je descends le tout de -12 dB ; tout ce qu'il y a apres le kick ne doit pas etre aussi fort que lui : mon sub a -14, mon snare a -13 ou -14 ; ensuite je rattrape au master ; la, le snare et le clap sonnent vraiment trop fort"). Measured before: at equal K-weighted loudness, the snare, the clap and the closed hats peaked 2 to 6 dB over the kick, the MM-ARP 2.5 dB over it. audio/shotsdsp.ts: the kick is set by its peak (SHOT_KICK_PEAK -2.5 dB, every kick model and sample alike); every other voice keeps a loudness target (SHOT_LOUD, lower than before) under a peak ceiling SHOT_BELOW dB below the kick (SD 1.5, CP 3.5, TOM 3, HT 3.5, CH 6, OH 5.5, CY 7), the lower wins. Measured at the output, one hit at a time: kick -4.5 to -6 dBFS, snare 2 to 3 dB under it, clap 2.5 to 6, toms 5 to 6, hats 6 to 7, cymbal 9. audio/drums.ts RYTM_TRIM 0.8 to 1 (the make-up: the whole pattern peaks under -1 dBFS). bass/bass.worklet.js: the SUB replaces part of the oscillator instead of adding to it (oscillator x (1 - 0.5 sub), sine 0.55 sub) and the volume constant 1.82: the default line peaks near -6 dBFS, 2 dB under the kick. audio/synth.ts VOLUME_K 1.03 to 0.7: the default arpeggio peaks near -9 dBFS, 4 dB under the kick.
R14-205. Factory presets rewritten from each style's construction (2026-10-07, Mika: "je n'aime pas du tout les presets, ce ne sont pas des sons dans les styles dont ils portent le nom ; refais-les en analysant la construction de chaque style ; pour les kicks et les snares, utilise mes samples"). state/factory.ts: ten styles (ACID 130, DARK DISCO 118, INDIE DANCE 122, MINIMAL 125, PSY PROG 138, TECHNO 132, HOUSE 124, ELECTRO 128, EBM 124, ITALO 120) plus DEEP SUB on the bass, every part written by hand from an analysis of the style (drum grid and dynamics, swing, kit, per-voice effects; bass line, sound and root; synth patch, arpeggio and chords), documented in the module header; no random line any more. Kicks and snares are Mika's samples (public/samples/rytm), chosen from an offline analysis (pitch, length, attack): BLUEPRINT house / italo, VNTM minimal, ENGELHARDT techno / electro, CARASSI acid / psy prog / EBM, STEIN dark disco, AFFKT indie dance; PSY 02 indie dance / techno / house, PSY 12 acid / minimal / psy prog, PSY 26 EBM (its tail cut by the voice DECAY), 707 dark disco / electro / italo; a missing sample falls back to a computed model. Kicks are tuned to F# (TUNE -1 semitone, VNTM +1). The bass lines are written as tokens (parseLine: '.' rest, '-' tie, degree with + octave, A accent, S slide); ROOT follows the MM-ARP chords (ARP) in the styles that change chords (dark disco, indie dance, house, EBM, italo), F# otherwise. Levels measured offline per preset: bass lines near -6 dBFS (VOLUME per preset), synth patches near -8.5 dBFS (MINIMAL's bleep lower), drums with snare + clap 1.1 to 3 dB under the kick (minimal softer) and the pattern under -1.4 dBFS.
R14-206. A 5-channel MIXER: three machines, two decks (2026-10-07, Mika: "etant donne qu'il y a MM-BASS maintenant, ca fait trois machines, donc on devrait avoir trois tranches disponibles dans le MIXER pour les machines, et deux pour les DECKS, donc 5"). dj/theme.ts: DJ_MACHINE_CHANNELS 3, channels 1 MM-RYTM, 2 MM-BASS, 3 MM-ARP, then the decks (deckChannel = 3 + deck index), DJ_CHANNELS 5 by default (up to 7 with four decks), DJ_CH_NAMES. audio/drums.ts: the MM-BASS has its own output (bassOut, SynthPort.bass; bass/engine.ts connects to it), MachineOuts { rytm, bass, arp } and routeMachines route the three outputs to channels 1 to 3. dj/state.ts: key mm.v4.dj.3; from mm.v4.dj.2 old 0 stays 0, old 1 (ARP) goes to 2, old decks shift by one, the BASS channel starts at fader 1; from mm.v4.dj.1 every channel shifts by three; FX TO follows the same mapping. The PLAY key on the mixer is MACHINES (PLAY/STOP MACHINES starts or stops the MM-RYTM clock, the arpeggio and the bass sequencer; its LED follows all three). Roto (midi/roto.ts): FX TO 6 steps ALL / RYTM / BASS / ARP / A / B; MIXER page 1 faders RYTM, BASS, ARP, A, B plus FILTER RYTM, A, B; page 2 FILTER BASS and ARP, HI / LOW for RYTM, BASS (peach) and ARP; page 3 the decks on channels 4 and 5; buttons page 1 of MIXER and LIVE: RUN RYTM, RUN BASS, RUN ARP, PLAY A / B, CUE A / B, MACHINES (REC MIX A dropped). MIDI ids dj-chN-*: ch2 is now BASS, ch3 ARP, ch4 and ch5 the decks (docs/midi regenerated, the Roto setups must be re-imported).
R14-207. The decks follow the machines (2026-10-07, Mika: "j'aimerais que les decks soient sync avec les machines, automatiquement, quand les machines jouent"). dj/actions.ts machinesOn (the MM-RYTM clock or the arpeggio running), machinesGrid, machinesBpm: while the machines play they are the SYNC reference of every deck (syncBpm, refBeat; syncDeck returns null). follow(d): a playing deck with a known BPM takes their tempo (matchTempo, half or double when closer) and aligns its beats on theirs (alignNow); a deck started while they play does it before it starts and waits for one of their beats. watchMachines (clock, arp, pattern): the machines starting clear the freed decks and follow every playing deck 80 ms later; a machine tempo change applies the tempo at once and realigns the beats 400 ms after the last change. Touching a deck's pitch (fader, wheel, PITCH - / +, keys) frees it (freed) until its next PLAY or a manual SYNC; when the machines stop every deck keeps its tempo. Verified muted: a deck started at 124 locks to 124.000, follows 126 then 128, a freed deck stays at 126.15, SYNC brings it back.
R14-208. Deck pitch to 0.05 BPM, with a finger too (2026-10-07, Mika: "je veux pouvoir modifier le pitch des decks finement, arriver a des .05 sans probleme, et avec le doigt aussi en mobile"). dj/actions.ts TEMPO_STEP 0.05 (was 0.1): djTempoStep moves the shown BPM to the next 0.05 grid point (Shift 1 BPM; 0.01 % without a known BPM); djSetPitch (pitch fader, mouse wheel, finger drag) quantizes the resulting tempo to the 0.05 BPM grid when the BPM is known, so a finger drag stops on .05 values. dj/gestures.ts: wheel and tempo keys use TEMPO_STEP; a held PITCH - / + repeats after 400 ms every 90 ms, then every 45 ms after 10 steps. dj/Twins.tsx and keys.ts (Z / X) follow. MIDI labels say 0.05 BPM.
R14-209. Bigger MM-ARP controls on the phone (2026-10-07, Mika: "en mobile, sur MM-ARP, je trouve les boutons petits"). voyager/theme.ts portrait only: SIZE_SCALE xl 1.5, l 1.1, m 1.08, s 0.98, sw 0.62, sel 0.76 (sized so no label overlaps); VOY_KNOB.scale 1.36, panelScale 1.2 and a touch radius touchR 0.42 (voyager/knobs.ts: the hotspot is at least that); buttons w 1.36, d 0.78, z 1.6; VOY_PAD size 0.86, zs 5.9, labelDz 0.62. Desktop unchanged.
R14-210. MM-BASS redesigned like a Monark, with Elektron habits (2026-10-08, Mika: "MM-BASS est un peu complexe ; je m'attendais plus a une machine qui ressemble a un MONARK de Native Instruments qu'a un T-1 incomprehensible ; j'aime Elektron : quelque chose d'intuitif, pour qu'on ne cherche pas les choses ; je voudrais aussi un bouton EDIT, je n'en vois pas"). bass/theme.ts: the face in signal-flow sections (BASS_SECTIONS, a bracket above each on desktop, under each row on the phone): GENERATOR next to the screen (STYLE s 1.45, DENSITY, GEN and MUTATE keys), then one sound row OSC (OCTAVE, WAVE, SUB) | FILTER (CUTOFF s 1.3, RESO) | ENVELOPE (ENV MOD, DECAY) | ACCENT / SLIDE (ACCENT, GLIDE) | OUTPUT (DRIVE, VOLUME); EDIT and OPEN top right, bigger (1.3 x 0.5), orange labels and an idle orange glow (GEN too); the play row RUN | CLEAR | ACCENT SLIDE | NOTE - NOTE + OCT - OCT +; BASS_KEYS carry their own place and size. Phone: four rows (GENERATOR with GEN / MUTATE, OSC, FILTER + ENVELOPE, ACCENT / SLIDE + OUTPUT), RUN CLEAR EDIT OPEN, then the step keys, two rows of eight steps, LOCK buttons 0.72 x 0.3. Header MONO BASS SYNTH, FIRMWARE V.2.0. The generator rules SLIDES / ACCENTS are labelled SLIDE PROB / ACC PROB (ids unchanged) and move under the hood with RANGE, ROOT, SCALE (params.ts plate flag, BASS_FACE_KNOBS / BASS_PLATE_KNOBS). The overview help text follows.
R14-211. MM-BASS OPEN and the TWEAKS plate (2026-10-08, Mika: "je voudrais un bouton OPEN, et dans le OPEN des parametres plus particuliers"). state/explode.ts bassExplode; actions.ts openToggle / hoodOf / escape; renderer.ts attachBass (reduced motion, the explode animator, OPEN / CLOSE reset the view, the open frame BASS_OPEN_FRAME); bass/rig.ts: the top is a lid (roundSlab + screen bezel brought into the top frame), the wedge is the tray, the MM-RYTM board (Pcb, model MM-BASS R2.0) rises from it with bass/tweaks.ts BassTweaks (scene/tweakplate.ts): GENERATOR row SLIDE PROB, ACC PROB, RANGE (3-step switch), ROOT, SCALE; VOICE row LENGTH (AUTO or 10-100 %), ACC DECAY (80-600 ms), SWEEP (0-4 oct), RELEASE (6-400 ms), SUB OCT (-1 / -2 switch), TUNE (+/-50 cents); desktop two rows of seven cells with the title, INFOS and CLOSE (DOM keys, ui/BassInfosKey.tsx, ui/HoodClose.tsx), phone four rows of three with INFOS in the last cell (CLOSE at the bottom, ui/PcbClose.tsx). Plate hotspots bass-tw-<id> (kind bassknob, the face gestures) live when open; twins follow. Keys O (OPEN) and I (INFOS); MIDI bass:key:open, bass:infos and the six new bass:knob targets (437 targets); Roto MM BASS knobs 19-23 LENGTH, ACC DECAY, SWEEP, RELEASE, TUNE. The six voice settings are worklet constants made knobs (bass.worklet.js, engine.ts, seq.ts gateOf / endNote); their defaults render bit-identical to the old worklet; LENGTH is lockable per step; a step's DECAY lock now wins over the accent decay.
R14-212. MM-BASS locks you can hear and see (2026-10-08, Mika: "je crois que mes parameter locks ne fonctionnent pas : je clique sur le bouton au-dessus des steps et ca ne fait rien"). The locks worked, but at stop nothing sounded and the machine did not change. bass/actions.ts: bassLockEnter auditions the step at stop (and 160 ms after each locked turn); an empty step being locked gets a root note; in LOCK, tapping another step moves the lock there (no note change) and tapping the locked step leaves LOCK; ACCENT locked on a non-accented note sets its accent. bass/gestures.ts: holding a step 350 ms without moving locks it (Elektron); a knob turned during the hold makes the release leave LOCK, otherwise LOCK stays (mouse: long press, release, turn). bass/rig.ts: the locked step blinks gold (760 ms), a LED dot beside each lockable face knob lights orange when it is locked on that step (dim in LOCK, off outside), the screen shows the LOCK grid (screen.ts drawLockGrid: header LOCK 05  F#2 ACC SLD, eleven cells, locked ones inverted) and, for 1.2 s after any knob turn, the knob echo (drawKnob: section, label and value large, the parameter's diagram). The trig bracket says LOCK (OR HOLD A STEP) + TURN A KNOB.
R14-213. MM-BASS generator that answers its knobs (2026-10-08, "pour qu'on ne cherche pas les choses"). bass/actions.ts: GEN draws a seed (mulberry32); while the line is still the one GEN wrote (same steps array), turning STYLE, DENSITY, SLIDE PROB, ACC PROB or RANGE rewrites it with the same seed (the knob is heard at once); after any edit the knob only changes the recipe and the screen says PRESS GEN.
R14-214. MM-BASS INFOS (2026-10-08, Mika: "dans le OPEN, un bouton INFOS : quand je clique dessus et que je survole chaque parametre du BASS, j'ai un descriptif qui vient dessus, du texte et meme une image"). state/bassInfos.ts (on, id, pinned; hover, show, hide, toggle); bass/infos.ts (41 French cards: section, title, text, tip; bassInfoIdOf maps hotspot ids); bass/diagrams.ts (a live 240 x 120 vector picture per control from the real laws: filter response, envelopes, accent circuit, waveforms, drive curve, glide, generator rolls replayed with fixed seeds...); bass/InfosCard.tsx + infos.css: the card above the control (flips below or to the side), a bottom sheet on the phone, and an INFOS ON chip with a close cross. Desktop hover (rig.setHover), phone tap shows without acting (a knob drag still turns it). Turned on by the INFOS key under the hood or I; stays on after CLOSE; Escape, the chip or I turn it off.
R14-215. A clean kick (2026-10-08, Mika: "quand j'essaie d'utiliser juste MM-RYTM, j'ai l'impression que mon kick a un chorus, qu'il est double ; je veux un bon kick"). Measured causes: the global CHORUS insert of the RYTM bus put a copy of the kick 7 to 28 ms late, different at every hit and between L and R (factory presets DARK DISCO 0.08, ITALO 0.15), the pattern REVERB / DELAY sends and DIST also took the kick; and the BD voice was polyphonic, the 909 tail (-13 dB at the next beat at 130 BPM) beat against the new kick at 12 to 0.6 Hz. audio/drums.ts: the kick has its own path (kickBus -> the pattern TONE only -> LEVEL), the other voices keep DIST, TONE, CHORUS and the sends (taken from a pads send, level squared); a kick chokes the previous one in 3 ms ending at its start (bdGate / bdEnd, restored on cancel and around offline renders). audio/shotsdsp.ts: kick samples summed to mono.
R14-216. Gain staging measured at the real output, the kick as the reference (2026-10-08, Mika: "tous les levels configures comme je te l'ai demande"). Measured before: Chrome's DynamicsCompressor on the RYTM bus added +3.7 dB of make-up under -14 dBFS and compressed the kick (the snare came out 0.75 dB under it), the three machines summed to +1.6 to +2.4 dBFS and the output limiter took 2 to 3 dB off the kicks. audio/drums.ts: no compressor in the path (lvl -> rytmOut), RYTM_TRIM 0.71; bass.worklet.js volume constant 0.97; synth.ts VOLUME_K 0.37; limiter.worklet.js ceiling -0.3 dBFS. Measured after (default kit, three machines): kick -9.35 dBFS, snare -1.5 dB, clap -3.5, toms, hats, cymbal -5.5 to -7, bass about -1.7 to -2 dB, ARP about -6 dB, the full mix peaks at -1.56 dBFS: the limiter no longer touches the kick. The site plays about 4.5 LU quieter (the decks' mastered tracks will sound louder).
R14-217. MIXER GAIN +/-12 dB and VU colours (2026-10-08, Mika: "je veux un gain de +12 dB et -12 dB ; les vumetres : blanc creme c'est vert, orange c'est jaune et rouge c'est rouge"). dj/math.ts gainDb (v x 12, the EQ law unchanged), engine.ts setGain, gestures.ts knobText. dj/theme.ts DJ_LIGHT.vu ['#2FD65A', '#FFD23F', '#FF2A1F'] and vuLight ['#14B83C', '#F2B800', '#D81E16'] (dj/controls.ts): green, yellow from -6 dBFS, red at -1.
R14-218. A deck pressed PLAY while loading arms it (2026-10-08, Mika: "quand on lance une track sur le deck de gauche puis celui de droite, le droit demarre pas quand on clic sur play ! c'est un gros probleme"). Measured: a 7-minute MP3 takes 2 to 5 s to read, decode and analyse, a 24-bit WAV up to 10 s the first time; a PLAY pressed in that window did nothing, and on a deck that already held a track it restarted the OLD track, which the decode then cut, leaving PLAY lit and the deck silent. dj/actions.ts djLoad: the old track is ejected at once (DjPlayer.unload), the screen shows the step (READING, DECODING, ANALYSING, or LOADING n % for a stream; dj/rig.ts loadText), PLAY pressed meanwhile arms the deck (DjDeckState.armed, the PLAY ring glows orange, PLAY ARMED on the screen; a second press disarms) and it starts as soon as it is ready; SYNC pressed meanwhile is applied when ready (syncArmed). A cue stored at the very end of a track no longer parks the deck there (djCue at the end goes back to the cue or 0, djPlay at the end restarts from the cue, a stale end cue resets on load). dj/gestures.ts: PLAY keeps a CUE preview only while a preview really plays, so a lost CUE release (a MIDI button in toggle mode) no longer blocks PLAY; window blur and a hidden tab release every CUE. Space plays the last deck touched by any input (djLastDeck). dj/rig.ts: the store cannot claim a deck plays while its player is stopped. audio/soundcloud.ts pauseForRun waits for the engine to confirm a pause (1.5 s at most) and the decks pause only when the site track starts. Also found: a Roto-Control still holding the MIXER / LIVE setups from before 2026-10-07 drives PLAY A with its PLAY B button (re-import the setups).
R14-219. Fixes from the critical review of R14-210 to R14-217 (2026-10-08). bass/gestures.ts: a knob grip rebases when LOCK changes under it (Grip.lock), so lifting the held step before the knob no longer writes the lock value into the global sound. bass/actions.ts: the root note LOCK gives an empty step goes away when the lock leaves it with nothing locked (lockNote); the accent an ACCENT lock adds goes away with the lock (accByLock); the stopped-machine audition uses the real gate (seq.ts gateOf, exported). actions.ts escape(): INFOS only when focus is bass. bass/InfosCard.tsx: a tap on the pinned card dismisses it. bass/rig.ts: LOCK polls instead of painting every frame; the knob LEDs follow LOCK in EDIT. bass/screen.ts: the knob echo says THE LINE FOLLOWS LIVE when the GEN knobs rewrite the line (bassGenLive). ui/Header.tsx goHome closes the MM-BASS hood; ui/MachineDrawer.tsx thumbnails follow it. audio/clock.ts stop() cancels pending voices last to first, so the kick after STOP chokes the tail still sounding.
R14-220. Roto-Control reliability (2026-10-08, Mika: "Roto control : des fois ca fonctionne, des fois ca ne fonctionne pas"). 14 failure modes were reproduced on a fake Web MIDI port; fixed in midi/midi.ts, roto.ts, targets.ts, the new midi/leader.ts and midi/rotoKeys.ts, ui/MidiPanel.tsx, audio/clock.ts. FOLLOW only on a press or a value move (never a release, never nav:*). Roto TOGGLE buttons flip their target on every message (the first press after a site change no longer gets lost) with a 15 ms loop guard. The Roto map answers only inputs named roto; learned keys only their own device (saved per key, field from); a duplicate from a second input within 3 ms is dropped; feedback goes only to the matching outputs. Resolution: learned in the focused machine, learned everywhere, the Roto map, then other machines' learned keys; the panel lists learned keys that sit on the Roto map with REMOVE CONFLICTS WITH THE ROTO MAP. Message order kept across lazy loads (one queue per machine, knob values merged). No silent RUN: clock.start() waits for the audio context to run (20 s at most, clock.waiting; a second RUN cancels), the LCD and the MIDI bubble say CLICK THE PAGE ONCE FOR SOUND; actions.ts machinesToggle starts MM-BASS when the waiting clock starts and STOP cancels a waiting RUN. Connection: the panel shows the browser permission, ASKING times out after 8 s, RESCAN, the feedback outputs listed, an unplugged input releases what it held, everything resent when the tab shows again. One tab drives the Roto (BroadcastChannel mm-midi, the last tab shown or focused leads; the others say MIDI IS USED BY ANOTHER TAB, USE MIDI HERE). A setup switch or a jumped knob does not apply its first jumped value (the motor gets the site value back). Feedback capped at 48 messages per 50 ms, nothing resent on a machine change except focus-dependent learned keys (was 214 messages). The panel says what each message did (CC 17 CH 12 -> PLAY A (MM-DECKS, Roto map MIXER)). Frozen addresses: rotoKeys.ts is an append-only registry of the current 360 (setup, channel, CC, target) lines; a moved control keeps its pair, a new target takes a free pair, a retired pair is never reused; setup names carry the version (RYTM 1008...), the panel shows the version to compare. The MIDI panel closes on a click outside it except during MIDI LEARN (it covered deck B). BASS: TUNE gets the centre detent, SLIDES and ACCENTS are named SLIDE PROB and ACC PROB.
R14-221. MM-RYTM PAGE view, step 1 of the Digitakt-style redesign (2026-10-08, Mika: "8 encoders assignables a condition de presser les bonnes touches... comme ca on a tout sur le panneau avec seulement 8 encoders et une selection des pages ; allons-y petit a petit ; j'adore l'ecran et le design de l'ecran mais j'aimerais qu'il soit plus utilise ; je veux le meme ecran"). The screen gains a PAGE view in the same OP-1 monochrome language: a header with the page pill (TRIG SRC FLTR AMP FX LFO), the voice and its sound, the 8 blocks of the page in 2x4 (the future 8 encoders), a full-width bottom line, SAMPLES across the width on SRC. HOME (today's screen) stays the default, pixel-identical. The PAGE view comes as a peek when a RYTM knob, encoder, kit control or step velocity is touched: the page of that parameter, its block outlined for 1.2 s (echo), back to HOME 4 s after the last touch (PAGE_PEEK_MS). Keyboard: H pins or unpins the PAGE view, [ and ] change page and pin it. VOLUME keeps the MIX overlay at HOME. Blocks not wired yet show their name faint and --; the FX bottom row says NO BD when BD is the track (the kick bypasses the global FX, R14-215). rytm/pages.ts, rytm/pageView.ts, state/rytmPage.ts (mm.v4.rytm.page.1: page and pin, flushed on pagehide), scene/screen.ts paintPage (redraws at most every 60 ms, the wait rounded up), renderer.ts seek band OLED_BAR_PAGE in PAGE view, useKeys.ts, ui/Lcd.tsx (". SRC PAGE"). No change to the sound, the face, the MIDI targets or the Roto setups. Next steps (rytm-synthesis plan): page keys and 8 page knobs on the face, then P-locks (hold a step + turn), then sample locks.
R14-222. MM-DECKS SYNC that starts at once, track analysis, phase lock, BACK (2026-10-08, Mika: "c'est quand j'utilise SYNC que quand j'appuie sur play ca met une demi seconde avant de se lancer... je devrais aussi avoir un bouton retour arriere pour aller choisir une autre track... je pense qu'il faut analyser la track"). Root cause: syncedStart() handed PLAY (and paused hot cues) a start time in the future so the deck's next beat would land on a reference beat, up to one full beat of waiting (measured 81 to 466 ms). Now PLAY always starts now (5.8 ms, the 256-sample start lead, with or without SYNC): startSynced() moves the play head by the smallest jump (at most half a beat, the other way below 0, wrapped inside an active loop) so the beats land on the reference's; one AudioContext instant is used for the maths, the start and the reference; every start and pause has a 3 ms fade and a jump while playing crossfades. The CUE preview and its keep-on-PLAY go through the same path; SYNC survives BACK + a new track (re-armed after analysis). Analysis rewritten (dj/math.ts trackGridSteps, in a Web Worker dj/grid.worker.ts through dj/grid.ts, one worker per analysis, an abort terminates it): low-band onset envelope over the whole track, autocorrelation 78 to 180 BPM, fold at +-0.6 BPM, the kick phase among the four 16ths, per-beat attacks then a weighted least-squares line (BPM to 0.001, offset to the ms); a tag BPM is the hint and is kept when the audio agrees (octaves included); the crate records bpmFrom tag or audio and never overwrites a tag. 36/36 synthetic tracks (118 to 132 BPM, offsets 0 to 0.402 s, intros and breakdowns), offset error under 2 ms (old code 20/36). Phase lock while synced: every 250 ms, above 4 ms of error the rate is nudged by at most 0.3 % until 1 ms, never a seek; nothing above 1/8 beat; a BEND or jog offset becomes the target until the next SYNC; two decks synced to each other: the first synced leads. A small phase meter under SYNC in the jog. BACK: an orange "< TRACKS" key at the top left of the deck screen opens that deck's browser without stopping or unloading the track; a NOW PLAYING row returns; on a playing deck the first tap on a track arms the load (DECK A IS PLAYING: TAP AGAIN TO LOAD, 3 s); Backspace toggles BACK on the last deck touched. debug: __v4.dj.phaseErrMs.
R14-223. MM-BASS as an Elektron machine (2026-10-08, Mika: "faire comme un principe de machine elektron... quand on clic sur un step on selectionne la partie qu'on veut modifier, voice, fx, enveloppe, et ensuite on tourne un encoder sur ce step... on voit a l'ecran que quand le sequenceur passe sur ce step alors le changement est fait... les valeurs de knobs sont a l'ecran de 0 a 127 ; EDIT ne fait que voir les patterns et les changements ; fais evoluer l'ecran"). Face (bass/theme.ts, rig.ts): the 11 dedicated sound knobs leave; a screen twice as big (5.5 x 3.5), 8 endless encoders A-H in 2x4 beside it (block k = encoder k; knurled black caps), page keys VOICE FILTER ENV FX with LEDs (half lit in LOCK on pages holding locks), GENERATOR, EDIT and OPEN kept, the LOCK row and the 16 steps kept with a LOCK legend; phone body 17.2 deep, every target 44 px or more; firmware V.3.0. Pages (bass/pages.ts, pageView.ts): VOICE WAVE PW SUB SUBOCT OCTAVE(GLOBAL) TUNE GLIDE; FILTER (default) CUTOFF RESO ENVMOD DECAY ACCENT ACCDECAY SWEEP KEYTRK; ENV ATTACK AMPDECAY SUSTAIN RELEASE LENGTH VOLUME; FX DRIVE DELAY DLYTIME(GLOBAL) DLYFB(GLOBAL) REVERB REVSIZE(GLOBAL) REVTONE(GLOBAL). Screen: always the PAGE view, values 0-127 (TUNE -64..+63, notches by name) with a unit and a live glyph; LOCK 05 pill, locked blocks inverted, the rest dimmed, GLOBAL refused with a message; while running, the blocks locked on the playing step flash inverted (a tie plays its note's locks, chainLocks); a 16-cell strip with lock dots; the 8 blocks are also hit zones (bass-blk-1..8). EDIT: piano roll + lock lanes of the page with their 0-127 values + the 16 patterns. Engine (bass.worklet.js): PW, KEY TRK, a real amp envelope (ATTACK, AMP DECAY to SUSTAIN, RELEASE), a tempo-synced ping-pong DELAY and a 4-line FDN REVERB inside the bass (mixer channel 2), sends lockable and smoothed, an idle FX sleeps only once its line is empty; defaults sample-identical to the old worklet. Every sound param lockable. MIDI: bass:knob:1..8 (page-relative), bass:page:<id>, bass:page, bass:key:p<id>, bass:key:i, bass:knob:<id> for the 11 new params; old ids kept. INFOS: the i in the screen corner (bass-key-i) toggles INFOS on the face; cards for encoders (the param of the page), page keys, LOCK keys (the hovered step), steps, GEN. Wheel 1/127 on encoders only. Stores: mm.v4.bass.page.
R14-224. MM-ARP big screen (2026-10-08, Mika: "je veux le meme type d'ecran pour ARP aussi plus gros plus de detail ! fais de la place"). voyager/lcd.ts is replaced by voyager/screen.ts (OP-1 monochrome, the RYTM/BASS family), preview.ts (arpPreview: the notes the arp plays for the held chord, RATE MODE RANGE NOTES OCTAVE and gate applied) and echo.ts. Desktop face rebuilt around a 5.3 x 2.06 screen (arpeggiator knobs in two rows on the left, transport keys as a 3+2 brick on the right); phone portrait: the body grows (15.6), the screen 7.0 x 2.42 under the knob rows. Screen: run state, preset name (patch.ts signature; INIT on the default sound), BPM, the chord with its degree and Camelot, the note ladder with playhead, gate bars and glide connectors, a parameter column (desktop), the 8-chord strip, a PRESETS pill; a touched knob shows its section, name, 0-127 value, unit and a matching drawing for about 1.5 s (held while dragging, per pointer). Redraws at most every 60 ms while running. EDIT and PRESETS restyled. INFOS: the i in the screen (hit zone vinfo), the I key, voy:infos, a twin button; state/voyInfos.ts, voyager/InfosCard.tsx, infos.ts (French), diagrams.ts, infoIds.ts; on the phone a tap shows the sheet on the side away from the control and never on the screen. Every hit-zone id kept.
R14-225. MM-RYTM Digitakt face, step 2 (2026-10-08, Mika: "Je ne vois AUCUN changement de ce que j'ai demande ! inadmissible !"). The GLOBAL FX and VOICE FX knob rows leave the face; 8 page encoders A-H in 2x4 under a larger screen (5.0 x 1.875 desktop, 6.9 x 2.59 phone), knob k under block k; MASTER and TEMPO as a fifth column; 6 page keys TRIG SRC SMPL FLTR AMP FX with LEDs (scene/rytmPageKeys.ts; Analog Rytm order, SMPL in place of LFO); RUN CLEAR RANDOM MUTE SOLO under the pads; a silk legend under the steps (HOLD A STEP + TURN A KNOB: THAT STEP ONLY). The PAGE view is the permanent default (the peek of R14-221 is gone; mm.v4.rytm.page.2); HOME with the lit key again or H. Blocks: 0-127 (bipolar -64..+63, notches by name), a unit line, a live drawing, FX rows tagged with the voice / ALL / NO BD; footer: the 16 steps with the playhead, page tabs (hit zones lcd-tab-<page>). Input: DialId p:0..7 through pageSlots (relative drag 150 px, wheel and arrows 1/127, double click resets), MIDI rytm:knob:1..8 (follows the page), rytm:page:<id>, rytm:page, rytm:home. Dock: a PAGES tab (6 keys, voices, 8 knobs 0-127). Audio unchanged at this step.
R14-226. MM-RYTM parameter locks, step 3 (2026-10-08). Hold a step 350 ms: LOCK (latched if released without turning, momentary after a turn; the hold is also measured from event time stamps under a stall); two fingers: hold a step and turn a page knob locks at once (orbit.claim keeps those pointers out of the pinch). In LOCK: page keys, page knobs write the lock, double click unlocks, CLEAR clears the step's locks, another step moves LOCK, the same step / Esc / EDIT / L leave; locking an empty step adds a VEL 9 trig. Lockable per hit: VEL, SOUND/SAMPLE (any family's sound), TUNE (+-24 st, rendered, never sped up), START, AMP DEC, PAN (constant power), VOL (exact, no cap), the kit knobs through a kit-knob ShotOverride prepared ahead, the voice FX sends; GLOBAL and NO LOCK blocks say so (voice DIST, CHORUS and TONE need channel automation, later). Screen: LOCK 05 pill, inverted locked blocks, a lock list in the empty half of the matrix, two-line foot; while running, the locked blocks of the playing step flash until the voice's next hit (280 to 900 ms); lock bars under the steps of the strip; HOME ticks; 3D steps with locks glow, the LOCK step blinks; BeatEditor lock dots; Dock: long press = LOCK, LOCK bar. Data: audio/locks.ts, state/rytmLock.ts, locks in mm.v4.pattern.3, the 16 slots, presets (a preset without locks clears them, missing VoiceFx fields get defaults), one hitParams() for trigger() and renderOffline({locks}); the mono kick, the kick bus and cancelVoice unchanged. MIDI rytm:lock:<0-15>, rytm:lock, rytm:enc:vtune|vpan|vstart, rytm:voice:<v>:tune|pan|start.
R14-227. MM-RYTM SYNTH and SAMPLE layers, Analog Rytm style, Mika's samples by default, step 4 (2026-10-08, Mika: "une machine pour la configuration a la main du Voice pour avoir des samples et aussi une configuration digitale du BD ou SD, comme la ANALOG Rytm ou on peut mettre des samples mais le kick peut etre parametre comme une machine... change les tout de suite pour BD et SD"). Each family plays a SYNTH layer (MACHINE 909 / 808 / MM, its knobs, LEVEL) and a SAMPLE layer (SAMPLE OFF or a family file, TUNE +-24, FINE +-64 cents, START, LEN 12 to 100 % with a fade, REV, LEVEL), rendered together into one shot by the shots worker; new machine knobs BD SWEEP, SD TUNE DECAY TONE (64 = the old sound); ATTACK, DRIVE, SNAPPY act on both layers (BOTH). Loudness: the sum takes the louder layer's loudness, peak at most +2 dB over the voice ceiling; the sample gain comes from the whole file (START and LEN never re-normalise). Pages: SRC = the SYNTH layer (BD MACHINE TUNE ATTACK SWEEP / DECAY DRIVE PITCH LEVEL), SMPL = the SAMPLE layer (TUNE FINE REV SAMPLE / START LEN - LEVEL), STRETCH to TRIG G; layer blocks dim when their layer is silent; the header shows what plays (BLUEPRINT, 909+BLUEPRINT, SILENT); CH+OH and TOM+HT tags (layers are per family). Defaults: a fresh kit puts BD and SD on the first file of their family (01 BluePrint, 01 Psy 02), synth at 0; old kits migrate (a sample choice becomes the SAMPLE layer with its TUNE and DECAY turned into layer TUNE and LEN; a model choice becomes the SYNTH layer); a saved kit still on the untouched old default (BD 909 / SD MM, knobs at default) moves to the samples; a vanished file falls back to the same number, then the first. Kit knobs and locks at 1/127 (saved values kept exactly). Sample locks on another family play alone; R2 locks migrated. MIDI: rytm:kit:sweep|sdtune|sddecay|sdtone and absolute targets for every layer setting. The plate KICK TUNE / DECAY read SYN TUNE / SYN DECAY.
R14-228. MM-RYTM INFOS and the i in the screen, step 5 (2026-10-08, Mika: "je veux un petit bouton i dans l'ecran a activer et de ce fait on peut voir les infos au survol, et je veux la meme chose pour RYTM aussi"). A circled i in the top-right corner of the screen on every view (hit zone lcd-i), the I key with RYTM focus, rytm:infos, a Dock key, an "i INFOS" chip when the hood is open; Esc leaves LOCK first, then INFOS. Desktop: a card after 250 ms of hover, beside the screen for encoders, blocks, page keys, steps and the header; clicks still act. Phone: a tap shows the sheet instead of acting (page keys and pads still navigate silently), drags still turn and the card follows, two-finger locks still work under INFOS, a tap through the sheet reaches the control. Cards: the parameter of the page and voice for each encoder (the screen marks the block), LOCK 05 when locked, SYNTH OFF / SAMPLE OFF / SILENT, live diagrams with ghost ranges, the LOCK card as numbered steps. state/rytmInfos.ts, rytm/InfosCard.tsx, rytm/infos.ts (88 French cards), rytm/diagrams.ts.
R14-229. OPEN with finesse (2026-10-08, Mika: "J'aimerais que tu fasses du plus fin, deja c'est super zoome, mais en plus c'est moche des grosses cases par dessus un PCB"). The black TWEAKS plates are gone on the MM-RYTM, MM-BASS and MM-ARP: the controls are mounted on the green PCB (scene/tweakplate.ts rewritten, scene/tweaklayout.ts data module): small knurled precision knobs on aluminium washers, slide switches for 2 or 3 positions, rotary selectors with a legend (the chosen entry bold with an orange dot, its own small texture), white silkscreen names, 270-degree scales with 11 ticks, end labels, RV/SW designators, thin group frames with cut-in titles, a small title block (machine, TWEAKS, revision); the layouts are data (RYTM_TWEAK_LAYOUT, bass/theme.ts, voyager/theme.ts); scene/pcb.ts clears components, traces, pour and texts from the zone and re-places designators. Camera: OPEN_VIEW frames the whole board (fill 0.70 desktop, 0.86 phone), the lifted lid slides further back and leaves the frame, its hit zones are off; neighbours are pushed out. CLOSE, INFOS and SCOPE become fine keys (1 px outline, small capitals, LED dot, 44 px touch); the MM-ARP OPEN gets an INFOS key (ui/VoyInfosKey.tsx); BASS ROOT and SCALE become legend selectors. Hit-zone ids, MIDI targets and values unchanged.
R14-230. scripts/import-rytm-samples.mjs generic (2026-10-08, Mika: "pour les samples les voici, change les tout de suite pour BD et SD : /Users/mauditemachine/Desktop/Samples"): every audio file of the folder, flat or in sub folders, family from the folder name (BD/Kick, SD/Snare, CP/Clap, HH/Hat, TOM, RS/Rim) then from the file name, clean numbered names (sampleLabel rules, key suffix only on a collision), --replace (only the families receiving files), --dry, --families, duplicates by content skipped, iCloud placeholders reported. Run on Mika's Mac: npm run samples:import -- "/Users/mauditemachine/Desktop/Samples" --replace. docs/midi regenerated (556 targets). Roto setups unchanged this round: every new target can be learned; a Roto sequencer (8 buttons = 8 steps with a running light, hold + turn = P-lock) is the next step.
R14-231. MENU: the Dark / Light switch, Mika's note and the new hoodie (2026-10-09, Mika: "je veux le selecteur darkmode et light mode au dessus de tout en haut a gauche dans le menu dans un bouton poussoir ... au lieu du soleil et de la lune quelque chose de plus proche de mon design"; "au dessus de Under The Hood ma description de Maudite Machine et un petit texte disant que je suis l'auteur de ce site web et de ces machines ... la boutique ou mon bandcamp"; "mettre ces hoodies en avant ... decoupe les hoodies fond transparent ... toute taille et ca coute 50$"). ui/AppearanceToggle.tsx rewritten as one pill switch at the very top left of the menu sheet, above the page list, desktop and phone (the DARK / LIGHT segmented control leaves the foot, RESET VIEW stays there at 44 px): the sliding knob carries the slanted A of the machines (ui/AMark.tsx, inline SVG from the logotype), the mode name in Robot Radicals beside a small LED (dark: black track, yellow knob left, yellow LED; light: cream track, black knob right, orange LED, as in Mika's reference), a short spring, the appearance applied 280 ms later so the 3D rebuild never freezes the knob mid-slide (reduced motion: immediate), role=switch, aria-checked, 112x44, same appearance store. "Behind the machines" (data.ts MENU_ABOUT): the press-kit bio shortened, then Mika's signed note (he built the site and every machine; support him with a tee or the new hoodie, or on Bandcamp), SHOP (opens MERCH) and BANDCAMP. ui/HoodieFeature.tsx + ui/menu.css: a yellow card with the cut-out hoodie (public/images/Merch_Hoodie-WAMM-Front|Back.png|webp, transparent, soft shadow), FRONT / BACK swap, NEW DROP, 50$ CAD, WE ARE MUSIC MAKERS, ALL SIZES S M L XL, SHOP THE HOODIE; the same card is the hero at the head of MERCH with size chips and ORDER into the CONTACT draft (product, size, price); compact phone layout. store.json ids 14 and 15, category hoodie-wamm (its own group in v2, v4, the SEO prerender and the admin selects). No existing price changed (T-shirt 30$, Sweatshirt 50$, Hoodie 50$, Hip Bag 80$). Desktop from 1200 px: three columns that fit 1440x900. Space / Enter on a focused button no longer start the MM-BASS or a DECK (bass/keys.ts, dj/keys.ts ownsPress).
R14-232. Roto-Control step sequencer (2026-10-09, Mika: "un sequenceur qui se suit, comme un vrai sequenceur, et j'ajoute mes steps ... pour les parameters lock j'appuie sur une touche et je tourne un encoder"). Two new setups, the 6 old ones unchanged (360 controls, same channel / CC / mode; ROTO_VERSION 2026-10-09, names 1009): RSEQ (MM-RYTM, setup 17, knobs channel 7, buttons 15) and BSEQ (MM-BASS, setup 18, knobs 8, buttons 16); rotoKeys.ts append-only. Page 1: the 8 knobs are the page encoders (rytm:knob / bass:knob, lock-aware, motors follow the LOCK step), the 8 buttons are STEP 1|9 .. STEP 8|16, a window over the 16 steps of the selected voice (BASS: the line) that FOLLOW scrolls with the playhead while running (STEPS 9-16 key, FOLLOW toggle). PUSH buttons: tap under 350 ms toggles the trig, hold 350 ms = LOCK, hold + turn = a P-lock on that step (an empty step gets a trig), release after a turn ends the momentary LOCK and restores the latched one, hold without a turn latches; several held RYTM steps are all locked; in EDIT the steps are the patterns.
