Pages 2-4: page keys, voices and their levels (RYTM), generator, note keys and TIE (BASS), globals and transport. src/v4/midi/seqlink.ts: playhead LEDs sent with MIDIOutput.send(data, timestamp) on the audio clock's step times (getOutputTimestamp, median of 9, up to 150 ms ahead), playhead inverted, LOCK step blinking at 4 Hz, corrections at +0.5 ms; the other LEDs through the 50 ms tick, only for keys placed in RSEQ / BSEQ, only to Roto outputs from the leader tab. midi.ts: hold length from the MIDI event time, 150 ms motor guard after a LOCK change then the 8 values resent, an echo guard on event times that learns a slow Roto's lag (up to 30 ms), held steps cancelled on unplug. MIDI panel: 8 setups in two rows, a per-page help, the option "Step keys in TOGGLE mode" (fallback if the Roto ignores incoming CC for PUSH LEDs: taps only, LOCK through the LOCK key), light-theme text fixed. Channels 7, 8, 15 and 16 are now used. docs/midi regenerated (585 targets, 8 setups).
R14-233. MM-BASS on the phone without encoders (2026-10-09, Mika: "en mobile c'est mieux si tu ne mets pas d'encoders ... on change dans l'ecran directement, et en dessous de l'ecran on retrouve les boutons ... donne-moi un ecran plus grand"). PORTRAIT only: no page encoders (no mesh, no bass-enc-* zones), the screen 7.6 x 6.4 instead of 4.05 (+58 %), blocks laid out top to bottom with the 0-127 value in large type, larger header, strip and foot; the 8 blocks bass-blk-1..8 (73x82 px) are the controls (relative drag on the encoder law, notches, double tap resets or unlocks, held ring, INFOS tap = card, MIDI LEARN bass:knob:N); VOICE FILTER ENV FX directly under the screen (46 px), header tabs bass-tab-* that turn the page (only the pattern opens PRESETS); while a finger covers a block the foot line repeats its value; a tap gives the hint DRAG UP OR DOWN / 2X: RESET; two-finger lock keeps LOCK until the block is released (lock generation guard). Double tap counts only presses released without moving (quick swipes no longer reset), phone and desktop. Phone texts say DRAG A VALUE. Desktop unchanged (325 zones, none moved).
R14-234. MM-BASS: 35 factory presets (2026-10-09, Mika: "une bonne grosse liste de presets ! fais-en au moins 30 avec des placements de notes differents"). 11 kept, 24 new, each with its own hand-written 16-step line (all masks unique, any two lines differ by at least 3 onsets): DARK DISCO (DARK DISCO, NIGHT DRIVE, VELVET DISCO, CURSED KISS, DESCENT, BLACK SATIN), INDIE DANCE (INDIE DANCE, NEON HEART, DISCO PUNK, SLOW BURN, NEW ROMANCE), MINIMAL (MINIMAL, GHOST NOTES, THREE STEP, DEEP CUT, MICRO BLEEP), PSY PROG (PSY PROG, MOON GALLOP), ITALO (ITALO, MUNICH 77, COSMIC LOVE), EBM (EBM, BODY MUSIC, STEEL BOOTS), ELECTRO (ELECTRO, DEEP SEA), ACID (ACID, BERLIN ACID, ACID RAIN), TECHNO (TECHNO, DUB CHAMBER), HOUSE (HOUSE, WAREHOUSE), SUB (DEEP SUB, SUB PULSE). Sound design over the full parameter set (BU helper in real units), mostly F# minor or ROOT ARP, scale colours only where the line plays them, pedal presets on roots inside F# minor; parseLine accepts _ and ++. Levels from OfflineAudioContext renders of the worklet: peaks -10.9 to -12.0 dBFS for a -11.3 target (2 dB under the kick). PRESETS view: style and rank (MINIMAL 13/35), the line drawn on the screen, reopens on the loaded preset (all machines), the loaded preset's name in the desktop header until an edit; LOCK no longer removes a loaded preset's note; 3-digit values clear of the block graphics.
R14-235. MM-RYTM face: MASTER and TEMPO apart, page keys under the screen, phone without encoders (2026-10-09, Mika: "j'aimerais que Master et Tempo soient des knobs differents au dessus des voices pour me separer des 8 encoders", "TRIG ... je veux ces boutons en dessous de l'ecran", "en mobile ... enleve [les encodeurs] pour RYTM et BASS ... donne-moi un ecran plus grand"). MASTER and TEMPO become smaller brushed-aluminium caps (fluted flank, chamfer, ringed top, engraved pointer; one InstancedMesh) with a printed 11-tick scale (MASTER -inf to 0 dB, TEMPO 100 to 150), above the voice pads; same ids enc-level / enc-tempo, MIDI and law. The six page keys sit right under the screen across its width, the 8 encoders form a clean 2x4 block below (desktop), the screen's footer tabs are off on the desktop (lcd-tab-* kept). Phone: no page encoders (penc-* gone), screen 7.28 x 3.50 (158 units), blocks 66x48 px always live in the page view with the encoder law, double tap reset / unlock, hold step + drag block, latched LOCK + drag, held outline, INFOS tap = card, dashed outlines for empty slots, only the list's block live while the SAMPLES list is open; page keys 45 px and MASTER / TEMPO 47 px hit zones; a value no longer jumps when the page changes under a held block; the SVG fallback draws the new face.
R14-236. MM-BASS stage 2: a tapped step is P-LOCK, the screen is the editor, the encoders are the global FX (2026-10-09, Mika: "on dirait que les parameters lock ne fonctionnent pas"; "autant en mobile qu'en desktop pouvoir modifier les choses directement sur l'ecran ... en desktop [les encodeurs] ne servent qu'a faire les modifs des FX globaux de la machine, les FX des parameters lock se font dans l'ecran"; "quand on selectionne un step on rentre en parameters lock et ca doit s'afficher dans ENV que nous sommes en P-LOCKS"; "le VOLUME du voice ou du step selectionne doit se retrouver dans VOICE"; "dans EDIT editer la hauteur des notes a la souris"; "je ne vois pas ce que STYLE et DENSITY font"). Select = P-LOCK: a tap on an empty step writes a note and latches P-LOCK on it, a tap on another note selects it in P-LOCK, a tap on the selected step cycles NOTE / TIE / OFF (OFF leaves); out with the LOCK key, Esc (overlays first) or the header pill bass-lcd-plock; a tap-latched P-LOCK leaves the Roto BSEQ taps and per-param MIDI knobs their old meaning. P-lock traps fixed: a double tap counts only on the release of a still press under 320 ms (quick second drags accumulate), INFOS on touch keeps locking, a LOCK key in EDIT closes EDIT and enters P-LOCK, 15 px touch slop, accelerated wheel, dimmed silent-lock hints (SUSTAIN FULL, SAW: NO PW, ACCENT STEPS, SLIDE STEPS, ROOT NOTE). Screen: negative header "P-LOCK STEP 05", the page title on every page ("AMP ENV · P-LOCKS"), P marks, the lock count everywhere, GLOBAL tags, a P-LOCK chip and strip flash during RUN. Pages: VOICE = WAVE PW SUB SUB OCT / OCTAVE TUNE GLIDE VOLUME; ENV = ATTACK AMP DECAY SUSTAIN RELEASE / LENGTH + a large live ADSR; FX = DRIVE, DELAY, REVERB lockable, DLY TIME, DLY FB, REV SIZE, REV TONE global. Desktop encoders fixed: A DRIVE, B DELAY, C DLY TIME, D DLY FB / E VOLUME, F REVERB, G REV SIZE, H REV TONE, silk under a GLOBAL FX bracket, never a lock, a popup on the screen while held; MIDI LEARN gives bass:global:<id> (8 new targets); bass:knob:1..8 stay page-relative and P-lock aware; desktop blocks editable by mouse (hover frame, ns-resize), header tabs turn the page on desktop too. EDIT piano roll (bass-roll): drag a note up / down (relative, note name shown), tap an empty column to add a note at that height (on release), tap a note for TIE then OFF, wheel on the hovered column. STYLE / DENSITY: a recipe (seed, base, rules, anchor for presets) stored with the line (mm.v4.bass.recipe, pattern slots, presets), steps flagged gen or hand, only free gen steps regenerated, hand steps and P-locks kept, DENSITY monotone with evenly spread thresholds and "MORE AT 34 · FEWER AT 26", factory presets answer (at their own STYLE the written line at their DENSITY, thinned or filled around it), the echo draws the real line with the changed steps, GEN keeps locks on surviving notes or says P-LOCKS CLEARED; SUB no longer goes silent. A fresh visit gets a generated ACID line. INFOS updated (French, desktop and phone).
R14-237. MM-RYTM stage 2: VOICE FLTR ENV FX, global FX on the encoders, MUTE once / twice (2026-10-09, Mika: "je veux merge SRC SMPL et TRIG ... on doit voir c'est quoi le sample"; "AMP doit s'appeler ENV et doit etre plus complet"; "les FX du Voice selectionne mais aussi les FX globaux"; "quand on appuie deux fois sur MUTE on peut selectionner plusieurs voices, une seule fois c'est juste une voice"). Four page keys VOICE FLTR ENV FX (pkey-voice|fltr|env|fx; the old pkey-trig|src|smpl|amp and lcd-tab-trig..fx ids are gone), pages with tabs (press the lit key again, or tap the header chip): VOICE MAIN (a wide SOUND block, one list OFF / 909 / 808 / MM / the family's samples, BLUEPRINT + 909 when layered; VOL, VEL = new-step velocity or the step's in P-LOCK, PITCH, FINE, LEN and REV for BD / SD) and VOICE SYNTH (MACHINE, MIX crossfader computed from the old levels, the machine knobs); FLTR a real per-hit filter LP / HP / BP (FREQ 20 Hz-20 kHz, RESO, ENV ±5 oct, F.ATK, F.DEC, TONE, live curve); ENV ATK (SNAP to 500 ms), HOLD (4 ms to 1 s), DEC, START, PAN with an AHD drawing; FX VOICE FX (DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB of the voice, all lockable, DIST / CHORUS locks held until the voice's next hit, audio/lockfx.ts) and GLOBAL FX (DIST CHORUS DELAY REVERB STRETCH SWING DLY TIME DLY FB, never locked, NO BD where the kick bypasses them). Desktop encoders fixed: A DIST, B CHORUS, C DELAY, D REVERB / E STRETCH, F SWING, G DLY TIME (new: 1/16 to 1/2, dotted 1/8 default), H DLY FB (new: 0-0.87, 0.58 default), silk under GLOBAL FX, never a lock, a popup on the screen; MIDI LEARN gives rytm:enc:<fx>. The screen is the editor on desktop and phone (drag, accelerated wheel, still double tap, hover frame, ns-resize). P-LOCK: negative header with padlock, "P-LOCK STEP 05", "ENV · P-LOCKS", the count, P corner tags, RUN chip. MIDI: rytm:page:voice / env new, trig / src / smpl / amp aliases, rytm:screen:<tab>, rytm:knob:1..8 = block k of the shown screen; RSEQ page 2 = VOICE FLTR ENV FX MUTE SOLO WINDOW FOLLOW (4 registry lines appended). Page store mm.v4.rytm.page.3 migrated from .2 (trig / src / smpl -> VOICE, amp -> ENV), default VOICE. MUTE / SOLO: OFF -> press = ONE (blinks, the next voice tap toggles it) -> press again at any delay = MULTI (steady, each tap toggles, the screen lists the voices) -> press = OFF keeping the mutes (LED dim, pads red-dim); hold 600 ms = all voices back; Esc leaves the mode on the MM-RYTM; mute and solo lists independent; the 1.5 s "voice then MUTE" shortcut removed; keys M and Shift+M; three-state LEDs on the 3D keys and the Dock (44 px). INFOS rewritten for the new pages (infoIds reads pages.ts). Old kits sound identical (offline render difference 0); the sample-only S.TUNE / S.FINE / S.START leave the screens but keep playing and their locks.
R14-238. MM-BASS never silent (2026-10-09, Mika: "je bouge DRIVE pour le baisser au max et plus de son dans BASS"). The output tanh is always applied (it was skipped at DRIVE exactly 0: unbounded output on some settings, or the harmonics lost and only the fundamental left, inaudible on laptop and phone speakers). The worklet accepts only finite numbers from params and locks, a non-finite or out-of-range sample never reaches the output and the voice heals within the block; soundLocks, bassParams.set and the bassDial lock path refuse NaN and Infinity. Before: one bad value latched NaN in the filter, the VCA and the DC blocker until reload.
R14-239. MM-BASS Monark engine (2026-10-09, Mika: "je veux vraiment un son a la MONARK de Native Instruments"). A Minimoog Model D voice: OSC 1 (saw to square with PW), OSC 2 and OSC 3 with the six Model D shapes (OSC 3 REV SAW), RANGE 32' to 4', SEMI -7..+7, FINE +-50 cents, pink NOISE, FEEDBACK, DRIVE = LOAD into the filter (0.5x to 4x with make-up gain, clean at 0); a zero-delay 4-pole ladder with per-stage saturation, bass compensation and modes LP24, LP12, LP6, BP and 303 (the previous voice, bit-exact); Model D contours (filter and amp), constant-rate glide, DRIFT; tabs VOICE MAIN / OSC / MIX and FILTER MAIN / CONTOUR (press the lit page key again, bass:screen:<id>). Default sound MM CLASSIC; a returning visitor's live sound moves to the new engine (line, locks, volume and FX kept); user presets saved before load in 303 (SAVED IN 303 MODE). Roto setups 1009.3 (BASS page 4: 8 Monark knobs).
R14-240. MM-BASS factory presets as Monark patches (2026-10-09, Mika: "les presets vraiment excellents"). The 35 presets keep their names, lines and FX; 32 become Model D patches built from 13 recipes in real units (CLASSIC, NIGHT, PUNK, BLOOP, MUNICH, BODY, ACID, SUB, SHARK, HOLLOW, SWELL, GROWL, PING), ACID, BERLIN ACID and ACID RAIN stay in 303. Peaks -11.3 +-1 dBFS (2 dB under the kick), loudness windows per style, offline quality checks (low end, fundamental, filter movement, accent contrast).
R14-241. MM-BASS generator made simple (2026-10-09, Mika: "je trouve STYLE et DENSITY complexes a utiliser"). STYLE plays that style's line at once (take 01, each style remembers its take); DENSITY becomes NOTES, 0 to 16, one notch = exactly one note in a fixed musical order; GEN gives the next numbered take (hold: the previous one); MUTATE changes 2 or 3 notes (hold: undo, 8 levels); notes you set yourself and P-locked steps are never touched; CLEAR then NOTES up brings the take back note by note; a fresh visit starts on DARK DISCO 01 at 12 notes. New MIDI targets bass:gen, bass:mutate, bass:gen:back, bass:mutate:undo, bass:notes:up / down; keys G / Shift+G, M / Shift+M.
R14-242. MM-BASS face, evening of 2026-10-09 (Mika: "les encoders j'aimerais qu'ils aillent de 0 a 127 normaux, la ils font deux tours", "le cutoff res et ADSR filtre directement en encoder, pour les fx laisse-les dans l'ecran", "je ne vois pas l'interet de ces boutons [LOCK] puisqu'on reste appuye pour un P-lock"). The 8 desktop encoders are absolute 0-127 pots over 270 degrees with an 11-tick scale under a FILTER bracket: A CUTOFF, B RESO, C ENV MOD, D DRIVE / E F.ATTACK, F DECAY, G F.SUSTAIN, H RELEASE, always the whole line, never a P-lock; the FX are set on the screen (FX page); bass:global:<old FX> MIDI targets kept. The LOCK keys above the steps are removed (BASS_LOCK_KEYS false: no mesh, no hit zone; bass:lock:<n> MIDI kept); on the phone the steps grow into their space. On the MM-RYTM the marks above the steps are the velocity LEDs (no LOCK keys there).
R14-243. MM-BASS as a bassline machine, simple to use (2026-10-09 evening, Mika: "agrandis l'ecran jusqu'aux steps, mets les boutons [ACCENT SLIDE NOTE- NOTE+ OCT- OCT+] dans l'ecran et mets les boutons RUN CLEAR GEN et rajoute un bouton PRESET et mets-les a droite", "MUTATE je comprends pas, enleve ca", "des presets vraiment differents entre eux, JE VEUX QUE CE SOIT UNE MACHINE A BASSLINE"). The screen goes down to the steps (desktop 5.5 x 5.25, phone 7.6 x 8.0); the six step keys are a band of on-screen buttons at its foot (STEP 05 F#2, ACCENT and SLIDE lit when set; same hit-zone ids and MIDI targets); RUN CLEAR GEN and a new PRESET key (opens the PRESETS view, LED lit while open, bass:key:preset) sit on the right (desktop: EDIT OPEN / STYLE NOTES / GEN PRESET / RUN CLEAR, the encoders staged under FILTER and CONTOUR brackets, the page keys on the RUN CLEAR row; phone: STYLE NOTES EDIT OPEN, then RUN CLEAR GEN PRESET); MUTATE leaves the face (its MIDI targets stay for the Roto). 33 factory presets, each a full patch of its own (no shared recipes), distinctiveness measured offline (minimum pair distance 2.80 against 0.72 before; unique lines): DARK DISCO, BLACK SATIN, SWELL, CURSED KISS, INDIE DANCE, NEON HEART, DISCO PUNK, SLOW BURN, ACID, ACID RAIN, BERLIN ACID, TECHNO, REESE, DUB CHAMBER, MINIMAL, MICRO BLEEP, DEEP CUT, ITALO, MUNICH 77, COSMIC LOVE, EBM, GROWL, BODY MUSIC, ELECTRO, LASER, DEEP SEA, HOUSE, RUBBER, ORGAN BASS, PSY PROG, MOON GALLOP, DEEP SUB, SUB PULSE; peaks -12.2 to -10.4 dBFS.

### R14-244 MM-BASS: line LENGTH 1 to 16 steps (2026-10-10)
- `bassState.len` (1..16, default 16, saved in `mm.v4.bass.len`), stored per pattern slot (`lens`) and per preset (`len`, omitted when 16).
- The sequencer loops on the first `len` steps; with a shorter length it advances on the RYTM grid by elapsed steps (polymetric against the RYTM).
- EDIT screen: a LEN bar on top of the roll, tap or drag to the wanted step; the steps beyond are dimmed on the screen and on the face LEDs.
- MIDI target `bass:len` (16 steps).

### R14-245 MM-BASS: a tap on a step is a note, hold for P-LOCK; EDIT note grid; plain ADSR (2026-10-10)

- A tap on a step key puts a note (the root) on an empty step and removes the note of a full one; any P-LOCK in progress ends. P-LOCK: hold the step (LOCK_HOLD_MS), as on an Elektron.
- EDIT roll is a note grid: one row per semitone (white keys lighter), each row named when it has room, the C rows always; notes are filled cells; a click in a row places the note at that pitch.
- AMP ENV is drawn as a plain ADSR: four straight segments, A, D and R wider when longer, the SUSTAIN plateau in the middle, NOTE OFF at its end, each value under its segment.
- Face and screen hints say TAP: NOTE ON, OFF / HOLD: P-LOCK.

### R14-246 MM-BASS: in EDIT the step keys show and edit the notes; patterns on the screen (2026-10-10)

- In EDIT the 16 step keys light the notes of the line (as outside EDIT) and a tap puts or removes a note; hold: P-LOCK (EDIT closes).
- Patterns are chosen on the screen's PATTERNS row: tap plays the pattern (at the bar when running), several in a row chain them, hold an empty one to copy the current line. The bass-roll hotspot reaches down to that row; the P-LOCK lanes in between do nothing.
- Step LEDs brighter: a note 0.78 orange, an accent full, a tie 0.35.

### R14-247 MM-RYTM: BIT and COMP replace DLY TIME and DLY FB; a global FX knob opens GLOBAL FX; CLEAR puts every FX to 0 on all machines (2026-10-10)

- Desktop encoders G and H (GLOBAL_ENCODERS) are now BIT (bit reduction, 16 to 4 bits plus a sample-rate divider up to 8) and COMP (compressor, threshold 0 to -30 dB, ratio 1:1 to 8:1, attack 3 ms, release 120 ms, 60 % makeup, no look-ahead). Both run in audio/glue.worklet.js on the whole MM-RYTM output (dry, REVERB and DELAY returns) between glueIn and rytmOut; at 0 they pass the sound as is. Stored in pattern fx as bits and comp (default 0). DLY TIME and DLY FB stay at their stored values (no longer on the face or the GLOBAL FX page); MIDI rytm:enc:bits and rytm:enc:comp are new.
- Turning a desktop global encoder shows the FX page on its GLOBAL tab, the turned block ringed (replaces the popup).
- CLEAR, outside P-LOCK: RYTM clears the steps and sets DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB, SWING, BIT, COMP to 0, STRETCH to the centre and every voice's DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB to 0; BASS clears the line and sets DRIVE, DELAY and REVERB sends to 0; ARP clears the chords and sets OVERDRIVE, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB to 0. The screen says CLEARED · FX OFF.

### R14-248 MM-BASS EDIT panel like MM-RYTM's, SIDECHAIN from the RYTM kick, P-LOCK exit by tap; MM-RYTM VOL and VEL merged (2026-10-10)

- MM-BASS EDIT is now a panel (bass/EditPanel.tsx) like the MM-RYTM's (ui/BeatEditor.tsx): on the machine over its step row on desktop (columns on the 16 steps, it grows upward to fit), below the machine on the phone. PTN row (tap, chain, hold an empty one to copy), a grid with one row per scale note (two octaves, OCT - and OCT + shift by an octave, roots coloured), ACC, SLIDE, TIE switches per step, LEN lane, CLEAR, DONE. Tap a cell: that step plays that note; tap the note: the step goes off; drag along a row: the same note on the crossed steps (from a note: they go off). The screen's EDIT roll is off (rig.ts EDIT_ON_SCREEN = false): the screen keeps its page.
- MM-BASS: a tap on the step that is in P-LOCK leaves P-LOCK and keeps the note (bassStepTap).
- MM-BASS SIDECHAIN (param sidechain, global, default 0, MIDI bass:global:sidechain): a Ducker on a new gain bassIn between the engine and bassOut, fired by the same RYTM kicks as the MM-ARP's; shown as OFF or -x DB ON KICK; FX block E (VOLUME stays on VOICE H). CLEAR sets it to 0.
- MM-RYTM VOICE page: the VEL block is gone, VOL spans two cells; new steps play HIGH (the stored per-voice new-step velocity is no longer read). VOICE knobs after VOL shift down by one (PITCH k2, FINE k3, LEN k4, REV k5, GATE k4).

### R14-249 MM-BASS: STYLE and NOTES knobs off the face, GEN next to RUN and CLEAR (2026-10-10)

- The face has no dedicated knob any more (style and density lose `face`, theme.ts SHOW_GEN_KNOBS = false, no GENERATOR bracket); both params stay in presets and MIDI.
- Desktop right column: EDIT OPEN PRESET on the top row, RUN CLEAR GEN on the row of the page keys (keys 0.62 wide). Phone: EDIT OPEN PRESET, then RUN CLEAR GEN, three keys per row.
- After CLEAR the screen says GEN: A NEW LINE (GEN on an empty line plays the take's full count).

### R14-250 MM-BASS EDIT panel: VEL lane (2026-10-10)

- A VEL lane under TIE: one bar per note step, its height the step's VOLUME P-lock (bassStepVel, 0.05 to 1 rounded to 1/127); a step without one shows the line's VOLUME, paler. Drag draws (crossed steps follow the line), double click gives the step back the line's value; keys Up/Down change it, Delete clears it. Off and tie steps have no bar.

### R14-251 MM-BASS: the screen stays live during EDIT; the EDIT panel sits below the machine (2026-10-10)

- With the EDIT panel open the screen keeps its page and its zones (blocks, tabs, title, chips) stay enabled: rig.ts gates them on rollOnScreen() (EDIT_ON_SCREEN and EDIT open), no longer on EDIT alone.
- The EDIT panel is below the machine on desktop too (useEditorPanel inset: the framing lifts the whole machine), so the screen, its six keys and the page keys are never covered; desktop rows 10 px, the 16 patterns on one row, the hint on one line.

### R14-252 MM-BASS hidden by default (2026-10-10)

- Mika: "hide BASS for now, it's too complex, I'll come back to it next week; focus on RYTM and ARP". state/focus.ts BASS is now false unless ?bass=1 (kept for the tab in sessionStorage mm.v4.bass; ?bass=0 hides it again). Without it: no MM-BASS on the table, in the header, the machine nav, the drawer or ?m=bass; its modules are not loaded (state/bassload.ts). The mixer keeps its channel 2 named BASS (silent).
- The untested preset rewrite (e2ef2ee) is reverted before deploying and kept in history for next week.

### R14-253 MM-RYTM: default voice levels raised; FX settings engine (2026-10-10)

- Mika: "the kick is too loud and you hear nothing else". Offline (muted) measure of the start pattern, energy under the kick: snare -10 dB, toms -12, open hat -19, closed hat -24. shotsdsp.ts: every voice but the kick goes up 2.5 to 4 dB (SHOT_LOUD SD -7, TOM -8.5, HT -9, CH -10.5, OH -11, CP -7.5, CY -12) under tighter peak caps (SHOT_BELOW SD 0.5, TOM 1.5, HT 2, CH 2.5, OH 2.5, CP 1.5, CY 3.5); the kick peak is unchanged (-9.4 dBFS out). VEL_GAIN softened (LOW -8 dB, MID -3.1 dB). Result in the pattern: snare +1.5 dB, closed hat +4.1, open hat +3, toms +3.3, clap +2.9; the whole pattern peaks at -3.7 dBFS before the limiter.
- The 10 FX settings (pattern fx dtone rsize rtone rpre xtone crate cdepth brate catk crel, fxLaw) now drive the sound: delay loop low pass, reverb impulse rebuilt (debounced, two convolvers crossfaded over 60 ms), dist wet low pass, bus chorus rate and depth, glue worklet rate / atk / rel; at their defaults the sound is bit-identical to before. 'bits' is now bit depth only (the sample-rate divider is brate). Their screen pages come next.

### R14-254 MM-RYTM: a settings page per global FX on the screen (2026-10-10)

- Six screens under GLOBAL FX, not in the FX key's tab cycle (rytm/pages.ts FX_DETAILS; state/rytmPage.ts detail, not stored): DIST (TONE), CHORUS (RATE, DEPTH), DELAY (TIME, FEEDBACK, TONE), REVERB (SIZE, TONE, PRE), BIT (RATE), COMP (ATTACK, RELEASE). The FX amount is the 2 x 2 block on the left, its settings on the right, readable units (4.5 KHZ, 2.4 S, 20 MS, X1.0, /2, 1/8D 346 MS, 58% FB); scope all, never locked (GLOBAL in P-LOCK).
- Enter: a single tap on the FX's GLOBAL FX block (opens after 360 ms so a double tap still resets; a drag still sets the value), turning its desktop encoder (STRETCH and SWING stay on GLOBAL FX), Enter on the block's twin, MIDI rytm:screen:fxdelay etc. Leave: the FX key (back to GLOBAL FX), any other page key or [ ], tapping GLOBAL in the header, Esc.
- Ten new INFOS cards; DLY TIME and DLY FB come back as TIME and FEEDBACK on the DELAY page.

### R14-255 Mixer without the hidden MM-BASS channel; Roto setups 1010 (2026-10-10)

- While MM-BASS is hidden (state/focus.ts BASS false), the mixer draws only RYTM, ARP, A and B (dj/theme.ts DJ_SHOWN, djShownOf): knobs, faders, VU, RCA, silk numbering (1 RYTM, 2 ARP, 3 A, 4 B), "4 CHANNEL" and FX TO steps (ALL, RYTM, ARP, A, B) follow it. Internal channel ids and indices stay (dj-ch1, dj-ch3...), so nothing stored moves; a FX TO pointing at the hidden channel reads ALL. ?bass=1 brings the 5-channel mixer back.
- Roto setups rebuilt, ROTO_VERSION 2026-10-10 (names end in 1010): RYTM page 1 = DIST, CHORUS, DELAY, REVERB, STRETCH, SWING, BIT, COMP; RYTM page 4 drops KICK ATTACK and SNAPPY (KICK SOUND, TUNE, DECAY, DRIVE, SNARE SOUND, HATS SOUND). MIXER and LIVE skip channel 2 while BASS is hidden (faders, filters, MIXER page 2 = HI, MID, LOW, GAIN of RYTM and ARP); the BASS RUN key becomes MM-STUDIO (nav:all). BASS and BSEQ setups are not generated while BASS is hidden (their old files stay in docs/midi/roto).
- New frozen ROTO_KEYS lines (midi/rotoKeys.ts, appended): rytm:enc:bits, rytm:enc:comp, dj-ch1-mid/gain, dj-ch3-mid/gain, nav:all (MIXER, LIVE), dj-ch3-filter (LIVE).
